import { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { apiRequest } from "../utils/http.js";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { loadTokens, saveTokens } from "./token-store.js";
import {
  assertAppCredentialFingerprint,
  resolveAppCredentials,
  type ResolvedAppCredentials,
} from "./app-credentials.js";
import type { MetaPage, MetaPlatform, Platform, StoredToken } from "../platforms/types.js";
import {
  InMemoryOAuthSessionStore,
  PostgresOAuthSessionStore,
  authorizationLifetimeMs,
  claimFailureResult,
  expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath,
  type AuthorizationSessionStatus,
  type OAuthSessionStore,
} from "./oauth-sessions.js";

export type { AuthorizationSessionStatus, OAuthSessionStore } from "./oauth-sessions.js";

interface MetaPermissions {
  data?: Array<{ permission: string; status: string }>;
}

interface MetaPagesResponse {
  data?: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    access_token: string;
    tasks?: string[];
    instagram_business_account?: { id: string; username?: string };
  }>;
}

interface TokenResponse {
  access_token: string;
  expires_in?: number;
  refresh_token?: string;
  scope?: string;
}

interface InstagramTokenResponse {
  access_token?: string;
  user_id?: string | number;
  permissions?: string | string[];
  expires_in?: number;
  data?: Array<{
    access_token?: string;
    user_id?: string | number;
    permissions?: string | string[];
    expires_in?: number;
  }>;
}

export type OAuthCallbackOutcome =
  | { result: "connected"; platform: Platform; sessionId: string }
  | { result: "cancelled" | "failed" | "expired"; platform?: Platform; sessionId?: string };

export interface OAuthCallbackDependencies {
  store: OAuthSessionStore;
  finish: (
    platform: Platform,
    userId: string,
    code: string,
    redirectUri: string,
    appCredentialFingerprint?: string,
  ) => Promise<void>;
}

const scheduledMedia = new Map<string, { imagePath: string; contentType: string }>();
const inMemoryOAuthSessionStore = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
const postgresOAuthSessionStore = new PostgresOAuthSessionStore();
let callbackServer: ReturnType<typeof createServer> | undefined;
let callbackServerPromise: Promise<void> | undefined;

export function instagramPermissionScopes(permissions: string | string[] | undefined): string[] {
  if (Array.isArray(permissions)) return permissions.flatMap((permission) => permission.split(/[,\s]+/)).filter(Boolean);
  return permissions?.split(/[,\s]+/).filter(Boolean) ?? [];
}

// The exact callback URL registered with each provider. It is derived from the
// configured base URL (an ngrok tunnel locally, the Render worker in cloud mode)
// and is persisted with the authorization session so the token exchange reuses
// byte-identical `redirect_uri` values.
export function oauthCallbackUrl(platform: Platform): string {
  return `${config.redirectBaseUrl}/oauth/${platform === "linkedin" ? "linkedin" : "meta"}/callback`;
}

// Store selected by OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE. Local mode keeps the original in-memory
// behavior; cloud mode persists sessions in PostgreSQL.
export function oauthSessionStore(): OAuthSessionStore {
  return config.oauthCallbackMode === "cloud" ? postgresOAuthSessionStore : inMemoryOAuthSessionStore;
}

// The public worker always completes callbacks against PostgreSQL, even if it is
// started without OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE so an existing deployment cannot break.
export function oauthPostgresSessionStore(): OAuthSessionStore {
  return postgresOAuthSessionStore;
}

function html(response: ServerResponse, message: string, status = 200): void {
  response.writeHead(status, { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
  response.end(`<!doctype html><html><body><p>${message}</p><p>You may close this tab.</p></body></html>`);
}

function safeErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown token exchange or storage error.";
  return message
    .replace(/(client_secret|access_token|refresh_token|authorization_code|code)=([^&\s]+)/gi, "$1=[redacted]")
    .slice(0, 700);
}

function requireClientSecret(credentials: ResolvedAppCredentials, provider: string): void {
  if (credentials.clientSecret) return;
  if (credentials.source === "user") {
    throw new Error(`Your saved ${provider} app secret could not be read on this server. Re-save it in Developer App Settings, or check APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY.`);
  }
  throw new Error(`Set the shared ${provider} app secret environment variable before completing this authorization.`);
}

async function handleCallback(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  const url = new URL(request.url ?? "/", `http://localhost:${config.imageServerPort}`);
  const mediaMatch = /^\/scheduled-media\/([a-f0-9]{64})$/.exec(url.pathname);
  if (mediaMatch && (request.method === "GET" || request.method === "HEAD")) {
    const media = scheduledMedia.get(mediaMatch[1]!);
    if (!media) {
      html(response, "Scheduled media was not found.", 404);
      return;
    }
    void readFile(media.imagePath).then(
      (image) => {
        response.writeHead(200, {
          "content-type": media.contentType,
          "content-length": image.byteLength,
          "cache-control": "no-store",
          "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
        });
        response.end(request.method === "HEAD" ? undefined : image);
      },
      () => html(response, "Scheduled media is temporarily unavailable.", 503),
    );
    return;
  }
  const expectedPlatforms = expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath(url.pathname);
  if (!expectedPlatforms) {
    html(response, "This OAuth callback route does not exist.", 404);
    return;
  }
  // Claim from the same store the session was created in. `beginAuthorization`
  // persists sessions in PostgreSQL when OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE=cloud, so a local
  // listener receiving tunneled callbacks in that mode must claim from the
  // Postgres store too; claiming from process memory would always fail.
  const outcome = await processOAuthCallback(url.searchParams, expectedPlatforms, {
    store: oauthSessionStore(),
    finish: finishAuthorization,
  });
  if (outcome.result === "connected") {
    html(response, "Authorization completed. Return to the MCP client and check the connection status.");
  } else if (outcome.result === "cancelled") {
    html(response, "Authorization was cancelled or denied. Return to the MCP client to start a new request.", 400);
  } else if (outcome.result === "expired") {
    html(response, "This authorization request is expired or invalid.", 400);
  } else {
    html(response, "Authorization could not be completed. Return to the MCP client for status and error details.", 400);
  }
}

export async function registerScheduledMedia(
  mediaToken: string,
  imagePath: string,
  contentType: string,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(mediaToken)) throw new Error("Scheduled media token is invalid.");
  await ensureCallbackServer();
  scheduledMedia.set(mediaToken, { imagePath, contentType });
}

export function unregisterScheduledMedia(mediaToken: string): void {
  scheduledMedia.delete(mediaToken);
}

async function ensureCallbackServer(): Promise<void> {
  if (callbackServer?.listening) return;
  if (callbackServerPromise) return callbackServerPromise;
  const server = createServer((request, response) => {
    void handleCallback(request, response).catch(() => {
      if (response.headersSent) {
        response.end();
        return;
      }
      response.writeHead(500, { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store" });
      response.end("OAuth callback failed.");
    });
  });
  callbackServer = server;
  callbackServerPromise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const onError = (error: NodeJS.ErrnoException) => {
      server.off("listening", onListening);
      if (callbackServer === server) callbackServer = undefined;
      callbackServerPromise = undefined;
      reject(new Error(`Could not start the local image/OAuth server on port ${config.imageServerPort}: ${error.message}`));
    };
    const onListening = () => {
      server.off("error", onError);
      callbackServerPromise = undefined;
      resolve();
    };
    server.once("error", onError);
    server.once("listening", onListening);
    server.listen(config.imageServerPort, "127.0.0.1");
  });
  return callbackServerPromise;
}

// Shared callback core used by both the local listener and the public worker
// callback handler. It claims the state atomically, handles provider
// cancellation, exchanges the code, and records the terminal status. It never
// logs or returns the state, code, tokens, or client secrets.
export async function processOAuthCallback(
  searchParams: URLSearchParams,
  expectedPlatforms: readonly Platform[],
  dependencies: OAuthCallbackDependencies,
): Promise<OAuthCallbackOutcome> {
  const state = searchParams.get("state");
  if (!state) return { result: "failed" };

  const claim = await dependencies.store.claim(state, expectedPlatforms);
  if (!claim.claimed) return { result: claimFailureResult(claim.reason) };
  const session = claim.session;

  const providerError = searchParams.get("error_description")
    ?? searchParams.get("error_reason")
    ?? searchParams.get("error");
  if (providerError) {
    await dependencies.store.updateStatus(session.sessionId, "cancelled", "The provider authorization was cancelled or denied.");
    return { result: "cancelled", platform: session.platform, sessionId: session.sessionId };
  }

  const code = searchParams.get("code");
  if (!code) {
    await dependencies.store.updateStatus(session.sessionId, "failed", "The provider callback did not include an authorization code.");
    return { result: "failed", platform: session.platform, sessionId: session.sessionId };
  }

  try {
    await dependencies.finish(session.platform, session.userId, code, session.redirectUri, session.appCredentialFingerprint);
    await dependencies.store.updateStatus(session.sessionId, "connected");
    return { result: "connected", platform: session.platform, sessionId: session.sessionId };
  } catch (error) {
    await dependencies.store.updateStatus(session.sessionId, "failed", safeErrorMessage(error));
    return { result: "failed", platform: session.platform, sessionId: session.sessionId };
  }
}

export async function getAuthorizationStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus> {
  return oauthSessionStore().getStatus(sessionId, userId);
}

export async function waitForAuthorization(
  sessionId: string,
  timeoutSeconds: number,
  userId: string,
): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus> {
  return oauthSessionStore().waitForStatus(sessionId, userId, timeoutSeconds);
}

function appLabel(platform: Platform): string {
  if (platform === "facebook") return "Facebook";
  if (platform === "instagram") return "Instagram";
  return "LinkedIn";
}

export function validateAuthorizationConfiguration(platform: Platform, credentials: ResolvedAppCredentials): void {
  const cloud = config.oauthCallbackMode === "cloud";
  if (!credentials.clientId) {
    throw new Error(credentials.source === "user"
      ? `Your saved ${appLabel(platform)} app/client ID is missing. Re-save it in Developer App Settings.`
      : `Set the shared ${appLabel(platform)} app/client ID before connecting.`);
  }
  // In cloud mode the secret is only needed by the worker that runs the code
  // exchange, never by the request that builds the consent URL.
  if (!cloud && !credentials.clientSecret) {
    throw new Error(credentials.source === "user"
      ? `Your saved ${appLabel(platform)} app secret is not available on this server. Re-save it in Developer App Settings.`
      : `Set the shared ${appLabel(platform)} app secret before connecting.`);
  }
  const redirectUri = oauthCallbackUrl(platform);
  if (cloud && !redirectUri.startsWith("https://")) {
    throw new Error("OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE=cloud requires OAUTH_REDIRECT_BASE_URL to be a public HTTPS base URL (for example the deployed worker URL).");
  }
  if (platform === "linkedin" && !redirectUri.startsWith("https://")) {
    throw new Error("LinkedIn requires an HTTPS callback URL. Set OAUTH_REDIRECT_BASE_URL to an HTTPS tunnel URL and register the matching callback in your LinkedIn app.");
  }
  if (platform === "instagram" && !redirectUri.startsWith("https://")) {
    throw new Error("Instagram Login requires an HTTPS callback URL. Set OAUTH_REDIRECT_BASE_URL to an HTTPS tunnel URL and register the matching callback in your Instagram app.");
  }
}

export async function beginAuthorization(platform: Platform, userId: string): Promise<{
  authorizationUrl: string;
  sessionId: string;
  expiresAt: number;
}> {
  // Build the consent URL with the user's own Developer App when configured,
  // otherwise the shared environment app. The secret is only decrypted when this
  // process also runs the token exchange (local mode); a cloud request needs only
  // the public client id and the version fingerprint.
  const credentials = await resolveAppCredentials(userId, platform, {
    includeSecret: config.oauthCallbackMode !== "cloud",
  });
  validateAuthorizationConfiguration(platform, credentials);
  const redirectUri = oauthCallbackUrl(platform);
  // Only local development needs the localhost listener. A cloud worker or
  // Vercel request must not bind a local port to receive the callback.
  if (config.oauthCallbackMode !== "cloud") await ensureCallbackServer();
  const state = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
  const sessionId = randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
  const expiresAt = Date.now() + authorizationLifetimeMs;
  await oauthSessionStore().create({
    sessionId,
    state,
    userId,
    platform,
    redirectUri,
    expiresAt,
    appCredentialFingerprint: credentials.fingerprint,
  });
  if (platform === "linkedin") {
    const url = new URL("https://www.linkedin.com/oauth/v2/authorization");
    url.search = new URLSearchParams({
      response_type: "code",
      client_id: credentials.clientId,
      redirect_uri: redirectUri,
      state,
      scope: config.linkedinScopes.join(" "),
    }).toString();
    return { authorizationUrl: url.toString(), sessionId, expiresAt };
  }
  if (platform === "instagram") {
    const url = new URL("https://www.instagram.com/oauth/authorize");
    url.search = new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: credentials.clientId,
      redirect_uri: redirectUri,
      state,
      response_type: "code",
      scope: config.instagramScopes.join(","),
      enable_fb_login: "false",
    }).toString();
    return { authorizationUrl: url.toString(), sessionId, expiresAt };
  }
  const url = new URL(`https://www.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/dialog/oauth`);
  url.search = new URLSearchParams({
    client_id: credentials.clientId,
    redirect_uri: redirectUri,
    state,
    response_type: "code",
    scope: ["pages_show_list", "pages_read_engagement", "pages_manage_posts"].join(","),
  }).toString();
  return { authorizationUrl: url.toString(), sessionId, expiresAt };
}

// Dispatches to the correct provider exchange. The platform stored in the
// authorization session decides which credentials and token slot are used, so a
// Facebook authorization can never overwrite an Instagram token. The optional
// fingerprint is the credential version recorded when the flow started; a
// mismatch means the user edited their app mid-flow, so the exchange is refused
// rather than run against a different app.
export async function finishAuthorization(
  platform: Platform,
  userId: string,
  code: string,
  redirectUri: string,
  appCredentialFingerprint?: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const credentials = await resolveAppCredentials(userId, platform);
  assertAppCredentialFingerprint(appCredentialFingerprint, credentials.fingerprint);
  if (platform === "linkedin") {
    requireClientSecret(credentials, "LinkedIn");
    await finishLinkedInAuthorization(userId, code, redirectUri, credentials);
    return;
  }
  if (platform === "instagram") {
    requireClientSecret(credentials, "Instagram");
    await finishInstagramAuthorization(userId, code, redirectUri, credentials);
    return;
  }
  requireClientSecret(credentials, "Facebook");
  await finishMetaAuthorization(userId, code, redirectUri, platform, credentials);
}

async function finishInstagramAuthorization(
  userId: string,
  code: string,
  redirectUri: string,
  credentials: ResolvedAppCredentials,
): Promise<void> {
  const form = new FormData();
  form.set("client_id", credentials.clientId);
  form.set("client_secret", credentials.clientSecret ?? "");
  form.set("grant_type", "authorization_code");
  form.set("redirect_uri", redirectUri);
  form.set("code", code);
  const { data: shortResponse } = await apiRequest<InstagramTokenResponse>(
    "https://api.instagram.com/oauth/access_token",
    { method: "POST", body: form },
  );
  const shortToken = shortResponse.data?.[0] ?? shortResponse;
  if (!shortToken.access_token || shortToken.user_id === undefined) {
    throw new Error("Instagram did not return an access token and user ID.");
  }

  const longTokenUrl = new URL("https://graph.instagram.com/access_token");
  longTokenUrl.search = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "ig_exchange_token",
    client_secret: credentials.clientSecret ?? "",
    access_token: shortToken.access_token,
  }).toString();
  const { data: longToken } = await apiRequest<TokenResponse>(longTokenUrl);
  const { data: profile } = await apiRequest<{ user_id?: string | number; id?: string; username?: string }>(
    `https://graph.instagram.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/me?fields=user_id,username`,
    { accessToken: longToken.access_token },
  );
  const accountId = profile.user_id ?? profile.id ?? shortToken.user_id;
  const scopes = instagramPermissionScopes(shortToken.permissions);
  const vault = await loadTokens(userId);
  vault.instagram = {
    accessToken: longToken.access_token,
    ...(longToken.expires_in ? { expiresAt: Date.now() + longToken.expires_in * 1000 } : {}),
    scopes,
    connectedAt: Date.now(),
    accountId: String(accountId),
    ...(profile.username ? { accountName: profile.username } : {}),
  };
  await saveTokens(userId, vault);
}

async function finishMetaAuthorization(
  userId: string,
  code: string,
  redirectUri: string,
  platform: MetaPlatform,
  credentials: ResolvedAppCredentials,
): Promise<void> {
  const shortToken = await exchangeMetaCode(code, redirectUri, credentials);
  const tokenUrl = new URL(`https://graph.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/oauth/access_token`);
  tokenUrl.search = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "fb_exchange_token",
    client_id: credentials.clientId,
    client_secret: credentials.clientSecret ?? "",
    fb_exchange_token: shortToken,
  }).toString();
  const { data: longToken } = await apiRequest<TokenResponse>(tokenUrl);
  const { data: profile } = await apiRequest<{ id: string; name?: string }>(
    `https://graph.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/me?fields=id,name`,
    { accessToken: longToken.access_token },
  );
  let scopes: string[] = [];
  try {
    const { data } = await apiRequest<MetaPermissions>(
      `https://graph.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/me/permissions`,
      { accessToken: longToken.access_token },
    );
    scopes = (data.data ?? []).filter((entry) => entry.status === "granted").map((entry) => entry.permission);
  } catch {
    scopes = [];
  }
  let pages: MetaPage[] = [];
  try {
    const { data } = await apiRequest<MetaPagesResponse>(
      `https://graph.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/me/accounts?fields=id,name,access_token,tasks,instagram_business_account{id,username}`,
      { accessToken: longToken.access_token },
    );
    pages = (data.data ?? []).map((page) => ({
      id: page.id,
      name: page.name,
      accessToken: page.access_token,
      tasks: page.tasks ?? [],
      ...(page.instagram_business_account ? { instagramAccount: page.instagram_business_account } : {}),
    }));
  } catch {
    pages = [];
  }
  const vault = await loadTokens(userId);
  const record: StoredToken = {
    accessToken: longToken.access_token,
    ...(longToken.expires_in ? { expiresAt: Date.now() + longToken.expires_in * 1000 } : {}),
    scopes,
    connectedAt: Date.now(),
    accountId: profile.id,
    ...(profile.name ? { accountName: profile.name } : {}),
    pages,
  };
  vault[platform] = record;
  await saveTokens(userId, vault);
}

async function exchangeMetaCode(
  code: string,
  redirectUri: string,
  credentials: ResolvedAppCredentials,
): Promise<string> {
  const url = new URL(`https://graph.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/oauth/access_token`);
  url.search = new URLSearchParams({
    client_id: credentials.clientId,
    client_secret: credentials.clientSecret ?? "",
    redirect_uri: redirectUri,
    code,
  }).toString();
  const { data } = await apiRequest<TokenResponse>(url);
  return data.access_token;
}

async function finishLinkedInAuthorization(
  userId: string,
  code: string,
  redirectUri: string,
  credentials: ResolvedAppCredentials,
): Promise<void> {
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "authorization_code",
    code,
    client_id: credentials.clientId,
    client_secret: credentials.clientSecret ?? "",
    redirect_uri: redirectUri,
  });
  const { data: token } = await apiRequest<TokenResponse>("https://www.linkedin.com/oauth/v2/accessToken", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  const { data: profile } = await apiRequest<{ sub: string; name?: string }>(
    "https://api.linkedin.com/v2/userinfo",
    { accessToken: token.access_token },
  );
  const vault = await loadTokens(userId);
  vault.linkedin = {
    accessToken: token.access_token,
    ...(token.refresh_token ? { refreshToken: token.refresh_token } : {}),
    ...(token.expires_in ? { expiresAt: Date.now() + token.expires_in * 1000 } : {}),
    scopes: token.scope?.split(/[,\s]+/).filter(Boolean) ?? config.linkedinScopes,
    connectedAt: Date.now(),
    accountId: profile.sub,
    ...(profile.name ? { accountName: profile.name } : {}),
  };
  await saveTokens(userId, vault);
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/auth/oauth-callback.ts
================================================================================

import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { buildFrontendRedirectUrl, expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath } from "./oauth-sessions.js";
import { finishAuthorization, oauthPostgresSessionStore, processOAuthCallback } from "./oauth.js";

const resultMessages: Record<string, string> = {
  connected: "Authorization completed. You can close this tab.",
  cancelled: "Authorization was cancelled or denied. You can close this tab.",
  expired: "This authorization request expired. Start a new connection from the accounts page.",
  failed: "Authorization could not be completed. Return to the accounts page for details.",
};

function sendHtml(response: ServerResponse, message: string, status = 200): void {
  response.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
    "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
  });
  response.end(`<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Authorization</title></head><body><p>${message}</p></body></html>`);
}

function redirect(response: ServerResponse, location: string): void {
  response.writeHead(302, {
    location,
    "cache-control": "no-store",
    "referrer-policy": "no-referrer",
  });
  response.end();
}

// Handles GET /oauth/meta/callback and GET /oauth/linkedin/callback on the
// public worker. It returns false for any other route so the worker can keep its
// own 404 behavior. The browser only ever learns a coarse outcome: the session
// status is read by the authenticated frontend, never from this response.
export async function handleOAuthCallback(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<boolean> {
  const url = new URL(request.url ?? "/", "http://localhost");
  const expectedPlatforms = expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath(url.pathname);
  if (!expectedPlatforms) return false;

  let outcome;
  try {
    outcome = await processOAuthCallback(url.searchParams, expectedPlatforms, {
      store: oauthPostgresSessionStore(),
      finish: finishAuthorization,
    });
  } catch {
    // Database or unexpected failure: never surface internals to the browser.
    outcome = { result: "failed" as const };
  }

  const location = buildFrontendRedirectUrl(config.oauthFrontendBaseUrl, outcome.result, outcome.platform);
  if (location) {
    redirect(response, location);
    return true;
  }
  sendHtml(response, resultMessages[outcome.result] ?? resultMessages["failed"]!);
  return true;
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/auth/oauth-sessions.ts
================================================================================

import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { databasePool } from "../database.js";
import { ensureDatabaseMigrations } from "../database-migrations.js";
import type { Platform } from "../platforms/types.js";

// Authorization sessions exist for the brief window between starting a provider
// OAuth flow and receiving the browser callback. In local mode they live in
// process memory (mirroring the original behavior); in cloud mode they are
// persisted in PostgreSQL so any Render instance can complete the callback.
export type OAuthSessionStatus = "pending" | "connected" | "cancelled" | "failed" | "expired";
// `processing` is an internal claim marker written atomically before the token
// exchange runs. The frontend still sees it as `pending`.
export type OAuthStoredStatus = OAuthSessionStatus | "processing";

export interface AuthorizationSessionStatus {
  sessionId: string;
  platform: Platform;
  status: OAuthSessionStatus;
  expiresAt: number;
  error?: string;
}

export interface CreateOAuthSessionInput {
  sessionId: string;
  state: string;
  userId: string;
  platform: Platform;
  redirectUri: string;
  expiresAt: number;
  // Fingerprint of the Developer App credentials used to build the consent URL.
  // Optional so sessions created before the feature keep working.
  appCredentialFingerprint?: string;
}

export interface ClaimedOAuthSession {
  sessionId: string;
  userId: string;
  platform: Platform;
  redirectUri: string;
  expiresAt: number;
  appCredentialFingerprint?: string;
}

export type OAuthClaimFailure = "unknown" | "expired" | "used" | "wrong_platform";
export type OAuthClaimResult =
  | { claimed: true; session: ClaimedOAuthSession }
  | { claimed: false; reason: OAuthClaimFailure };

export interface OAuthSessionStore {
  create(input: CreateOAuthSessionInput): Promise<void>;
  claim(state: string, expectedPlatforms: readonly Platform[]): Promise<OAuthClaimResult>;
  getStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus>;
  updateStatus(sessionId: string, status: OAuthSessionStatus, error?: string): Promise<void>;
  waitForStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string, timeoutSeconds: number): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus>;
}

export const authorizationLifetimeMs = 15 * 60 * 1000;
const sessionRetentionMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const sessionMissingMessage = "Authorization session not found or expired. Start a new authorization request.";

// The raw state value is a bearer-style secret: whoever holds it can complete a
// pending authorization. Cloud persistence therefore stores only its SHA-256
// digest, which is enough for an exact-match lookup and never exposes the value.
export function hashOAuthState(state: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(state, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath(pathname: string): readonly Platform[] | null {
  if (pathname === "/oauth/meta/callback") return ["facebook", "instagram"];
  if (pathname === "/oauth/linkedin/callback") return ["linkedin"];
  return null;
}

export function isOAuthCallbackPath(rawUrl: string | undefined): boolean {
  if (!rawUrl) return false;
  let pathname: string;
  try {
    pathname = new URL(rawUrl, "http://localhost").pathname;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
  return expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath(pathname) !== null;
}

export type OAuthRedirectResult = "connected" | "cancelled" | "failed" | "expired";

// Builds the only browser redirect this app ever emits after a provider
// callback: a fixed `/accounts` path on the configured frontend origin. Because
// the origin comes from configuration (never the request) and the path is
// constant, there is no way for a caller to turn this into an open redirect.
export function buildFrontendRedirectUrl(
  frontendBaseUrl: string,
  result: OAuthRedirectResult,
  platform?: Platform,
): string | null {
  const base = frontendBaseUrl.trim();
  if (!base) return null;
  let parsed: URL;
  try {
    parsed = new URL(base);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (parsed.protocol !== "https:" && parsed.protocol !== "http:") return null;
  if (parsed.username || parsed.password) return null;
  const redirect = new URL("/accounts", parsed);
  redirect.searchParams.set("oauth", result);
  if (platform) redirect.searchParams.set("platform", platform);
  return redirect.toString();
}

function redirectResultForClaimFailure(reason: OAuthClaimFailure): OAuthRedirectResult {
  return reason === "expired" ? "expired" : "failed";
}

export function claimFailureResult(reason: OAuthClaimFailure): "failed" | "expired" {
  return redirectResultForClaimFailure(reason) === "expired" ? "expired" : "failed";
}

interface MemoryRecord {
  sessionId: string;
  state: string;
  userId: string;
  platform: Platform;
  redirectUri: string;
  status: OAuthStoredStatus;
  error?: string;
  createdAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  completedAt?: number;
  appCredentialFingerprint?: string;
}

function isTerminal(status: OAuthStoredStatus): boolean {
  return status === "connected" || status === "cancelled" || status === "failed" || status === "expired";
}

function statusView(record: MemoryRecord): AuthorizationSessionStatus {
  const expiredByTime = record.expiresAt <= Date.now();
  const status: OAuthSessionStatus = record.status === "processing"
    ? "pending"
    : record.status;
  const resolved: OAuthSessionStatus = expiredByTime && status === "pending" ? "expired" : status;
  return {
    sessionId: record.sessionId,
    platform: record.platform,
    status: resolved,
    expiresAt: record.expiresAt,
    ...(record.error ? { error: record.error } : {}),
  };
}

export class InMemoryOAuthSessionStore implements OAuthSessionStore {
  private readonly byState = new Map<string, MemoryRecord>();
  private readonly byId = new Map<string, MemoryRecord>();
  private readonly waiters = new Map<string, Set<(status: AuthorizationSessionStatus) => void>>();
  private readonly timers = new Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>();

  async create(input: CreateOAuthSessionInput): Promise<void> {
    this.prune();
    const record: MemoryRecord = {
      sessionId: input.sessionId,
      state: input.state,
      userId: input.userId,
      platform: input.platform,
      redirectUri: input.redirectUri,
      status: "pending",
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      expiresAt: input.expiresAt,
      ...(input.appCredentialFingerprint ? { appCredentialFingerprint: input.appCredentialFingerprint } : {}),
    };
    this.byState.set(record.state, record);
    this.byId.set(record.sessionId, record);
    const delay = Math.max(0, record.expiresAt - Date.now());
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.timers.delete(record.sessionId);
      if (!isTerminal(record.status)) {
        this.applyStatus(record, "expired", "The authorization request expired before the provider callback arrived.");
      }
    }, delay);
    timer.unref();
    this.timers.set(record.sessionId, timer);
  }

  async claim(state: string, expectedPlatforms: readonly Platform[]): Promise<OAuthClaimResult> {
    const record = this.byState.get(state);
    if (!record) return { claimed: false, reason: "unknown" };
    // Reject a state presented on the wrong callback route before consuming it
    // so a legitimate callback on the correct route can still succeed.
    if (!expectedPlatforms.includes(record.platform)) return { claimed: false, reason: "wrong_platform" };
    if (record.expiresAt <= Date.now()) {
      if (!isTerminal(record.status)) {
        this.applyStatus(record, "expired", "The authorization request expired before the provider callback arrived.");
      }
      return { claimed: false, reason: "expired" };
    }
    if (record.status !== "pending") return { claimed: false, reason: "used" };
    record.status = "processing";
    return {
      claimed: true,
      session: {
        sessionId: record.sessionId,
        userId: record.userId,
        platform: record.platform,
        redirectUri: record.redirectUri,
        expiresAt: record.expiresAt,
        ...(record.appCredentialFingerprint ? { appCredentialFingerprint: record.appCredentialFingerprint } : {}),
      },
    };
  }

  async getStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus> {
    const record = this.requireOwned(sessionId, userId);
    this.expireIfNeeded(record);
    return statusView(record);
  }

  async updateStatus(sessionId: string, status: OAuthSessionStatus, error?: string): Promise<void> {
    const record = this.byId.get(sessionId);
    if (!record) return;
    this.applyStatus(record, status, error);
  }

  async waitForStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string, timeoutSeconds: number): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus> {
    const record = this.requireOwned(sessionId, userId);
    this.expireIfNeeded(record);
    if (record.status !== "pending" || timeoutSeconds === 0) return statusView(record);
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const waiters = this.waiters.get(sessionId) ?? new Set<(status: AuthorizationSessionStatus) => void>();
      let timer: NodeJS.Timeout;
      const finish = (status: AuthorizationSessionStatus) => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        waiters.delete(finish);
        resolve(status);
      };
      timer = setTimeout(() => finish(statusView(record)), timeoutSeconds * 1000);
      timer.unref();
      waiters.add(finish);
      this.waiters.set(sessionId, waiters);
    });
  }

  private requireOwned(sessionId: string, userId: string): MemoryRecord {
    const record = this.byId.get(sessionId);
    if (!record || record.userId !== userId) throw new Error(sessionMissingMessage);
    return record;
  }

  private expireIfNeeded(record: MemoryRecord): void {
    if (record.expiresAt <= Date.now() && !isTerminal(record.status)) {
      const timer = this.timers.get(record.sessionId);
      if (timer) this.timers.delete(record.sessionId);
      this.applyStatus(record, "expired", "The authorization request expired before the provider callback arrived.");
    }
  }

  private applyStatus(record: MemoryRecord, status: OAuthSessionStatus, error?: string): void {
    record.status = status;
    if (error) record.error = error;
    else delete record.error;
    record.completedAt = Date.now();
    const view = statusView(record);
    const waiters = this.waiters.get(record.sessionId);
    if (waiters) {
      for (const resolve of waiters) resolve(view);
      waiters.clear();
    }
    const timer = this.timers.get(record.sessionId);
    if (timer && isTerminal(status)) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      this.timers.delete(record.sessionId);
    }
  }

  private prune(): void {
    const cutoff = Date.now() - sessionRetentionMs;
    for (const [sessionId, record] of this.byId) {
      if (record.completedAt === undefined || record.completedAt >= cutoff) continue;
      this.byId.delete(sessionId);
      this.byState.delete(record.state);
      const timer = this.timers.get(sessionId);
      if (timer) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        this.timers.delete(sessionId);
      }
      this.waiters.delete(sessionId);
    }
  }
}

interface PostgresSessionRow {
  session_id: string;
  platform: Platform;
  status: OAuthStoredStatus;
  expires_at: Date;
  error: string | null;
}

function postgresStatus(row: { status: OAuthStoredStatus; expires_at: Date }): OAuthSessionStatus {
  const status: OAuthSessionStatus = row.status === "processing" ? "pending" : row.status;
  return row.expires_at.getTime() <= Date.now() && status === "pending" ? "expired" : status;
}

function delay(milliseconds: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds);
    timer.unref();
  });
}

export class PostgresOAuthSessionStore implements OAuthSessionStore {
  async create(input: CreateOAuthSessionInput): Promise<void> {
    // Ensure migration 009 (which adds app_credential_fingerprint) has run before
    // writing the session. The runner is idempotent and memoized, so this adds a
    // single check on the first session after a deploy.
    await ensureDatabaseMigrations();
    await databasePool().query(
      `INSERT INTO public.oauth_authorization_sessions
         (session_id, state_hash, user_id, platform, redirect_uri, status, expires_at, app_credential_fingerprint)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'pending', $6, $7)`,
      [
        input.sessionId,
        hashOAuthState(input.state),
        input.userId,
        input.platform,
        input.redirectUri,
        new Date(input.expiresAt),
        input.appCredentialFingerprint ?? null,
      ],
    );
  }

  async claim(state: string, expectedPlatforms: readonly Platform[]): Promise<OAuthClaimResult> {
    const stateHash = hashOAuthState(state);
    // A single conditional UPDATE is the atomic claim: only one caller can flip
    // `pending` to `processing`, and only for the expected callback platform.
    const { rows } = await databasePool().query<{
      session_id: string;
      user_id: string;
      platform: Platform;
      redirect_uri: string;
      expires_at: Date;
      app_credential_fingerprint: string | null;
    }>(
      `UPDATE public.oauth_authorization_sessions
          SET status = 'processing', claimed_at = NOW()
        WHERE state_hash = $1
          AND status = 'pending'
          AND expires_at > NOW()
          AND platform = ANY($2::text[])
        RETURNING session_id, user_id, platform, redirect_uri, expires_at, app_credential_fingerprint`,
      [stateHash, [...expectedPlatforms]],
    );
    const row = rows[0];
    if (row) {
      return {
        claimed: true,
        session: {
          sessionId: row.session_id,
          userId: row.user_id,
          platform: row.platform,
          redirectUri: row.redirect_uri,
          expiresAt: row.expires_at.getTime(),
          ...(row.app_credential_fingerprint ? { appCredentialFingerprint: row.app_credential_fingerprint } : {}),
        },
      };
    }

    // Nothing was claimed; determine why without mutating unrelated state.
    const { rows: probe } = await databasePool().query<{
      status: OAuthStoredStatus;
      expires_at: Date;
      platform: Platform;
    }>(
      `SELECT status, expires_at, platform
         FROM public.oauth_authorization_sessions
        WHERE state_hash = $1`,
      [stateHash],
    );
    const existing = probe[0];
    if (!existing) return { claimed: false, reason: "unknown" };
    if (!expectedPlatforms.includes(existing.platform)) return { claimed: false, reason: "wrong_platform" };
    if (existing.expires_at.getTime() <= Date.now()) {
      await databasePool().query(
        `UPDATE public.oauth_authorization_sessions
            SET status = 'expired', completed_at = NOW()
          WHERE state_hash = $1 AND status = 'pending'`,
        [stateHash],
      );
      return { claimed: false, reason: "expired" };
    }
    return { claimed: false, reason: "used" };
  }

  async getStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus> {
    const { rows } = await databasePool().query<PostgresSessionRow>(
      `SELECT session_id, platform, status, expires_at, error
         FROM public.oauth_authorization_sessions
        WHERE session_id = $1 AND user_id = $2`,
      [sessionId, userId],
    );
    const row = rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new Error(sessionMissingMessage);
    return {
      sessionId: row.session_id,
      platform: row.platform,
      status: postgresStatus(row),
      expiresAt: row.expires_at.getTime(),
      ...(row.error ? { error: row.error } : {}),
    };
  }

  async updateStatus(sessionId: string, status: OAuthSessionStatus, error?: string): Promise<void> {
    await databasePool().query(
      `UPDATE public.oauth_authorization_sessions
          SET status = $2,
              error = $3,
              completed_at = CASE WHEN $2 IN ('connected', 'cancelled', 'failed', 'expired')
                                  THEN NOW() ELSE completed_at END
        WHERE session_id = $1`,
      [sessionId, status, error ?? null],
    );
  }

  async waitForStatus(sessionId: string, userId: string, timeoutSeconds: number): Promise<AuthorizationSessionStatus> {
    const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutSeconds * 1000;
    for (;;) {
      const status = await this.getStatus(sessionId, userId);
      if (status.status !== "pending" || timeoutSeconds === 0) return status;
      if (Date.now() >= deadline) return status;
      await delay(Math.min(1000, Math.max(0, deadline - Date.now())));
    }
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/auth/token-store.ts
================================================================================

import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdir, readFile, rename, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { databasePool } from "../database.js";
import type { Platform, StoredToken, TokenVault } from "../platforms/types.js";

interface EncryptedFile { version: 1; iv: string; tag: string; ciphertext: string }
interface EncryptedCloudToken { version: 1; iv: string; tag: string; ciphertext: string }

// One provider-token vault per application user. The local encrypted file and the
// cloud oauth_tokens table both key records by (user_id, platform).
type UserTokenVaults = Record<string, TokenVault>;

const platforms: Platform[] = ["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"];

function isPlatform(key: string): key is Platform {
  return (platforms as string[]).includes(key);
}

function encryptionKey(envName: string, value: string): Buffer {
  if (!/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(value)) {
    throw new Error(`Set ${envName} to a randomly generated 64-character hex key.`);
  }
  return Buffer.from(value, "hex");
}

function localEncryptionKey(): Buffer {
  return encryptionKey("SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY", config.tokenEncryptionKey);
}

function cloudEncryptionKey(): Buffer {
  return encryptionKey("CLOUD_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY", config.cloudTokenEncryptionKey);
}

function encryptCloudToken(platform: Platform, token: StoredToken): EncryptedCloudToken {
  const iv = randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", cloudEncryptionKey(), iv);
  cipher.setAAD(Buffer.from(`social-media-mcp:${platform}:v1`, "utf8"));
  const ciphertext = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(token), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  return {
    version: 1,
    iv: iv.toString("hex"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("hex"),
    ciphertext: ciphertext.toString("hex"),
  };
}

function decryptCloudToken(platform: Platform, envelope: EncryptedCloudToken): StoredToken {
  if (envelope.version !== 1) throw new Error("Unsupported cloud token record version.");
  const decipher = createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", cloudEncryptionKey(), Buffer.from(envelope.iv, "hex"));
  decipher.setAAD(Buffer.from(`social-media-mcp:${platform}:v1`, "utf8"));
  decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(envelope.tag, "hex"));
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([
    decipher.update(Buffer.from(envelope.ciphertext, "hex")),
    decipher.final(),
  ]).toString("utf8")) as StoredToken;
}

function normalizeUserVaults(parsed: unknown): UserTokenVaults {
  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return {};
  const record = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
  // Legacy single-user files store a bare platform -> token object. Wrap it under
  // the owner identity so existing local installs keep working after upgrading.
  if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => isPlatform(key))) {
    return { [config.ownerUserId]: record as TokenVault };
  }
  const vaults: UserTokenVaults = {};
  for (const [userId, vault] of Object.entries(record)) {
    if (vault && typeof vault === "object") vaults[userId] = vault as TokenVault;
  }
  return vaults;
}

async function readLocalVaults(): Promise<UserTokenVaults> {
  let file: string;
  try {
    file = await readFile(config.tokenFile, "utf8");
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") return {};
    throw new Error("Could not read the encrypted token store.");
  }
  try {
    const envelope = JSON.parse(file) as EncryptedFile;
    if (envelope.version !== 1) throw new Error("Unsupported token store version.");
    const decipher = createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", localEncryptionKey(), Buffer.from(envelope.iv, "hex"));
    decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(envelope.tag, "hex"));
    const plaintext = Buffer.concat([
      decipher.update(Buffer.from(envelope.ciphertext, "hex")),
      decipher.final(),
    ]).toString("utf8");
    return normalizeUserVaults(JSON.parse(plaintext));
  } catch {
    throw new Error("Could not decrypt the token store. Check the encryption key or remove the stored file to reconnect.");
  }
}

async function writeLocalVaults(vaults: UserTokenVaults): Promise<void> {
  const iv = randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", localEncryptionKey(), iv);
  const ciphertext = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(vaults), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const envelope: EncryptedFile = {
    version: 1,
    iv: iv.toString("hex"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("hex"),
    ciphertext: ciphertext.toString("hex"),
  };
  await mkdir(path.dirname(config.tokenFile), { recursive: true });
  const temporaryPath = `${config.tokenFile}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(temporaryPath, JSON.stringify(envelope), { mode: 0o600 });
  await rename(temporaryPath, config.tokenFile);
}

export async function readLocalTokens(ownerId: string): Promise<TokenVault> {
  return (await readLocalVaults())[ownerId] ?? {};
}

export async function saveLocalTokens(ownerId: string, tokens: TokenVault): Promise<void> {
  const vaults = await readLocalVaults();
  vaults[ownerId] = tokens;
  await writeLocalVaults(vaults);
}

async function loadCloudTokens(ownerId: string): Promise<TokenVault> {
  const { rows } = await databasePool().query<{ platform: Platform; token_ciphertext: EncryptedCloudToken }>(
    "SELECT platform, token_ciphertext FROM public.oauth_tokens WHERE user_id = $1 AND platform = ANY($2::text[])",
    [ownerId, platforms],
  );
  const tokens: TokenVault = {};
  for (const row of rows) tokens[row.platform] = decryptCloudToken(row.platform, row.token_ciphertext);
  return tokens;
}

export async function saveCloudTokens(
  ownerId: string,
  tokens: TokenVault,
  overwrite = true,
): Promise<void> {
  const client = await databasePool().connect();
  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");
    if (!overwrite) {
      const { rows } = await client.query<{ platform: Platform }>(
        "SELECT platform FROM public.oauth_tokens WHERE user_id = $1 AND platform = ANY($2::text[]) FOR UPDATE",
        [ownerId, platforms],
      );
      const existing = rows.filter((row) => Boolean(tokens[row.platform]));
      if (existing.length) {
        throw new Error(
          `Cloud OAuth records already exist for user ${ownerId} (${existing.map((row) => row.platform).join(", ")}). Pass --overwrite to replace them.`,
        );
      }
    }
    for (const platform of platforms) {
      const token = tokens[platform];
      if (!token) continue;
      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO public.oauth_tokens (user_id, platform, token_ciphertext, updated_at)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3::jsonb, NOW())
         ON CONFLICT (user_id, platform) DO UPDATE
         SET token_ciphertext = EXCLUDED.token_ciphertext, updated_at = NOW()`,
        [ownerId, platform, JSON.stringify(encryptCloudToken(platform, token))],
      );
    }
    await client.query("COMMIT");
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK").catch(() => undefined);
    throw error;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
}

export async function loadTokens(ownerId: string): Promise<TokenVault> {
  return config.tokenStoreBackend === "supabase" ? loadCloudTokens(ownerId) : readLocalTokens(ownerId);
}

export async function saveTokens(ownerId: string, tokens: TokenVault): Promise<void> {
  if (config.tokenStoreBackend === "supabase") {
    await saveCloudTokens(ownerId, tokens);
    return;
  }
  await saveLocalTokens(ownerId, tokens);
}

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import dotenv from "dotenv";

// Directory for local-only state (developer token vault, scheduled-media files).
// `USERPROFILE`/`HOME` are unset inside serverless runtimes; falling back to the
// OS temp directory keeps the path absolute there so local-mode code never
// attempts a relative mkdir like `.social-media-mcp` (ENOENT). Production must
// use the cloud backends (`TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND=supabase` /
// `SCHEDULED_IMAGE_STORAGE=supabase`) instead of this local fallback.
function localStateDirectory(): string {
  const configured = process.env["USERPROFILE"] || process.env["HOME"];
  if (configured) return configured;
  try {
    return os.homedir() || os.tmpdir();
  } catch {
    return os.tmpdir();
  }
}

dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../.env") });

function value(name: string, fallback = ""): string {
  return process.env[name]?.trim() || fallback;
}

function integer(name: string, fallback: string, minimum: number): number {
  const parsed = Number(value(name, fallback));
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(parsed) || parsed < minimum) {
    throw new Error(`${name} must be an integer greater than or equal to ${minimum}.`);
  }
  return parsed;
}

FILE: social-media-mcp/src/database.ts

import { Pool } from "pg";
import { config } from "./config/env.js";

let pool: Pool | undefined;

export function databasePool(): Pool {
  if (!config.databaseUrl) throw new Error("Set DATABASE_URL to a PostgreSQL connection string to use scheduled posts.");
  if (!pool) {
    pool = new Pool({
      connectionString: config.databaseUrl,
      max: config.databasePoolMax,
      connectionTimeoutMillis: config.databaseConnectionTimeoutMs,
      idleTimeoutMillis: config.databaseIdleTimeoutMs,
      keepAlive: true,
    });
    pool.on("error", (error: Error & { code?: string }) => {
      console.error(JSON.stringify({
        event: "database_pool_error",
        code: error.code ?? "UNKNOWN",
        message: "An idle PostgreSQL connection failed; the pool will establish a replacement as needed.",
      }));
    });
  }
  return pool;
}

export async function closeDatabasePool(): Promise<void> {
  if (!pool) return;
  const currentPool = pool;
  pool = undefined;
  await currentPool.end();
}

import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { databasePool } from "./database.js";

const migrationNames = [
  "001_scheduled_posts.sql",
  "002_scheduled_posts_constraints.sql",
  "003_cloud_scheduling.sql",
  "005_linkedin_documents.sql",
  "006_scheduled_post_media.sql",
  "007_multi_user.sql",
  "008_oauth_sessions.sql",
  "009_user_app_credentials.sql",
  "010_mcp_oauth.sql",
  "011_mcp_media_upload_intents.sql",
  "012_mcp_intent_rate_limit.sql",
];

let migrations: Promise<void> | undefined;

export function ensureDatabaseMigrations(): Promise<void> {
  if (!migrations) {
    migrations = (async () => {
      const directory = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../migrations");
      for (const name of migrationNames) {
        await databasePool().query(await readFile(path.join(directory, name), "utf8"));
      }
    })().catch((error: unknown) => {
      migrations = undefined;
      throw error;
    });
  }
  return migrations;
}

FILE: social-media-mcp/src/mcp/handler.ts

import {
  McpServer,
  createMcpHandler,
  getOAuthProtectedResourceMetadataUrl,
  requireBearerAuth,
  type AuthInfo,
  type McpServerFactory,
} from "@modelcontextprotocol/server";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { verifyMcpAccessToken } from "../auth/mcp-oauth.js";
import { registerTools } from "../tools/register.js";

// Per-request server factory. The bearer-auth gate verifies the opaque access
// token and puts the owning better-auth user id in `authInfo.extra.userId`.
// Every tool then reads and writes only that user's tokens, scheduled posts,
// and connected accounts.
const factory: McpServerFactory = (context) => {
  const userId = typeof context.authInfo?.extra?.["userId"] === "string"
    ? context.authInfo.extra["userId"]
    : undefined;
  if (!userId) throw new Error("MCP request is missing an authenticated user.");
  const server = new McpServer({ name: "social-media-mcp", version: "1.0.0" });
  registerTools(server, { userId, remote: true });
  return server;
};

export const mcpHandler = createMcpHandler(factory, { legacy: "stateless" });

const mcpUrl = new URL(config.mcpPublicUrl);
const resourceMetadataUrl = getOAuthProtectedResourceMetadataUrl(mcpUrl);

export const mcpAuthGate = requireBearerAuth({
  verifier: { verifyAccessToken: verifyMcpAccessToken },
  requiredScopes: [config.mcpOauthScope],
  expectedResource: mcpUrl,
  resourceMetadataUrl,
});

export async function authenticateMcpRequest(request: Request): Promise<AuthInfo | Response> {
  return mcpAuthGate(request);
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/mcp/http-adapter.ts
================================================================================

import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";

// Small Node <-> Web adapter for the MCP Streamable HTTP handler. The SDK's
// `createMcpHandler` is fetch-shaped; `@modelcontextprotocol/node` is not
// installed, so this module converts one node:http request/response pair at a
// time. Bodies are bounded before parsing, and SSE responses are streamed.

export const DEFAULT_MCP_BODY_LIMIT = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

export class HttpBodyTooLargeError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("Request body is too large.");
    this.name = "HttpBodyTooLargeError";
  }
}

function requestUrl(request: IncomingMessage, publicUrl: string): URL {
  return new URL(request.url ?? "/", publicUrl);
}

function requestHeaders(request: IncomingMessage): Headers {
  const headers = new Headers();
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(request.headers)) {
    if (value === undefined) continue;
    if (name === "connection" || name === "transfer-encoding" || name === "keep-alive") continue;
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const item of value) headers.append(name, item);
    } else {
      headers.set(name, value);
    }
  }
  return headers;
}

async function readBody(request: IncomingMessage, maxBytes: number): Promise<Uint8Array | undefined> {
  const declared = Number(request.headers["content-length"] ?? "0");
  if (Number.isFinite(declared) && declared > maxBytes) {
    request.resume();
    throw new HttpBodyTooLargeError();
  }
  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  let total = 0;
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    const buffer = Buffer.isBuffer(chunk) ? chunk : Buffer.from(chunk);
    total += buffer.byteLength;
    if (total > maxBytes) {
      request.resume();
      throw new HttpBodyTooLargeError();
    }
    chunks.push(buffer);
  }
  if (!total) return undefined;
  return Buffer.concat(chunks, total);
}

export async function toWebRequest(
  request: IncomingMessage,
  publicUrl: string,
  maxBytes = DEFAULT_MCP_BODY_LIMIT,
): Promise<Request> {
  const method = (request.method ?? "GET").toUpperCase();
  const body = method === "GET" || method === "HEAD" ? undefined : await readBody(request, maxBytes);
  return new Request(requestUrl(request, publicUrl), {
    method,
    headers: requestHeaders(request),
    ...(body ? { body: Uint8Array.from(body).buffer } : {}),
  });
}

function responseHeaders(response: Response): Record<string, string> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
  response.headers.forEach((value, name) => {
    if (name === "connection" || name === "keep-alive" || name === "transfer-encoding" || name === "upgrade") return;
    headers[name] = value;
  });
  return headers;
}

export async function sendWebResponse(response: ServerResponse, webResponse: Response): Promise<void> {
  const headers = responseHeaders(webResponse);
  const contentType = webResponse.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (contentType.includes("text/event-stream") && webResponse.body) {
    response.writeHead(webResponse.status, headers);
    const reader = webResponse.body.getReader();
    try {
      for (;;) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        if (!response.write(Buffer.from(value))) {
          await new Promise<void>((resolve) => response.once("drain", resolve));
        }
      }
    } finally {
      reader.releaseLock();
    }
    response.end();
    return;
  }
  const body = Buffer.from(await webResponse.arrayBuffer());
  if (webResponse.status !== 204 && webResponse.status !== 304) {
    headers["content-length"] = String(body.byteLength);
  }
  response.writeHead(webResponse.status, headers);
  response.end(body);
}

FILE: social-media-mcp/src/media/upload-endpoint.ts
