import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import {
  UploadIntentError,
  claimPendingMediaUploadIntent,
  completeMediaUploadIntent,
  createSlidingWindowPenaltyBox,
  evaluateMediaUpload,
  extensionForContentType,
  failMediaUploadIntent,
  type MediaUploadIntentRow,
} from "../storage/upload-intents.js";
import {
  deleteScheduledDocument,
  deleteScheduledImage,
  deleteScheduledVideo,
  matchesMediaSignature,
  uploadScheduledDocument,
  uploadScheduledImage,
  uploadScheduledVideo,
} from "../storage/scheduled-images.js";

// Single-use upload endpoint for MCP media intents, served by the cloud worker
// at POST /media/upload. The endpoint authenticates with the per-intent bearer
// token (never an MCP OAuth access token and never the Supabase service-role
// key), claims the intent atomically, validates the declared and actual
// content type, size, and file signature server-side, then writes the bytes
// into the private bucket under the claiming user's per-user prefix. The
// response's `storagePath` is the permanent object reference for scheduling
// and publishing; no expiring signed URL is produced or returned here.

// 1 MiB of transport slack above the largest accepted video.
export const mediaUploadBodyLimit = config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes + 1024 * 1024;

// Failed-attempt penalty box (HTTP 429). Blocks noise from repeated bad tokens
// or rejected bodies: per client address, in-memory per cloud-worker process,
// on top of the cryptographic single-use claim (which already bounds what any
// one token can do). Configured by MEDIA_UPLOAD_ATTEMPT_LIMIT over a rolling
// 10-minute window; successful uploads never record a penalty hit.
const attemptPenalty = createSlidingWindowPenaltyBox({
  maxAttempts: config.mediaUploadAttemptLimit,
  windowMs: 600_000,
});

// Best-effort client attribution for throttling only (never logged raw; the
// Render proxy supplies x-forwarded-for).
function requestClientKey(request: Request): string {
  const forwarded = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0]?.trim();
  return forwarded || "unknown";
}

function redactError(error: unknown): string {
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown upload error.";
  return message
    .replace(/postgres(?:ql)?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/gi, "[database URL redacted]")
    .replace(/Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+/-]+=*/gi, "Bearer [redacted]")
    .slice(0, 500);
}

function jsonResponse(status: number, value: Record<string, unknown>, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(value), {
    status,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store", ...headers },
  });
}

// The capability token may arrive as `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (preferred:
// URLs are commonly logged, bearer headers are not) or as a `?token=` query
// parameter fallback for clients that cannot set headers.
export function extractUploadToken(request: Request): string | undefined {
  const match = /^Bearer\s+(\S+)$/i.exec(request.headers.get("authorization") ?? "");
  if (match) return match[1]!;
  return new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("token") ?? undefined;
}

function requestContentType(request: Request): string {
  return request.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0]?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "";
}

// The intent row is claimed (single-use) before the bytes are accepted, so a
// malformed or hostile upload can never retry against the same token. Any
// rejected or failed attempt burns the intent; the client must request a new
// one. That keeps "one token, at most one accepted upload" unconditional.
export async function mediaUploadResponse(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
  const token = extractUploadToken(request);
  const clientKey = requestClientKey(request);
  try {
    if (request.method !== "POST") {
      return jsonResponse(405, { error: "Use POST." }, { allow: "POST" });
    }
    if (!config.supabaseUrl || !config.supabaseServiceRoleKey || !config.databaseUrl) {
      return jsonResponse(503, { error: "Media uploads are not configured on this deployment." });
    }
    if (attemptPenalty.blocked(clientKey)) {
      console.warn(JSON.stringify({ event: "media_upload_throttled", status: 429, knownClient: clientKey !== "unknown" }));
      return jsonResponse(429, {
        error: "Too many failed upload attempts. Wait ten minutes and request a new upload intent.",
      });
    }
    if (!token || token.length < 32) {
      attemptPenalty.record(clientKey);
      return jsonResponse(401, { error: "Provide the upload token as an Authorization: Bearer header." });
    }
    const intent = await claimPendingMediaUploadIntent(token);
    const userId = intent.user_id;
    const kind = intent.kind as "image" | "video" | "document";
    const declaredContentType: string = intent.content_type;
    try {
      // Read the body exactly once, bounded by the route-level limit.
      const bytes = Buffer.from(await request.arrayBuffer());
      const problem = evaluateMediaUpload(
        { kind, contentType: declaredContentType, expectedBytes: intent.expected_bytes },
        requestContentType(request),
        bytes,
      );
      if (problem) {
        await failMediaUploadIntent(intent.id);
        attemptPenalty.record(clientKey);
        console.warn(JSON.stringify({ event: "media_upload_rejected", intentId: intent.id, status: problem.status }));
        return jsonResponse(problem.status, { error: problem.message });
      }
      if (intent.expected_bytes > 0 && bytes.byteLength !== intent.expected_bytes) {
        // Declared size is metadata: log drift, enforce only the kind limit.
        console.warn(JSON.stringify({
          event: "media_upload_size_mismatch",
          intentId: intent.id,
          declared: intent.expected_bytes,
          actual: bytes.byteLength,
        }));
      }
      // The file signature is validated here (against the bytes, not the
      // header) and again by every later download/validate call in the worker.
      if (!matchesMediaSignature(bytes, declaredContentType)) {
        await failMediaUploadIntent(intent.id);
        attemptPenalty.record(clientKey);
        console.warn(JSON.stringify({ event: "media_upload_bad_signature", intentId: intent.id }));
        return jsonResponse(415, {
          error: `File contents do not match ${declaredContentType}. The upload intent is single-use; request a new one.`,
        });
      }
      const extension = extensionForContentType(declaredContentType)!;
      // Every uploader generates a fresh object path under this user's prefix
      // (scheduled/<owner-segment>/<48-hex><ext>), so the returned reference is
      // owner-scoped and path-traversal-proof by construction.
      const storagePath = kind === "video"
        ? await uploadScheduledVideo(bytes, declaredContentType, extension, userId)
        : kind === "document"
          ? await uploadScheduledDocument(bytes, declaredContentType, extension, userId)
          : await uploadScheduledImage(bytes, declaredContentType, extension, userId);
      let finalized = false;
      try {
        finalized = await completeMediaUploadIntent(intent.id, storagePath, bytes.byteLength);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(JSON.stringify({
          event: "media_upload_finalize_failed",
          intentId: intent.id,
          kind,
          error: redactError(error),
        }));
      }
      if (!finalized) {
        // The reference must not exist without a confirmed record: clean up the
        // object best-effort, then mark the intent terminal so the same token
        // can never be replayed.
        const cleanup = kind === "video"
          ? deleteScheduledVideo(storagePath)
          : kind === "document"
            ? deleteScheduledDocument(storagePath)
            : deleteScheduledImage(storagePath);
        await cleanup.catch((cleanupError: unknown) => {
          console.error(JSON.stringify({
            event: "media_upload_orphan_cleanup_failed",
            intentId: intent.id,
            storagePath,
            error: redactError(cleanupError),
          }));
        });
        await failMediaUploadIntent(intent.id);
        return jsonResponse(500, {
          error: "The upload completed but could not be confirmed. Request a new upload intent and try again.",
        });
      }
      console.info(JSON.stringify({
        event: "media_upload_completed",
        intentId: intent.id,
        kind,
        bytes: bytes.byteLength,
        tokenFingerprint: hashForLogging(token),
      }));
      return jsonResponse(201, {
        storagePath,
        kind,
        contentType: declaredContentType,
        bytes: bytes.byteLength,
        note: "Pass storagePath as imageStoragePath in social_publish, or as media[].storagePath / documentStoragePath when scheduling. It is permanent; never store a signed URL.",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      await failMediaUploadIntent(intent.id);
      throw error;
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof UploadIntentError) {
      // Deliberately log identity without any token material beyond a short,
      // unsalted digest prefix for tracing — never the raw token itself.
      attemptPenalty.record(clientKey);
      console.warn(JSON.stringify({
        event: "media_upload_rejected",
        tokenFingerprint: token ? hashForLogging(token) : "missing",
        status: error.status,
      }));
      return jsonResponse(error.status, { error: error.message });
    }
    attemptPenalty.record(clientKey);
    console.error(JSON.stringify({ event: "media_upload_failed", error: redactError(error) }));
    return jsonResponse(500, { error: "The upload failed. Request a new upload intent and try again." });
  }
}

function hashForLogging(token: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(token, "utf8").digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
}

// Narrow row shape kept on the wire (no token material is ever returned).
export type { MediaUploadIntentRow };

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/platforms/linkedin.ts
================================================================================

import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { loadTokens, saveTokens } from "../auth/token-store.js";
import { resolveAppCredentials, type ResolvedAppCredentials } from "../auth/app-credentials.js";
import { apiRequest } from "../utils/http.js";
import { validateLinkedInAuthorUrn } from "../utils/validation.js";
import type { LinkedInPost, PublishedPost, StoredToken } from "./types.js";

interface LinkedInProfile { sub: string; name?: string; email?: string; picture?: string }
interface LinkedInPostList { elements?: LinkedInPost[] }
interface LinkedInTokenResponse { access_token?: string; refresh_token?: string; expires_in?: number }

const restHeaders = {
  "Linkedin-Version": config.linkedinApiVersion,
  "X-Restli-Protocol-Version": "2.0.0",
};
// Refresh deduplication is tracked per user so concurrent refreshes for different
// accounts never share or leak a token.
const refreshInFlight = new Map<string, Promise<StoredToken>>();

export async function linkedinTokenForUse(
  ownerId: string,
  // Resolves the LinkedIn app credentials for the user. Injectable so tests can
  // exercise the refresh path with user-owned credentials without a database.
  resolveCredentials: (userId: string) => Promise<ResolvedAppCredentials> =
    (userId) => resolveAppCredentials(userId, "linkedin"),
): Promise<StoredToken> {
  const vault = await loadTokens(ownerId);
  const current = vault.linkedin;
  if (!current) throw new Error("LinkedIn is not connected. Start OAuth first.");
  if (current.expiresAt === undefined || current.expiresAt > Date.now() + 60_000) return current;
  if (!current.refreshToken) {
    throw new Error("LinkedIn access token is expired or about to expire and has no refresh token. Reauthorize LinkedIn.");
  }
  // Refresh must use the LinkedIn app that issued the refresh token. A user who
  // configured their own app gets that app; everyone else keeps the shared app.
  const credentials = await resolveCredentials(ownerId);
  if (!credentials.clientId || !credentials.clientSecret) {
    throw new Error("LinkedIn token refresh requires the LinkedIn app client ID and secret. Configure them in Developer App Settings, or set LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID and LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET for the shared app.");
  }
  const inFlight = refreshInFlight.get(ownerId);
  if (inFlight) return inFlight;

  const refresh = (async () => {
    const latestVault = await loadTokens(ownerId);
    const latest = latestVault.linkedin;
    if (!latest) throw new Error("LinkedIn is not connected. Start OAuth first.");
    if (latest.expiresAt === undefined || latest.expiresAt > Date.now() + 60_000) return latest;
    if (!latest.refreshToken) {
      throw new Error("LinkedIn access token is expired and has no refresh token. Reauthorize LinkedIn.");
    }
    let data: LinkedInTokenResponse;
    try {
      ({ data } = await apiRequest<LinkedInTokenResponse>("https://www.linkedin.com/oauth/v2/accessToken", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
        body: new URLSearchParams({
          grant_type: "refresh_token",
          refresh_token: latest.refreshToken,
          client_id: credentials.clientId,
          client_secret: credentials.clientSecret ?? "",
        }),
      }));
    } catch (error) {
      // Changing the app after connecting invalidates the refresh token's issuer.
      // Surface an actionable message instead of silently trying another app.
      throw new Error(`LinkedIn token refresh failed. If you changed your LinkedIn app credentials after connecting, reconnect LinkedIn. ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error."}`);
    }
    if (!data.access_token || !data.expires_in || data.expires_in <= 0) {
      throw new Error("LinkedIn refresh did not return a valid access token and expiry. Reauthorize LinkedIn.");
    }
    const refreshed: StoredToken = {
      ...latest,
      accessToken: data.access_token,
      ...(data.refresh_token ? { refreshToken: data.refresh_token } : {}),
      expiresAt: Date.now() + data.expires_in * 1000,
    };
    await saveTokens(ownerId, { ...latestVault, linkedin: refreshed });
    return refreshed;
  })().finally(() => {
    refreshInFlight.delete(ownerId);
  });

  refreshInFlight.set(ownerId, refresh);
  return refresh;
}

export function normalizeLinkedInScopes(scopes: string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(scopes.flatMap((scope) => scope.split(/[,\s]+/)).filter(Boolean))];
}

function authorUrn(input?: string, accountId?: string): string {
  const author = input || config.linkedinAuthorUrn || (accountId ? `urn:li:person:${accountId}` : "");
  if (!author) throw new Error("Set LINKEDIN_AUTHOR_URN or provide an authorUrn.");
  const issue = validateLinkedInAuthorUrn(author);
  if (issue) throw new Error(issue);
  return author;
}

export async function linkedinConnectionStatus(ownerId: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const token = (await loadTokens(ownerId)).linkedin;
  if (!token) return { platform: "linkedin", connected: false, reason: "Authorize this LinkedIn app first." };
  const scopes = normalizeLinkedInScopes(token.scopes);
  try {
    const { data } = await apiRequest<LinkedInProfile>("https://api.linkedin.com/v2/userinfo", { accessToken: token.accessToken });
    return {
      platform: "linkedin",
      connected: true,
      accountName: data.name ?? token.accountName,
      grantedScopes: scopes,
      expiresAt: token.expiresAt,
      tokenExpired: token.expiresAt !== undefined && token.expiresAt <= Date.now(),
      memberPublishingAvailable: scopes.includes("w_member_social"),
      organizationPublishingAvailable: scopes.includes("w_organization_social"),
      organizationReadingAvailable: scopes.includes("r_organization_social"),
      memberPostReadingAvailable: scopes.includes("r_member_social"),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return { platform: "linkedin", connected: false, reason: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Token validation failed." };
  }
}

export async function linkedinAccountInfo(ownerId: string, author?: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const token = await linkedinTokenForUse(ownerId);
  const { data } = await apiRequest<LinkedInProfile>("https://api.linkedin.com/v2/userinfo", { accessToken: token.accessToken });
  const scopes = normalizeLinkedInScopes(token.scopes);
  return {
    platform: "linkedin",
    memberId: data.sub,
    name: data.name ?? token.accountName,
    ...(author ? { selectedAuthorUrn: authorUrn(author, data.sub) } : {}),
    grantedScopes: scopes,
  };
}

export async function readLinkedInPosts(ownerId: string, count: number, author?: string): Promise<LinkedInPost[]> {
  const token = await linkedinTokenForUse(ownerId);
  const selectedAuthor = authorUrn(author, token.accountId);
  const readScope = selectedAuthor.startsWith("urn:li:organization:") ? "r_organization_social" : "r_member_social";
  if (!normalizeLinkedInScopes(token.scopes).includes(readScope)) {
    throw new Error(`LinkedIn post reading requires ${readScope}, which is not granted to this app. LinkedIn restricts r_member_social to approved users.`);
  }
  const query = new URLSearchParams({
    author: selectedAuthor,
    q: "author",
    count: String(count),
    sortBy: "CREATED",
  });
  const { data } = await apiRequest<LinkedInPostList>(`https://api.linkedin.com/rest/posts?${query}`, {
    accessToken: token.accessToken,
    headers: restHeaders,
  });
  return data.elements ?? [];
}

function confirmedId(response: Response): string {
  const id = response.headers.get("x-restli-id");
  if (!id) throw new Error("LinkedIn returned success without x-restli-id; publication is unconfirmed.");
  return id;
}

async function uploadImage(
  token: string,
  author: string,
  imagePath?: string,
  imageData?: { bytes: Buffer; contentType: string },
): Promise<string> {
  const image = imageData?.bytes ?? (imagePath ? await readFile(imagePath) : undefined);
  if (!image) throw new Error("A LinkedIn image path or image buffer is required.");
  if (!image.byteLength) throw new Error("The selected LinkedIn image file is empty.");
  const mimeType = imageData?.contentType ?? (imagePath?.toLowerCase().endsWith(".png")
    ? "image/png"
    : imagePath?.toLowerCase().endsWith(".gif") ? "image/gif" : "image/jpeg");
  const initialized = await apiRequest<{ value?: { uploadUrl?: string; image?: string } }>(
    "https://api.linkedin.com/rest/images?action=initializeUpload",
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { ...restHeaders, "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ initializeUploadRequest: { owner: author } }),
      accessToken: token,
    },
  );
  const uploadUrl = initialized.data.value?.uploadUrl;
  const imageUrn = initialized.data.value?.image;
  if (!uploadUrl || !imageUrn) throw new Error("LinkedIn did not return an image upload URL and image URN.");
  const imageBody = new ArrayBuffer(image.byteLength);
  new Uint8Array(imageBody).set(image);
  const response = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: { "content-type": mimeType }, body: imageBody });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`LinkedIn image upload failed with HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  return imageUrn;
}

async function uploadDocument(
  token: string,
  author: string,
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
): Promise<string> {
  if (!bytes.byteLength) throw new Error("The selected LinkedIn document file is empty.");
  const initialized = await apiRequest<{ value?: { uploadUrl?: string; document?: string } }>(
    "https://api.linkedin.com/rest/documents?action=initializeUpload",
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { ...restHeaders, "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ initializeUploadRequest: { owner: author } }),
      accessToken: token,
    },
  );
  const uploadUrl = initialized.data.value?.uploadUrl;
  const documentUrn = initialized.data.value?.document;
  if (!uploadUrl || !documentUrn) throw new Error("LinkedIn did not return a document upload URL and document URN.");
  const documentBody = new ArrayBuffer(bytes.byteLength);
  new Uint8Array(documentBody).set(bytes);
  const response = await fetch(uploadUrl, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { "content-type": contentType },
    body: documentBody,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`LinkedIn document upload failed with HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  return documentUrn;
}

async function uploadVideo(
  token: string,
  author: string,
  bytes: Buffer,
): Promise<string> {
  if (!bytes.byteLength) throw new Error("The selected LinkedIn video file is empty.");
  const initialized = await apiRequest<{
    value?: {
      uploadToken?: string;
      video?: string;
      uploadInstructions?: Array<{ uploadUrl: string; firstByte: number; lastByte: number }>;
    };
  }>(
    "https://api.linkedin.com/rest/videos?action=initializeUpload",
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { ...restHeaders, "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        initializeUploadRequest: { owner: author, fileSizeBytes: bytes.byteLength },
      }),
      accessToken: token,
    },
  );
  const uploadToken = initialized.data.value?.uploadToken;
  const videoUrn = initialized.data.value?.video;
  const instructions = initialized.data.value?.uploadInstructions ?? [];
  if (!uploadToken || !videoUrn || instructions.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("LinkedIn did not return a video upload token, video URN, and upload instructions.");
  }
  const uploadedPartIds: string[] = [];
  for (const instruction of instructions) {
    const start = instruction.firstByte;
    const end = Math.min(instruction.lastByte + 1, bytes.byteLength);
    const slice = bytes.subarray(start, end);
    const body = new ArrayBuffer(slice.byteLength);
    new Uint8Array(body).set(slice);
    const response = await fetch(instruction.uploadUrl, {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/octet-stream" },
      body,
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`LinkedIn video part upload failed with HTTP ${response.status}.`);
    const etag = response.headers.get("etag")?.replace(/^"|"$/g, "");
    if (etag) uploadedPartIds.push(etag);
    await response.body?.cancel();
  }
  await apiRequest("https://api.linkedin.com/rest/videos?action=finalizeUpload", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { ...restHeaders, "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      finalizeUploadRequest: { video: videoUrn, uploadToken, uploadedPartIds },
    }),
    accessToken: token,
  });
  return videoUrn;
}

async function publishLinkedInInternal(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  imagePath: string | undefined,
  requestedAuthor: string | undefined,
  imageData?: { bytes: Buffer; contentType: string },
  document?: { bytes: Buffer; contentType: string; title: string },
  video?: { bytes: Buffer; contentType: string },
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  const token = await linkedinTokenForUse(ownerId);
  const author = authorUrn(requestedAuthor, token.accountId);
  const writeScope = author.startsWith("urn:li:organization:") ? "w_organization_social" : "w_member_social";
  if (!normalizeLinkedInScopes(token.scopes).includes(writeScope)) {
    throw new Error(`LinkedIn publishing requires the granted ${writeScope} scope. Check your app's approved LinkedIn product and reconnect.`);
  }
  let content: { media: { id: string; title?: string } } | undefined;
  if (document) {
    const documentUrn = await uploadDocument(token.accessToken, author, document.bytes, document.contentType);
    content = { media: { id: documentUrn, title: document.title } };
  } else if (video) {
    const videoUrn = await uploadVideo(token.accessToken, author, video.bytes);
    content = { media: { id: videoUrn } };
  } else if (imagePath || imageData) {
    const imageUrn = await uploadImage(token.accessToken, author, imagePath, imageData);
    content = { media: { id: imageUrn } };
  }
  const post = {
    author,
    commentary: text ?? "",
    visibility: "PUBLIC",
    distribution: { feedDistribution: "MAIN_FEED", targetEntities: [], thirdPartyDistributionChannels: [] },
    ...(content ? { content } : {}),
    lifecycleState: "PUBLISHED",
    isReshareDisabledByAuthor: false,
  };
  const { response } = await apiRequest("https://api.linkedin.com/rest/posts", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { ...restHeaders, "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(post),
    accessToken: token.accessToken,
  });
  return { platform: "linkedin", id: confirmedId(response), confirmed: true };
}

export async function publishLinkedIn(ownerId: string, text?: string, imagePath?: string, requestedAuthor?: string): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  return publishLinkedInInternal(ownerId, text, imagePath, requestedAuthor);
}

export async function publishLinkedInWithImage(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
  requestedAuthor?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  return publishLinkedInInternal(ownerId, text, undefined, requestedAuthor, { bytes, contentType });
}

export async function publishLinkedInWithDocument(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
  title: string,
  requestedAuthor?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  return publishLinkedInInternal(ownerId, text, undefined, requestedAuthor, undefined, { bytes, contentType, title });
}

export async function publishLinkedInWithVideo(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
  requestedAuthor?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  return publishLinkedInInternal(ownerId, text, undefined, requestedAuthor, undefined, undefined, { bytes, contentType });
}

import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { loadTokens } from "../auth/token-store.js";
import { apiRequest, explainError } from "../utils/http.js";
import type { MetaPage, MetaPlatform, PublishedPost, StoredToken } from "./types.js";

interface MetaProfile { id?: string; user_id?: string | number; name?: string; username?: string; account_type?: string }
interface MetaMedia { id: string; caption?: string; media_type?: string; permalink?: string; timestamp?: string }
interface MetaList<T> { data?: T[] }
interface InstagramContainerStatus { status_code?: "IN_PROGRESS" | "FINISHED" | "ERROR" | "EXPIRED"; }

function facebookGraph(path: string): string {
  return `https://graph.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/${path}`;
}

function facebookGraphVideo(path: string): string {
  return `https://graph-video.facebook.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/${path}`;
}

function instagramGraph(path: string): string {
  return `https://graph.instagram.com/${config.metaGraphVersion}/${path}`;
}

function pageForFacebook(pages: MetaPage[], pageId?: string): MetaPage {
  const selectedId = pageId || config.facebookPageId;
  const page = selectedId ? pages.find((entry) => entry.id === selectedId) : pages[0];
  if (!page) throw new Error("No authorized Facebook Page is available. Connect Facebook and choose an accessible Page ID.");
  if (!page.tasks.some((task) => ["CREATE_CONTENT", "MANAGE"].includes(task))) {
    throw new Error("The connected account lacks a content-management task on this Page.");
  }
  return page;
}

function selectInstagramAccountId(accountId: string | undefined, tokenAccountId: string | undefined): string {
  const selectedId = accountId || tokenAccountId;
  if (!selectedId) throw new Error("No Instagram account ID is available. Reconnect Instagram or provide instagramAccountId.");
  return selectedId;
}

export async function waitForInstagramContainer(containerId: string, accessToken: string): Promise<void> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + 120_000;
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const { data } = await apiRequest<InstagramContainerStatus>(
      instagramGraph(`${containerId}?fields=status_code`),
      { accessToken },
    );
    if (data.status_code === "FINISHED") return;
    if (data.status_code === "ERROR" || data.status_code === "EXPIRED") {
      throw new Error(`Instagram media container processing ended with status ${data.status_code}.`);
    }
    if (data.status_code !== "IN_PROGRESS") {
      throw new Error("Instagram returned an unknown media container processing status.");
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
  }
  throw new Error("Instagram media container did not finish processing within 120 seconds.");
}

export async function metaConnectionStatus(ownerId: string, platform: MetaPlatform): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const token = (await loadTokens(ownerId))[platform];
  if (!token) return { platform, connected: false, reason: "Authorize this platform using social_authorization_start." };
  const requiredScopes = platform === "facebook"
    ? ["pages_show_list", "pages_read_engagement", "pages_manage_posts"]
    : ["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"];
  const missingScopes = requiredScopes.filter((scope) => !token.scopes.includes(scope));
  try {
    const profile = platform === "facebook"
      ? await apiRequest<MetaProfile>(facebookGraph("me?fields=id,name"), { accessToken: token.accessToken })
      : await apiRequest<MetaProfile>(instagramGraph("me?fields=user_id,username"), { accessToken: token.accessToken });
    const hasPages = (token.pages ?? []).length > 0;
    return {
      platform,
      connected: true,
      tokenExpired: token.expiresAt !== undefined && token.expiresAt <= Date.now(),
      grantedPermissions: token.scopes,
      missingPermissions: missingScopes,
      ...(platform === "facebook"
        ? { accessiblePageCount: token.pages?.length ?? 0 }
        : { professionalAccountAvailable: Boolean(profile.data.user_id ?? profile.data.id) }),
      ...(platform === "facebook" && !hasPages ? { warning: "No managed Pages were returned during authorization." } : {}),
      ...(platform === "instagram" && !(profile.data.user_id ?? profile.data.id)
        ? { warning: "No Instagram professional account ID was returned by Instagram Login." }
        : {}),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return { platform, connected: false, reason: explainError(error) };
  }
}

export async function metaAccountInfo(ownerId: string, platform: MetaPlatform): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const token = (await loadTokens(ownerId))[platform];
  if (!token) throw new Error(`${platform} is not connected. Start OAuth first.`);
  if (platform === "facebook") {
    const { data } = await apiRequest<MetaProfile>(facebookGraph("me?fields=id,name"), { accessToken: token.accessToken });
    return {
      platform,
      id: data.id,
      name: data.name,
      pages: (token.pages ?? []).map((page) => ({ id: page.id, name: page.name, tasks: page.tasks })),
    };
  }
  const { data } = await apiRequest<MetaProfile>(
    instagramGraph("me?fields=user_id,username,account_type"),
    { accessToken: token.accessToken },
  );
  return { platform, ...data, id: data.user_id ?? data.id };
}

export async function readMetaPosts(ownerId: string, platform: MetaPlatform, count: number): Promise<MetaMedia[]> {
  const token = (await loadTokens(ownerId))[platform];
  if (!token) throw new Error(`${platform} is not connected. Start OAuth first.`);
  const requiredScopes = platform === "facebook"
    ? ["pages_show_list", "pages_read_engagement"]
    : ["instagram_business_basic"];
  const missingScopes = requiredScopes.filter((scope) => !token.scopes.includes(scope));
  if (missingScopes.length) throw new Error(`The ${platform} token is missing granted permission(s): ${missingScopes.join(", ")}. Reconnect and grant them.`);
  if (platform === "facebook") {
    const page = pageForFacebook(token.pages ?? []);
    const { data } = await apiRequest<MetaList<MetaMedia>>(
      facebookGraph(`${page.id}/feed?fields=id,message,created_time,permalink_url&limit=${count}`),
      { accessToken: page.accessToken },
    );
    return data.data ?? [];
  }
  const accountId = selectInstagramAccountId(undefined, token.accountId);
  const { data } = await apiRequest<MetaList<MetaMedia>>(
    instagramGraph(`${accountId}/media?fields=id,caption,media_type,permalink,timestamp&limit=${count}`),
    { accessToken: token.accessToken },
  );
  return data.data ?? [];
}

async function requireMetaToken(ownerId: string, platform: MetaPlatform): Promise<StoredToken> {
  const token = (await loadTokens(ownerId))[platform];
  if (!token) throw new Error(`${platform} is not connected. Start OAuth first.`);
  const requiredScopes = platform === "facebook"
    ? ["pages_show_list", "pages_read_engagement", "pages_manage_posts"]
    : ["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"];
  const missingScopes = requiredScopes.filter((scope) => !token.scopes.includes(scope));
  if (missingScopes.length) throw new Error(`The ${platform} token is missing granted permission(s): ${missingScopes.join(", ")}. Reconnect and grant them.`);
  return token;
}

async function createInstagramContainer(
  accountId: string,
  params: URLSearchParams,
  accessToken: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const container = await apiRequest<{ id?: string }>(instagramGraph(`${accountId}/media`), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body: params,
    accessToken,
  });
  if (!container.data.id) throw new Error("Instagram did not return a media container ID.");
  return container.data.id;
}

async function publishInstagramContainer(
  accountId: string,
  creationId: string,
  accessToken: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const published = await apiRequest<{ id?: string }>(instagramGraph(`${accountId}/media_publish`), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body: new URLSearchParams({ creation_id: creationId }),
    accessToken,
  });
  if (!published.data.id) throw new Error("Instagram did not confirm publication with a media ID.");
  return published.data.id;
}

export async function publishFacebookVideo(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  videoUrl: string,
  pageId?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  const token = await requireMetaToken(ownerId, "facebook");
  const page = pageForFacebook(token.pages ?? [], pageId);
  const { data } = await apiRequest<{ id?: string }>(facebookGraphVideo(`${page.id}/videos`), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body: new URLSearchParams({
      file_url: videoUrl,
      ...(text ? { description: text, title: text.slice(0, 255) } : {}),
    }),
    accessToken: page.accessToken,
  });
  if (!data.id) throw new Error("Facebook did not return a video ID; publication is unconfirmed.");
  return { platform: "facebook", id: data.id, confirmed: true };
}

export async function publishInstagramReel(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  videoUrl: string,
  instagramAccountId?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  const token = await requireMetaToken(ownerId, "instagram");
  const accountId = selectInstagramAccountId(instagramAccountId, token.accountId);
  const creationId = await createInstagramContainer(
    accountId,
    new URLSearchParams({ media_type: "REELS", video_url: videoUrl, ...(text ? { caption: text } : {}) }),
    token.accessToken,
  );
  await waitForInstagramContainer(creationId, token.accessToken);
  const publishedId = await publishInstagramContainer(accountId, creationId, token.accessToken);
  return { platform: "instagram", id: publishedId, confirmed: true };
}

export async function publishInstagramCarousel(
  ownerId: string,
  text: string | undefined,
  imageUrls: string[],
  instagramAccountId?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  if (imageUrls.length < 2 || imageUrls.length > 10) {
    throw new Error("An Instagram carousel must contain between 2 and 10 images.");
  }
  const token = await requireMetaToken(ownerId, "instagram");
  const accountId = selectInstagramAccountId(instagramAccountId, token.accountId);
  const children: string[] = [];
  for (const imageUrl of imageUrls) {
    children.push(await createInstagramContainer(
      accountId,
      new URLSearchParams({ image_url: imageUrl, is_carousel_item: "true" }),
      token.accessToken,
    ));
  }
  const creationId = await createInstagramContainer(
    accountId,
    new URLSearchParams({ media_type: "CAROUSEL", children: children.join(","), ...(text ? { caption: text } : {}) }),
    token.accessToken,
  );
  await waitForInstagramContainer(creationId, token.accessToken);
  const publishedId = await publishInstagramContainer(accountId, creationId, token.accessToken);
  return { platform: "instagram", id: publishedId, confirmed: true };
}

export async function publishMeta(
  ownerId: string,
  platform: MetaPlatform,
  text: string | undefined,
  imageUrl: string | undefined,
  pageId?: string,
  instagramAccountId?: string,
): Promise<PublishedPost> {
  const token = (await loadTokens(ownerId))[platform];
  if (!token) throw new Error(`${platform} is not connected. Start OAuth first.`);
  const requiredScopes = platform === "facebook"
    ? ["pages_show_list", "pages_read_engagement", "pages_manage_posts"]
    : ["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"];
  const missingScopes = requiredScopes.filter((scope) => !token.scopes.includes(scope));
  if (missingScopes.length) throw new Error(`The ${platform} token is missing granted permission(s): ${missingScopes.join(", ")}. Reconnect and grant them.`);
  if (platform === "facebook") {
    const page = pageForFacebook(token.pages ?? [], pageId);
    if (imageUrl) {
      const { data } = await apiRequest<{ id?: string; post_id?: string }>(facebookGraph(`${page.id}/photos`), {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
        body: new URLSearchParams({ url: imageUrl, ...(text ? { message: text } : {}) }),
        accessToken: page.accessToken,
      });
      const id = data.post_id ?? data.id;
      if (!id) throw new Error("Facebook did not return a post or photo ID; publication is unconfirmed.");
      return { platform, id, confirmed: true };
    }
    const { data } = await apiRequest<{ id?: string }>(facebookGraph(`${page.id}/feed`), {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body: new URLSearchParams({ message: text ?? "" }),
      accessToken: page.accessToken,
    });
    if (!data.id) throw new Error("Facebook did not return a post ID; publication is unconfirmed.");
    return { platform, id: data.id, confirmed: true };
  }
  if (!imageUrl) throw new Error("Instagram publishing requires a publicly reachable HTTPS image URL.");
  const accountId = selectInstagramAccountId(instagramAccountId, token.accountId);
  const container = await apiRequest<{ id?: string }>(instagramGraph(`${accountId}/media`), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body: new URLSearchParams({ image_url: imageUrl, ...(text ? { caption: text } : {}) }),
    accessToken: token.accessToken,
  });
  if (!container.data.id) throw new Error("Instagram did not return a media container ID.");
  await waitForInstagramContainer(container.data.id, token.accessToken);
  const published = await apiRequest<{ id?: string }>(instagramGraph(`${accountId}/media_publish`), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body: new URLSearchParams({ creation_id: container.data.id }),
    accessToken: token.accessToken,
  });
  if (!published.data.id) throw new Error("Instagram did not confirm publication with a media ID.");
  return { platform, id: published.data.id, confirmed: true };
}

import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdir, readFile, unlink, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import type { PoolClient, QueryResultRow } from "pg";
import { config } from "./config/env.js";
import { databasePool } from "./database.js";

import { registerScheduledMedia, unregisterScheduledMedia } from "./auth/oauth.js";
import { publishMeta, publishFacebookVideo, publishInstagramReel, publishInstagramCarousel } from "./platforms/meta.js";
import { linkedinTokenForUse, publishLinkedIn, publishLinkedInWithDocument, publishLinkedInWithImage, publishLinkedInWithVideo } from "./platforms/linkedin.js";
import type { MetaPlatform, Platform, PublishedPost } from "./platforms/types.js";
import {
  assertOwnedStorageObjectPath,
  createScheduledImageUrl,
  deleteScheduledDocument,
  deleteScheduledImage,
  deleteScheduledVideo,
  downloadScheduledDocument,
  downloadScheduledImage,
  downloadScheduledVideo,
