import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import type { ScheduledImageValidation } from "../utils/scheduled-image.js";

export interface StoredImage {
  bytes: Buffer;
  contentType: string;
}

export interface StoredDocumentValidation {
  valid: boolean;
  checkedAt: string;
  contentType?: string;
  bytes?: number;
  error?: string;
  retryable: boolean;
}

const imageTypes = new Set(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/gif"]);
const documentTypes = new Set(["application/pdf"]);
const videoTypes = new Set(["video/mp4", "video/quicktime"]);

export function isPdf(bytes: Uint8Array): boolean {
  return bytes.length >= 5
    && bytes[0] === 0x25 && bytes[1] === 0x50 && bytes[2] === 0x44 && bytes[3] === 0x46 && bytes[4] === 0x2d;
}

export function isIsoBmffVideo(bytes: Uint8Array): boolean {
  return bytes.length >= 12
    && bytes[4] === 0x66 && bytes[5] === 0x74 && bytes[6] === 0x79 && bytes[7] === 0x70;
}

export function matchesMediaSignature(bytes: Uint8Array, mimeType: string): boolean {
  return matchesSignature(bytes, mimeType);
}

function matchesSignature(bytes: Uint8Array, mimeType: string): boolean {
  if (videoTypes.has(mimeType)) return isIsoBmffVideo(bytes);
  if (mimeType === "image/jpeg") return bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8 && bytes[2] === 0xff;
  if (mimeType === "image/png") {
    return bytes.length >= 8 && bytes[0] === 0x89 && bytes[1] === 0x50 && bytes[2] === 0x4e
      && bytes[3] === 0x47 && bytes[4] === 0x0d && bytes[5] === 0x0a && bytes[6] === 0x1a && bytes[7] === 0x0a;
  }
  if (mimeType === "application/pdf") return isPdf(bytes);
  return mimeType === "image/gif" && bytes.length >= 6
    && /^GIF8[79]a$/.test(String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(0, 6)));
}

async function readBounded(response: Response, maxBytes: number): Promise<Buffer> {
  if (!response.body) throw new Error("Storage endpoint returned an empty response.");
  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let length = 0;
  try {
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      length += value.byteLength;
      if (length > maxBytes) {
        await reader.cancel();
        throw new Error("Stored object exceeds its configured maximum size.");
      }
      chunks.push(value);
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks.map((chunk) => Buffer.from(chunk)), length);
}

function storageUrl(path = ""): string {
  const encodedPath = path.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/");
  return `${config.supabaseUrl}/storage/v1/object/${encodedPath}`;
}

function storageHeaders(contentType?: string): HeadersInit {
  return {
    apikey: config.supabaseServiceRoleKey,
    authorization: `Bearer ${config.supabaseServiceRoleKey}`,
    ...(contentType ? { "content-type": contentType } : {}),
  };
}

async function responseError(response: Response): Promise<never> {
  await response.body?.cancel();
  throw new Error(`Supabase Storage request failed with HTTP ${response.status}.`);
}

// --- Per-user storage namespacing -------------------------------------------
//
// Every object lives under `scheduled/<owner-segment>/<random><ext>`. The
// owner-segment is derived from the authenticated user id so one user's media
// can never collide with, or be resolved as, another user's object. The bucket
// stays private and is only reachable with the service-role key, so the prefix
// is defense in depth rather than the sole access control.
//
// A storage object path is a permanent reference: it is what scheduling rows
// store, and the worker re-downloads it (and re-signs a fresh URL) at publish
// time. A storage object path is never an expiring signed URL.

export function storageOwnerSegment(ownerId: string): string {
  const trimmed = ownerId.trim();
  if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(trimmed)) return trimmed;
  // Fall back to a stable, URL-safe digest for ids with unusual characters so
  // the segment can never contain a path separator or resolve above `scheduled/`.
  return createHash("sha256").update(trimmed || "anonymous").digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
}

export function storageOwnerPrefix(ownerId: string): string {
  return `scheduled/${storageOwnerSegment(ownerId)}/`;
}

const storageFilePattern = /^[a-f0-9]{48}\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png|gif|mp4|mov|pdf)$/;

export function isOwnedStorageObjectPath(objectPath: string, ownerId: string): boolean {
  if (typeof objectPath !== "string") return false;
  const prefix = storageOwnerPrefix(ownerId);
  if (!objectPath.startsWith(prefix)) return false;
  return storageFilePattern.test(objectPath.slice(prefix.length));
}

// Throws unless `objectPath` is a permanent private-storage reference created
// for this exact user. Caller-supplied references (for example from the upload
// API) are validated with this before they are ever persisted or downloaded,
// which prevents a request from referencing another user's media or an
// arbitrary/expiring URL.
export function assertOwnedStorageObjectPath(objectPath: string, ownerId: string): string {
  if (!isOwnedStorageObjectPath(objectPath, ownerId)) {
    throw new Error("The media storage reference does not belong to this account.");
  }
  return objectPath;
}

export function scheduledObjectPath(ownerId: string, extension: string): string {
  return `${storageOwnerPrefix(ownerId)}${randomBytes(24).toString("hex")}${extension.toLowerCase()}`;
}

interface UploadOptions {
  bytes: Buffer;
  contentType: string;
  extension: string;
  kind: "image" | "document" | "video";
  allowed: Set<string>;
  maxBytes: number;
  ownerId: string;
}

async function uploadObject({ bytes, contentType, extension, kind, allowed, maxBytes, ownerId }: UploadOptions): Promise<string> {
  if (!allowed.has(contentType)) throw new Error(`Unsupported scheduled ${kind} MIME type: ${contentType}.`);
  if (bytes.byteLength === 0 || bytes.byteLength > maxBytes) {
    throw new Error(`Scheduled ${kind} is empty or exceeds its configured maximum size.`);
  }
  if (!matchesSignature(bytes, contentType)) throw new Error(`Selected ${kind} data does not match ${contentType}.`);
  const objectPath = scheduledObjectPath(ownerId, extension);
  const body = new ArrayBuffer(bytes.byteLength);
  new Uint8Array(body).set(bytes);
  const url = storageUrl(`${config.supabaseStorageBucket}/${objectPath}`);
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= config.scheduleImageMaxAttempts; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { ...storageHeaders(contentType), "x-upsert": "true" },
        body,
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs),
      });
      if (response.ok) return objectPath;
      const retryable = [408, 425, 429].includes(response.status) || response.status >= 500;
      const status = response.status;
      await response.body?.cancel();
      if (!retryable || attempt === config.scheduleImageMaxAttempts) {
        throw new Error(`Supabase Storage ${kind} upload failed with HTTP ${status}.`);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown Storage upload error.";
      const retryable = error instanceof TypeError
        || (error instanceof Error && ["TimeoutError", "AbortError"].includes(error.name));
      if (!retryable || attempt === config.scheduleImageMaxAttempts) {
        throw new Error(`Supabase Storage ${kind} upload failed after ${attempt} attempt(s): ${message}`);
      }
    }
    const delayMs = Math.min(
      config.scheduleImageRetryBaseDelayMs * 2 ** (attempt - 1),
      config.scheduleImageRetryMaxDelayMs,
    );
    console.warn(JSON.stringify({
      event: `scheduled_${kind}_storage_upload_retry`,
      retryCount: attempt,
      delayMs,
    }));
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs));
  }
  throw new Error(`Supabase Storage ${kind} upload retry loop ended unexpectedly.`);
}

export async function uploadScheduledImage(
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
  extension: string,
  ownerId: string,
): Promise<string> {
  return uploadObject({
    bytes,
    contentType,
    extension,
    kind: "image",
    allowed: imageTypes,
    maxBytes: config.scheduleImageMaxBytes,
    ownerId,
  });
}

export async function uploadScheduledDocument(
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
  extension: string,
  ownerId: string,
): Promise<string> {
  return uploadObject({
    bytes,
    contentType,
    extension,
    kind: "document",
    allowed: documentTypes,
    maxBytes: config.scheduleDocumentMaxBytes,
    ownerId,
  });
}

export async function uploadScheduledVideo(
  bytes: Buffer,
  contentType: string,
  extension: string,
  ownerId: string,
): Promise<string> {
  return uploadObject({
    bytes,
    contentType,
    extension,
    kind: "video",
    allowed: videoTypes,
    maxBytes: config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes,
    ownerId,
  });
}

async function deleteObject(objectPath: string): Promise<void> {
  const response = await fetch(storageUrl(`${config.supabaseStorageBucket}/${objectPath}`), {
    method: "DELETE",
    headers: storageHeaders(),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs),
  });
  if (!response.ok) await responseError(response);
}

export async function deleteScheduledImage(objectPath: string): Promise<void> {
  await deleteObject(objectPath);
}

export async function deleteScheduledDocument(objectPath: string): Promise<void> {
  await deleteObject(objectPath);
}

export async function deleteScheduledVideo(objectPath: string): Promise<void> {
  await deleteObject(objectPath);
}

interface DownloadOptions {
  objectPath: string;
  kind: "image" | "document" | "video";
  allowed: Set<string>;
  maxBytes: number;
}

async function downloadObject({ objectPath, kind, allowed, maxBytes }: DownloadOptions): Promise<StoredImage> {
  const response = await fetch(storageUrl(`${config.supabaseStorageBucket}/${objectPath}`), {
    headers: storageHeaders(),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs),
  });
  if (!response.ok) await responseError(response);
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0]?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const declaredLength = Number(response.headers.get("content-length") ?? "0");
  if (!allowed.has(contentType)) throw new Error(`Stored ${kind} has invalid MIME type: ${contentType || "missing"}.`);
  if (declaredLength > maxBytes) throw new Error(`Stored ${kind} exceeds its configured maximum size.`);
  const bytes = await readBounded(response, maxBytes);
  if (bytes.byteLength === 0 || bytes.byteLength > maxBytes) {
    throw new Error(`Stored ${kind} is empty or exceeds its configured maximum size.`);
  }
  if (!matchesSignature(bytes, contentType)) throw new Error(`Stored ${kind} data does not match ${contentType}.`);
  return { bytes, contentType };
}

export async function downloadScheduledImage(objectPath: string): Promise<StoredImage> {
  return downloadObject({
    objectPath,
    kind: "image",
    allowed: imageTypes,
    maxBytes: config.scheduleImageMaxBytes,
  });
}

export async function downloadScheduledDocument(objectPath: string): Promise<StoredImage> {
  return downloadObject({
    objectPath,
    kind: "document",
    allowed: documentTypes,
    maxBytes: config.scheduleDocumentMaxBytes,
  });
}

export async function downloadScheduledVideo(objectPath: string): Promise<StoredImage> {
  return downloadObject({
    objectPath,
    kind: "video",
    allowed: videoTypes,
    maxBytes: config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes,
  });
}

export async function createScheduledImageUrl(
  objectPath: string,
  expiresInSeconds: number = config.scheduleSignedUrlTtlSeconds,
): Promise<string> {
  const response = await fetch(storageUrl(`sign/${config.supabaseStorageBucket}/${objectPath}`), {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { ...storageHeaders(), "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ expiresIn: expiresInSeconds }),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs),
  });
  if (!response.ok) await responseError(response);
  const result = await response.json() as { signedURL?: string };
  if (!result.signedURL) throw new Error("Supabase Storage did not return a signed image URL.");
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(result.signedURL)) return result.signedURL;
  const signedPath = result.signedURL.replace(/^\/+/, "");
  const storagePath = signedPath.startsWith("storage/v1/") ? signedPath : `storage/v1/${signedPath}`;
  return new URL(storagePath, `${config.supabaseUrl}/`).toString();
}

export async function validatePublicImageUrl(url: string, expectedType: string): Promise<void> {
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs) });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.name === "TimeoutError") {
      throw new Error("Signed public image URL validation timed out.");
    }
    throw new Error("Signed public image URL is not reachable.");
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    await response.body?.cancel();
    throw new Error(`Signed public image URL returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  }
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0]?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "";
  if (!imageTypes.has(contentType) || contentType !== expectedType) {
    await response.body?.cancel();
    throw new Error(`Signed public image URL returned invalid MIME type: ${contentType || "missing"}.`);
  }
  const declaredLength = Number(response.headers.get("content-length") ?? "0");
  if (declaredLength > config.scheduleImageMaxBytes) {
    await response.body?.cancel();
    throw new Error("Signed public image URL exceeds SCHEDULE_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES.");
  }
  const bytes = await readBounded(response, config.scheduleImageMaxBytes);
  if (!bytes.byteLength || bytes.byteLength > config.scheduleImageMaxBytes) {
    throw new Error("Signed public image URL returned empty or oversized data.");
  }
  if (!matchesSignature(bytes, contentType)) throw new Error(`Signed public image URL did not contain valid ${contentType} data.`);
}

export async function validatePublicVideoUrl(url: string, expectedType: string): Promise<void> {
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs) });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.name === "TimeoutError") {
      throw new Error("Signed public video URL validation timed out.");
    }
    throw new Error("Signed public video URL is not reachable.");
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    await response.body?.cancel();
    throw new Error(`Signed public video URL returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  }
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0]?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "";
  if (!videoTypes.has(contentType) || contentType !== expectedType) {
    await response.body?.cancel();
    throw new Error(`Signed public video URL returned invalid MIME type: ${contentType || "missing"}.`);
  }
  const declaredLength = Number(response.headers.get("content-length") ?? "0");
  if (declaredLength > config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes) {
    await response.body?.cancel();
    throw new Error("Signed public video URL exceeds SCHEDULE_VIDEO_MAX_BYTES.");
  }
  const bytes = await readBounded(response, config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes);
  if (!bytes.byteLength || bytes.byteLength > config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes) {
    throw new Error("Signed public video URL returned empty or oversized data.");
  }
  if (!matchesSignature(bytes, contentType)) throw new Error(`Signed public video URL did not contain valid ${contentType} data.`);
}

function retryableValidationError(message: string): boolean {
  return /timed out|not reachable|fetch failed|connection|HTTP (408|425|429|5\d\d)/i.test(message);
}

export async function validateStoredImage(
  objectPath: string,
  checkPublic: boolean,
): Promise<ScheduledImageValidation> {
  const checkedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  try {
    const image = await downloadScheduledImage(objectPath);
    const local = { valid: true, contentType: image.contentType, bytes: image.bytes.byteLength };
    if (!checkPublic) return { valid: true, checkedAt, local, retryable: false };
    const imageUrl = await createScheduledImageUrl(objectPath);
    try {
      await validatePublicImageUrl(imageUrl, image.contentType);
      return {
        valid: true,
        checkedAt,
        local,
        public: { valid: true, contentType: image.contentType, bytes: image.bytes.byteLength },
        retryable: false,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Signed public image URL validation failed.";
      const retryable = retryableValidationError(message);
      const publicResult = { valid: false, error: message, retryable };
      return { valid: false, checkedAt, local, public: publicResult, error: message, retryable };
    }
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Stored image validation failed.";
    const retryable = retryableValidationError(message);
    const local = { valid: false, error: message, retryable };
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, local, error: message, retryable };
  }
}

export async function validateStoredVideo(
  objectPath: string,
  checkPublic: boolean,
): Promise<ScheduledImageValidation> {
  const checkedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  try {
    const video = await downloadScheduledVideo(objectPath);
    const local = { valid: true, contentType: video.contentType, bytes: video.bytes.byteLength };
    if (!checkPublic) return { valid: true, checkedAt, local, retryable: false };
    const videoUrl = await createScheduledImageUrl(objectPath);
    try {
      await validatePublicVideoUrl(videoUrl, video.contentType);
      return {
        valid: true,
        checkedAt,
        local,
        public: { valid: true, contentType: video.contentType, bytes: video.bytes.byteLength },
        retryable: false,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Signed public video URL validation failed.";
      const retryable = retryableValidationError(message);
      const publicResult = { valid: false, error: message, retryable };
      return { valid: false, checkedAt, local, public: publicResult, error: message, retryable };
    }
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Stored video validation failed.";
    const retryable = retryableValidationError(message);
    const local = { valid: false, error: message, retryable };
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, local, error: message, retryable };
  }
}

export async function validateStoredDocument(objectPath: string): Promise<StoredDocumentValidation> {
  const checkedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  try {
    const document = await downloadScheduledDocument(objectPath);
    return {
      valid: true,
      checkedAt,
      contentType: document.contentType,
      bytes: document.bytes.byteLength,
      retryable: false,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Stored document validation failed.";
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, error: message, retryable: retryableValidationError(message) };
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/storage/upload-intents.ts
================================================================================

import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import path from "node:path";
import type { QueryResultRow } from "pg";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";
import { databasePool } from "../database.js";
import { deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects, type StorageObjectCandidate } from "../scheduler.js";

// Single-use media upload intents for MCP clients.
//
// The published MCP spec has no client→server file-upload primitive, so remote
// tools never receive attachment bytes. A tool therefore mints a short-lived,
// single-use intent bound to the authenticated better-auth user and returns an
// HTTPS upload endpoint plus a bearer upload token (shown once, hashed at
// rest). The cloud worker's POST /media/upload claims the intent atomically,
// validates the file server-side, stores it in the private Supabase bucket
// under the owner's per-user prefix, and returns the permanent object path.
//
// Cross-runtime note: the web route /api/media/upload keeps its own identical
// MIME/extension map because Vercel and Render deploy independently; both maps
// cover exactly the types accepted by schedulePosts/scheduleMediaPosts.

export type MediaUploadKind = "image" | "video" | "document";

export const mediaUploadExtensionsByContentType: Record<string, string> = {
  "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
  "image/png": ".png",
  "image/gif": ".gif",
  "video/mp4": ".mp4",
  "video/quicktime": ".mov",
  "application/pdf": ".pdf",
};

const contentTypesByKind: Record<MediaUploadKind, ReadonlySet<string>> = {
  image: new Set(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/gif"]),
  video: new Set(["video/mp4", "video/quicktime"]),
  document: new Set(["application/pdf"]),
};

const kindLabels: Record<MediaUploadKind, string> = {
  image: "image",
  video: "video",
  document: "PDF document",
};

export function isMediaUploadKind(value: string): value is MediaUploadKind {
  return value === "image" || value === "video" || value === "document";
}

export function maxBytesForKind(kind: MediaUploadKind): number {
  return kind === "video"
    ? config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes
    : kind === "document"
      ? config.scheduleDocumentMaxBytes
      : config.scheduleImageMaxBytes;
}

export function extensionForContentType(contentType: string): string | undefined {
  return mediaUploadExtensionsByContentType[contentType];
}

// Filename inference accepts every legal variant (.jpg and .jpeg map to the
// same content type), independent of the canonical object-key extension above.
const contentTypesByExtension: Record<string, string> = {
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".gif": "image/gif",
  ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".mov": "video/quicktime",
  ".pdf": "application/pdf",
};

export function contentTypeForFilename(filename: string): string | undefined {
  const extension = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
  return extension ? contentTypesByExtension[extension] : undefined;
}

// Display-only: the object path always comes from scheduledObjectPath(), which
// is random hex under the owner prefix, so the filename can never influence a
// storage key and path traversal is structurally impossible. Still strip
// separators and control characters before persisting the label.
export function sanitizeUploadFilename(filename: string): string {
  const cleaned = filename
    .split(/[\\/]+/)
    .pop()!
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, "")
    .trim();
  return cleaned.slice(0, 200);
}

export function hashUploadToken(token: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(token, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function newUploadToken(): string {
  return randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
}

// Throws unless the declared content type is supported for the requested kind.
export function assertSupportedMediaUpload(kind: MediaUploadKind, contentType: string): void {
  if (!extensionForContentType(contentType)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unsupported upload content type: ${contentType || "missing"}. Only JPEG, PNG, or GIF images, MP4 or MOV videos, and PDF documents are supported.`,
    );
  }
  if (!contentTypesByKind[kind].has(contentType)) {
    throw new Error(`Content type ${contentType} is not valid for a ${kindLabels[kind]} upload.`);
  }
}

// A caller-declared size is bounded immediately so an obviously oversized
// intent never ships an upload URL. The actual byte size is re-checked
// server-side (and against the kind limit) at upload time.
export function assertAllowedDeclaredSize(kind: MediaUploadKind, declaredBytes: number): void {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(declaredBytes) || declaredBytes < 1) {
    throw new Error("Declared file size must be a positive integer.");
  }
  if (declaredBytes > maxBytesForKind(kind)) {
    throw new Error(`Declared file exceeds the ${maxBytesForKind(kind)}-byte limit for ${kindLabels[kind]} uploads.`);
  }
}

export interface MediaUploadCheckInput {
  kind: MediaUploadKind;
  contentType: string;
  expectedBytes: number;
}

// Pure request validation for the upload endpoint, split out so the wire-level
// handler stays thin and the checks stay unit-testable without a database.
// The caller has already split the header's MIME parameters.
export function evaluateMediaUpload(
  intent: MediaUploadCheckInput,
  contentType: string,
  bytes: Uint8Array,
): { status: number; message: string } | undefined {
  if (!extensionForContentType(contentType)) {
    return { status: 415, message: `Unsupported upload content type: ${contentType || "missing"}.` };
  }
  if (contentType !== intent.contentType) {
    return {
      status: 415,
      message: `Upload declares ${contentType} but the intent was created for ${intent.contentType}. The upload intent is single-use; request a new one.`,
    };
  }
  if (bytes.byteLength === 0) {
    return { status: 400, message: "The upload body is empty." };
  }
  if (bytes.byteLength > maxBytesForKind(intent.kind)) {
    return {
      status: 413,
      message: `The upload exceeds the ${maxBytesForKind(intent.kind)}-byte limit for ${kindLabels[intent.kind]}s.`,
    };
  }
  return undefined;
}

// HTTPS (or loopback for local development) upload endpoint, derived from the
// same public worker origin the MCP clients already trust.
export function assertUploadEndpointUrl(url: string): string {
  let parsed: URL;
  try {
    parsed = new URL(url);
  } catch {
    throw new Error("The media upload endpoint URL must be a valid absolute URL.");
  }
  const loopback = parsed.hostname === "localhost" || parsed.hostname === "127.0.0.1" || parsed.hostname === "[::1]";
  if (parsed.protocol !== "https:" && !loopback) {
    throw new Error("The media upload endpoint must use HTTPS outside localhost.");
  }
  if (parsed.username || parsed.password || parsed.hash) {
    throw new Error("The media upload endpoint must not contain credentials or a fragment.");
  }
  return url;
}

export function mediaUploadEndpointUrl(): string {
  const origin = new URL(config.mcpPublicUrl).origin;
  return assertUploadEndpointUrl(new URL("/media/upload", origin).toString());
}

// Browser fallback for clients that cannot upload bytes (returns undefined in
// local setups without a deployed HTTPS frontend).
export function fallbackMediaUploadPageUrl(): string | undefined {
  if (!config.oauthFrontendBaseUrl) return undefined;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(config.oauthFrontendBaseUrl);
    if (parsed.protocol !== "https:") return undefined;
    return new URL("/media", parsed.origin).toString();
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

// --- Rate limiting -----------------------------------------------------------
//
// Sliding-window limiter keyed by authenticated user, per cloud-worker process.
// Intent creation is the only mutation this protects; the upload endpoint is
// already single-use and capped by token.
//
// Two layers guard intent creation: this in-process window (fast, always
// available) and a durable per-process-safe database window below, so the
// configured limit survives scale-to-zero restarts and multiple instances.

export interface SlidingWindowRateLimiter {
  allow(key: string, nowMs?: number): boolean;
}

export function createSlidingWindowRateLimiter(options: {
  maxRequests: number;
  windowMs: number;
}): SlidingWindowRateLimiter {
  const { maxRequests, windowMs } = options;
  const hits = new Map<string, number[]>();
  return {
    allow(key: string, nowMs: number = Date.now()): boolean {
      const window = (hits.get(key) ?? []).filter((timestamp) => timestamp > nowMs - windowMs);
      if (window.length >= maxRequests) {
        hits.set(key, window);
        return false;
      }
      window.push(nowMs);
      hits.set(key, window);
      return true;
    },
  };
}

// Penalty box for failed upload attempts (bad tokens, rejected bytes). Unlike
// the limiter above, per-key hits are recorded only on failures and blocking
// consults the recorded history without adding a hit, so a client cannot lift
// its own block by retrying. The endpoint answers 429 while blocked.
export interface SlidingWindowPenaltyBox {
  record(key: string, nowMs?: number): void;
  blocked(key: string, nowMs?: number): boolean;
}

export function createSlidingWindowPenaltyBox(options: {
  maxAttempts: number;
  windowMs: number;
}): SlidingWindowPenaltyBox {
  const { maxAttempts, windowMs } = options;
  const hits = new Map<string, number[]>();
  return {
    record(key: string, nowMs: number = Date.now()): void {
      const window = (hits.get(key) ?? []).filter((timestamp) => timestamp > nowMs - windowMs);
      window.push(nowMs);
      hits.set(key, window);
    },
    blocked(key: string, nowMs: number = Date.now()): boolean {
      const window = (hits.get(key) ?? []).filter((timestamp) => timestamp > nowMs - windowMs);
      hits.set(key, window);
      return window.length >= maxAttempts;
    },
  };
}

// The rolling window shared by the in-process limiter and the database check.
export const INTENT_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS = 600;

const intentRateLimiter = createSlidingWindowRateLimiter({
  maxRequests: config.mediaUploadIntentRateLimit,
  windowMs: INTENT_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000,
});

export class UploadIntentError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;

  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "UploadIntentError";
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export interface MediaUploadIntentRow extends QueryResultRow {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  client_id: string | null;
  token_hash: string;
  original_filename: string;
  content_type: string;
  expected_bytes: number;
  kind: string;
  status: string;
  storage_path: string | null;
  uploaded_bytes: number | null;
  created_at: Date;
  expires_at: Date;
  consumed_at: Date | null;
  uploaded_at: Date | null;
}

export interface CreatedMediaUploadIntent {
  intentId: string;
  kind: MediaUploadKind;
  contentType: string;
  filename: string;
  expectedBytes: number;
  maxBytes: number;
  token: string;
  expiresAt: string;
}

export interface CreateMediaUploadIntentInput {
  userId: string;
  kind: MediaUploadKind;
  contentType: string;
  filename?: string;
  expectedBytes?: number;
  clientId?: string;
}

function requireCloudStorageInProcess(): void {
  if (config.scheduledImageStorage !== "supabase" || !config.supabaseUrl || !config.supabaseServiceRoleKey) {
    throw new Error(
      "Media upload intents require the cloud storage backend: set SCHEDULED_IMAGE_STORAGE=supabase, SUPABASE_URL, and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY.",
    );
  }
  if (!config.databaseUrl) {
    throw new Error("Media upload intents require DATABASE_URL to store the single-use intent record.");
  }
}

// Durable intent-creation rate check over the migration-012 hit history. The
// statement counts this user's hits inside the rolling window and inserts a
// new hit only when the configured limit allows one, in the same statement:
// concurrent workers each atomically claim a slot (or find the window full).
// Old hits outside the window are pruned in the same statement so the table
// stays small. Returns false when the window is already at its limit.
export async function recordMediaIntentRateHit(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const { rows } = await databasePool().query<{ inserted_id: string | null }>(
    `WITH existing AS (
       SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS hits
       FROM public.mcp_media_intent_rate_hits
       WHERE user_id = $1 AND hit_at > NOW() - make_interval(secs => $2)
     ), pruned AS (
       DELETE FROM public.mcp_media_intent_rate_hits
       WHERE user_id = $1 AND hit_at <= NOW() - make_interval(secs => $2)
       RETURNING 1
     ), inserted AS (
       INSERT INTO public.mcp_media_intent_rate_hits (id, user_id, hit_at)
       SELECT $3, $1, NOW()
       WHERE (SELECT hits FROM existing) < $4::int
       RETURNING id
     )
     SELECT (SELECT id FROM inserted) AS inserted_id`,
    [userId, INTENT_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS, randomBytes(12).toString("hex"), config.mediaUploadIntentRateLimit],
  );
  return rows[0]?.inserted_id != null;
}

// Best-effort sweep of dead rows and their orphaned storage objects whenever a
// new intent is created. A swept intent's stored object may already be
// scheduled by the user's posts, so every deletion goes through the shared
// reference-counted helper: objects referenced by an active scheduled or
// publishing post are always kept. Completed rows keep only audit metadata
// (the storagePath reference never depends on this table), so sweeping cannot
// affect a scheduled post.
async function sweepStaleIntents(ownerId: string): Promise<void> {
  const stale = await databasePool().query<MediaUploadIntentRow>(
    `SELECT id, storage_path, kind
     FROM public.mcp_media_upload_intents
     WHERE user_id = $1
       AND storage_path IS NOT NULL
       AND (
         expires_at < NOW()
         OR (status = 'uploaded' AND created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days')
       )`,
    [ownerId],
  );
  const candidates: StorageObjectCandidate[] = stale.rows
    .map((row) => ({ kind: row.kind as StorageObjectCandidate["kind"], path: row.storage_path }))
    .filter((candidate): candidate is StorageObjectCandidate => Boolean(candidate.path));
  const cleanup = await deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects(candidates);
  // Rows whose object could not be deleted keep their record so a later sweep
  // can retry; deleted (or referenced-but-unreachable) audit rows are swept.
  const retryPaths = stale.rows
    .map((row) => ({ id: row.id, path: row.storage_path }))
    .filter((row): row is { id: string; path: string } => Boolean(row.path));
  const deletableIds = retryPaths
    .filter((row) => cleanup.deleted.has(row.path))
    .map((row) => row.id);
  await databasePool().query(
    `DELETE FROM public.mcp_media_upload_intents
     WHERE user_id = $1
       AND (
         expires_at < NOW()
         OR (status = 'uploaded' AND created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days')
       )
       AND (storage_path IS NULL OR id = ANY($2::text[]))`,
    [ownerId, deletableIds],
  );
}

export async function createMediaUploadIntent(
  input: CreateMediaUploadIntentInput,
): Promise<CreatedMediaUploadIntent> {
  const userId = input.userId.trim();
  if (!userId) throw new Error("An authenticated user is required to create an upload intent.");
  const contentType = input.contentType.trim().toLowerCase();
  assertSupportedMediaUpload(input.kind, contentType);
  if (input.expectedBytes !== undefined) assertAllowedDeclaredSize(input.kind, input.expectedBytes);
  requireCloudStorageInProcess();
  if (!intentRateLimiter.allow(userId)) {
    throw new Error(
      "Too many upload intents requested. Wait a few minutes and request a single new upload intent.",
    );
  }
  // The in-process window is the always-available guard; this durable check
  // makes the same limit hold across restarts and worker instances. A database
  // error here fails open (the in-process window still applies) instead of
  // blocking uploads while the database recovers.
  try {
    if (!(await recordMediaIntentRateHit(userId))) {
      throw new Error(
        "Too many upload intents requested. Wait a few minutes and request a single new upload intent.",
      );
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message.startsWith("Too many upload intents requested")) {
      throw error;
    }
    console.error(JSON.stringify({
      event: "media_upload_intent_rate_limit_database_unavailable",
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown rate-limit database error.",
    }));
  }
  const token = newUploadToken();
  const id = randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
  const { rows } = await databasePool().query<MediaUploadIntentRow>(
    `INSERT INTO public.mcp_media_upload_intents
       (id, user_id, client_id, token_hash, original_filename, content_type, expected_bytes, kind, status, expires_at)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, 'pending', NOW() + make_interval(secs => $9))
     RETURNING id, expires_at`,
    [
      id,
      userId,
      input.clientId?.trim() || null,
      hashUploadToken(token),
      input.filename ? sanitizeUploadFilename(input.filename) : "",
      contentType,
      input.expectedBytes ?? 0,
      input.kind,
      config.mediaUploadIntentTtlSeconds,
    ],
  );
  const row = rows[0];
  if (!row) throw new Error("The upload intent could not be stored. Try again.");
  try {
    await sweepStaleIntents(userId);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(JSON.stringify({
      event: "media_upload_intent_sweep_failed",
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown sweep error.",
    }));
  }
  return {
    intentId: row.id,
    kind: input.kind,
    contentType,
    filename: input.filename ? sanitizeUploadFilename(input.filename) : "",
    expectedBytes: input.expectedBytes ?? 0,
    maxBytes: maxBytesForKind(input.kind),
    token,
    expiresAt: row.expires_at.toISOString(),
  };
}

async function findMediaUploadIntentRow(token: string): Promise<MediaUploadIntentRow | undefined> {
  const { rows } = await databasePool().query<MediaUploadIntentRow>(
    "SELECT * FROM public.mcp_media_upload_intents WHERE token_hash = $1",
    [hashUploadToken(token)],
  );
  return rows[0];
}

// Atomic single-use claim. The UPDATE only matches pending, unconsumed,
// unexpired intents, so two concurrent uploads of the same token can never both
// claim it (and replay is structurally impossible: the claim flips the row, and
// re-verifying a spent token lands on the "already used" reply). Failed
// attempts burn the intent — the client must request a new one, which keeps
// "one token, at most one accepted upload" true without a second state machine.
export async function claimPendingMediaUploadIntent(token: string): Promise<MediaUploadIntentRow> {
  const { rows } = await databasePool().query<MediaUploadIntentRow>(
    `UPDATE public.mcp_media_upload_intents
     SET consumed_at = NOW()
     WHERE token_hash = $1
       AND status = 'pending'
       AND consumed_at IS NULL
       AND expires_at > NOW()
     RETURNING id, user_id, client_id, original_filename, content_type, expected_bytes, kind, status`,
    [hashUploadToken(token)],
  );
  const claimed = rows[0];
  if (claimed) return claimed;
  const existing = await findMediaUploadIntentRow(token);
  if (!existing) {
    throw new UploadIntentError(404, "Unknown upload token. Request a new upload intent.");
  }
  if (existing.status === "uploaded") {
    throw new UploadIntentError(409, "This upload intent was already used. Request a new upload intent.");
  }
  if (existing.status === "failed" || existing.consumed_at) {
    throw new UploadIntentError(409, "This upload intent has already been consumed. Request a new one.");
  }
  throw new UploadIntentError(410, "This upload intent has expired. Request a new one.");
}

// Atomic finalize. Returns false when the row was not in its claimed state
// (which must not happen after a claim; the endpoint treats it as uncertainty).
export async function completeMediaUploadIntent(
  intentId: string,
  storagePath: string,
  uploadedBytes: number,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const { rowCount } = await databasePool().query(
    `UPDATE public.mcp_media_upload_intents
     SET status = 'uploaded', storage_path = $2, uploaded_bytes = $3, uploaded_at = NOW()
     WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'pending' AND consumed_at IS NOT NULL`,
    [intentId, storagePath, uploadedBytes],
  );
  return rowCount === 1;
}

// Best-effort terminal state after a failed upload. Never throws: the endpoint
// already has a real error to report.
export async function failMediaUploadIntent(intentId: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await databasePool().query(
      `UPDATE public.mcp_media_upload_intents
       SET status = 'failed'
       WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'pending'`,
      [intentId],
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(JSON.stringify({
      event: "media_upload_intent_failure_record_failed",
      intentId,
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown failure-record error.",
    }));
  }
}

// Composes the tool-visible instruction payload. The raw token appears here
// exactly once; it is single-use, upload-only, and short-lived (hashed at rest).
export function buildUploadInstructions(intent: CreatedMediaUploadIntent): {
  intentId: string;
  kind: MediaUploadKind;
  contentType: string;
  filename: string;
  declaredSizeBytes: number;
  maxBytes: number;
  upload: {
    method: "POST";
    endpoint: string;
    headers: Record<string, string>;
    body: string;
  };
  uploadToken: string;
  command: string;
  steps: string[];
  fallbackUrl?: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  expiresInMinutes: number;
  note: string;
} {
  const endpoint = mediaUploadEndpointUrl();
  const minutes = Math.max(1, Math.round(config.mediaUploadIntentTtlSeconds / 60));
  const command = [
    `curl -X POST --data-binary "@/path/to/${intent.filename || "file"}" \\`,
    `  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${intent.token}" \\`,
    `  -H "Content-Type: ${intent.contentType}" \\`,
    `  "${endpoint}"`,
  ].join("\n");
  return {
    intentId: intent.intentId,
    kind: intent.kind,
    contentType: intent.contentType,
    filename: intent.filename,
    declaredSizeBytes: intent.expectedBytes,
    maxBytes: intent.maxBytes,
    upload: {
      method: "POST",
      endpoint,
      headers: {
        authorization: `Bearer ${intent.token}`,
        "content-type": intent.contentType,
      },
      body: "The raw file bytes (no multipart wrapper).",
    },
    uploadToken: intent.token,
    command,
    steps: [
      "Run the curl command from an environment that can read the file's bytes. In Claude.ai code execution the chat attachment is available on a local path in the sandbox (for example /mnt/user-data/uploads); in Claude Code the file is on disk. If the sandbox cannot reach the endpoint, use the fallback upload instead.",
      "The response JSON contains a permanent storagePath — pass it as imageStoragePath in social_publish, or as media[].storagePath / documentStoragePath in social_schedule_media or social_schedule_posts. Do not paste any signed URL.",
    ],
    ...(fallbackMediaUploadPageUrl() ? { fallbackUrl: fallbackMediaUploadPageUrl() as string } : {}),
    expiresAt: intent.expiresAt,
    expiresInMinutes: minutes,
    note: "The upload token is single-use, expires at the time shown, and is only valid for one upload of this kind. It is not an MCP access token. If the upload fails or you skip it, request a new intent. Keep the token out of URLs and logs; it is shown here because the sandbox itself must perform the upload.",
  };
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/tools/media-input.ts
================================================================================

// Remote MCP requests are served by the Render worker, which must never read
// files chosen by a remote caller. Local stdio callers keep full path support.
//
// Keep this guard in one place and call it before any filesystem or network
// operation so a remote request can never reach `readFile(imagePath)`,
// `readFile(media[].path)`, or `readFile(documentPath)`.

export interface LocalMediaFields {
  imagePath?: string | undefined;
  documentPath?: string | undefined;
  mediaPaths?: ReadonlyArray<string | undefined> | undefined;
}

export const REMOTE_MEDIA_HINT =
  "Remote MCP clients cannot use local filesystem paths. Provide a public HTTPS "
  + "URL (imageUrl, media[].url, or documentUrl). To share a local image or PDF, "
  + "upload it through the web app first and pass the returned HTTPS URL.";

/**
 * Throws when a remote MCP caller supplies a local filesystem path.
 *
 * @param remote true when serving the remote Streamable HTTP endpoint
 * @param fields local-path fields present in the tool arguments
 */
export function rejectRemoteLocalPaths(remote: boolean, fields: LocalMediaFields): void {
  if (!remote) return;
  const rejected: string[] = [];
  if (fields.imagePath) rejected.push("imagePath");
  if (fields.documentPath) rejected.push("documentPath");
  if (fields.mediaPaths?.some((value) => Boolean(value))) rejected.push("media[].path");
  if (rejected.length) {
    throw new Error(
      `Local filesystem paths are not accepted over remote MCP (${rejected.join(", ")}). ${REMOTE_MEDIA_HINT}`,
    );
  }
}

import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/server";

  scheduledPostStatus,

  type NewScheduledImage,

const imageContentTypes = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/gif"] as const;
const videoContentTypes = ["video/mp4", "video/quicktime"] as const;
const documentContentTypes = ["application/pdf"] as const;

function imageContentTypeFor(extension: string): string {
  if (extension === ".png") return "image/png";
  if (extension === ".jpg" || extension === ".jpeg") return "image/jpeg";
  if (extension === ".gif") return "image/gif";
  throw new Error("Scheduled images must be PNG, JPEG, or GIF files.");
}

function videoContentTypeFor(extension: string): string {
  if (extension === ".mp4") return "video/mp4";
  if (extension === ".mov") return "video/quicktime";
  throw new Error("Scheduled videos must be MP4 or MOV files.");
}

function textResult(value: unknown) {
  return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) }] };
}

async function safely(run: () => Promise<unknown>) {
  try {
    return textResult(await run());
  } catch (error) {
    return textResult({ success: false, error: explainError(error) });
  }
}

async function fetchRemoteImage(url: string) {
  return fetchRemoteMedia(url, {
    maxBytes: config.scheduleImageMaxBytes,
    timeoutMs: config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs,
    allowedContentTypes: imageContentTypes,
  });
}

async function fetchRemoteVideo(url: string) {
  return fetchRemoteMedia(url, {
    maxBytes: config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes,
    timeoutMs: config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs,
    allowedContentTypes: videoContentTypes,
  });
}

async function fetchRemoteDocument(url: string) {
  return fetchRemoteMedia(url, {
    maxBytes: config.scheduleDocumentMaxBytes,
    timeoutMs: config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs,
    allowedContentTypes: documentContentTypes,
  });
}

async function remoteDocument(url: string, title?: string): Promise<NewScheduledDocument> {
  const document = await fetchRemoteDocument(url);
  return {
    bytes: document.bytes,
    contentType: document.contentType,
    ...(title ? { title } : {}),
  };
}

export interface RegisterToolsOptions {
  // The authenticated better-auth user for remote MCP requests. The stdio
  // server omits this and keeps using MCP_OWNER_USER_ID.
  userId?: string;
  // Remote mode never starts a local callback listener or reads local media
  // paths; callers use public HTTPS URLs instead.
  remote?: boolean;
}

export function registerTools(server: McpServer, options: RegisterToolsOptions = {}): void {
  const userId = options.userId ?? config.ownerUserId;
  const remote = options.remote === true;

  server.registerTool("social_authorization_start", {
    description: remote
      ? "Start provider OAuth. Open the returned URL in a browser, approve or cancel, then use social_authorization_status or social_authorization_wait with the returned authorizationSessionId. The cloud worker completes the callback; you do not need to keep this MCP server process running."
      : "Start provider OAuth. Open the returned URL in a browser, approve or cancel, then use social_authorization_wait with the returned authorizationSessionId. Keep this MCP server process running until it completes.",
    inputSchema: z.object({ platform: z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"]) }),
  }, async ({ platform }) => safely(async () => {
    const authorization = await beginAuthorization(platform, userId);
    return {
      platform,
      authorizationUrl: authorization.authorizationUrl,
      authorizationSessionId: authorization.sessionId,
      callback: `${config.redirectBaseUrl}/oauth/${platform === "linkedin" ? "linkedin" : "meta"}/callback`,
      expiresAt: new Date(authorization.expiresAt).toISOString(),
      expiresInMinutes: 15,
      note: remote
        ? "Open the URL in a browser and approve or cancel. The cloud worker completes the callback; use social_authorization_status or social_authorization_wait to check progress. The URL contains temporary state; do not share it."
        : "Keep this MCP server process running through browser approval and callback. The URL contains temporary state; do not share it.",
    };
  }));

  server.registerTool("social_authorization_status", {
    description: "Check whether an OAuth authorization session is pending, connected, cancelled, failed, or expired.",
    inputSchema: z.object({ authorizationSessionId: z.string().min(1) }),
  }, async ({ authorizationSessionId }) => safely(async () => getAuthorizationStatus(authorizationSessionId, userId)));

  server.registerTool("social_authorization_wait", {
    description: remote
      ? "Wait briefly for the OAuth callback. Use short waits and call again if it is still pending; the cloud worker completes the callback independently."
      : "Wait briefly for the OAuth callback on this same MCP server process. Use short waits and call again if it is still pending; long tool calls can time out in MCP clients.",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      authorizationSessionId: z.string().min(1),
      timeoutSeconds: z.number().int().min(0).max(20).default(20),
    }),
  }, async ({ authorizationSessionId, timeoutSeconds }) => safely(async () =>
    waitForAuthorization(authorizationSessionId, timeoutSeconds, userId)));

  server.registerTool("social_connection_status", {
    description: "Verify provider authorization and API access without publishing.",
    inputSchema: z.object({ platform: z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"]).optional() }),
  }, async ({ platform }) => safely(async () => {
    const platforms: Platform[] = platform ? [platform] : ["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"];
    return Promise.all(platforms.map((item) => item === "linkedin" ? linkedinConnectionStatus(userId) : metaConnectionStatus(userId, item)));
  }));

  server.registerTool("social_account_info", {
    description: "Return authenticated Instagram or LinkedIn account details, or Facebook account and accessible Pages.",
    inputSchema: z.object({ platform: z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"]), authorUrn: z.string().optional() }),
  }, async ({ platform, authorUrn }) => safely(async () => platform === "linkedin"
    ? linkedinAccountInfo(userId, authorUrn)
    : metaAccountInfo(userId, platform)));

  server.registerTool("social_recent_posts", {
    description: "Read recent posts allowed by the connected account and granted scopes.",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      platform: z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"]),
      count: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(10),
      authorUrn: z.string().optional(),
    }),
  }, async ({ platform, count, authorUrn }) => safely(async () => ({
    platform,
    posts: platform === "linkedin" ? await readLinkedInPosts(userId, count, authorUrn) : await readMetaPosts(userId, platform, Math.min(count, 25)),
  })));

  server.registerTool("social_upload_media_intent", {
    description: remote
      ? "Create a single-use, time-limited upload intent (up to 15 minutes) for an image, video, or PDF that will be stored privately in Supabase Storage under this account. Returns an HTTPS upload endpoint, a one-time bearer upload token, and a curl command. Run that command from an environment that can read the file bytes — in Claude.ai with code execution enabled, the chat attachment is available on a local sandbox path; in Claude Code the file is on disk. If you cannot upload bytes yourself, use the fallbackUrl and ask the user to upload the file in a browser, then continue with the storagePath they receive. Then pass the returned storagePath as imageStoragePath (social_publish) or media[].storagePath / documentStoragePath (social_schedule_media / social_schedule_posts)."
      : "Create a single-use upload intent for an image, video, or PDF and upload it with the returned token to obtain a permanent storagePath for scheduling and publishing. Local stdio users can also pass file paths directly; storage paths are mainly useful for cloud runs.",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      kind: z.enum(["image", "video", "document"])
        .describe("Media kind to upload: image (JPEG/PNG/GIF), video (MP4/MOV), or document (PDF, LinkedIn only)."),
      filename: z.string().trim().max(255).optional()
        .describe("Original file name for display only; the stored object key is always random. Used to infer the content type when contentType is omitted."),
      contentType: z.string().trim().max(100).optional()
        .describe("Exact MIME type: image/jpeg, image/png, image/gif, video/mp4, video/quicktime, or application/pdf."),
      sizeBytes: z.number().int().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Known file size in bytes. Checked against the kind limit up front; the actual upload size is re-validated server-side."),
    }),
  }, async ({ kind, filename, contentType, sizeBytes }) => safely(async () => {
    // Ownership authority is the authenticated session user, never a caller
    // supplied id. The intent, its upload token, and the stored object are all
    // bound to this user.
    const resolvedContentType = contentType?.toLowerCase()
      ?? (filename ? contentTypeForFilename(filename) : undefined);
    if (!resolvedContentType) {
      throw new Error("Provide contentType or a filename whose extension identifies the media type.");
    }
    const intent = await createMediaUploadIntent({
      userId,
      kind,
      contentType: resolvedContentType,
      ...(filename !== undefined ? { filename } : {}),
      ...(sizeBytes !== undefined ? { expectedBytes: sizeBytes } : {}),
    });
    return buildUploadInstructions(intent);
  }));

  server.registerTool("social_publish", {
    description: remote
      ? "Validate or publish to selected platforms. Dry-run is the default; real posts require dryRun=false and confirmPublish=true. Remote MCP clients provide a public HTTPS imageUrl or a permanent imageStoragePath from social_upload_media_intent or POST /api/media/upload (the server resolves or freshly signs it at publish time; signed URLs are never stored). Local imagePath is not accepted in remote mode."
      : "Validate or publish to selected platforms. Dry-run is the default; real posts require dryRun=false and confirmPublish=true. Meta accepts a public HTTPS imageUrl or a permanent imageStoragePath; LinkedIn accepts imageUrl (downloaded server-side), imageStoragePath, or a local imagePath in stdio mode.",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      platforms: z.array(z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"])).min(1).max(3),
      text: z.string().max(3000).optional(),
      imageUrl: z.string().optional()
        .describe("Public HTTPS image URL."),
      imageStoragePath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Permanent private-storage object path returned by social_upload_media_intent or POST /api/media/upload. Validated to belong to the authenticated user; Meta gets a freshly signed URL only for the publish call, LinkedIn gets the re-downloaded bytes. Preferred over a public URL for remote clients."),
      imagePath: z.string().optional()
        .describe("Local image path. Available in stdio mode only; remote MCP clients are rejected."),
      pageId: z.string().optional(),
      instagramAccountId: z.string().optional(),
      authorUrn: z.string().optional(),
      dryRun: z.boolean().default(true),
      confirmPublish: z.boolean().default(false),
    }),
  }, async (input) => safely(async () => {
    // Reject remote local paths before validation, fetching, or any filesystem read.
    rejectRemoteLocalPaths(remote, { imagePath: input.imagePath });
    const platforms = [...new Set(input.platforms)];
    const contentIssues = validatePostContent(input.text, input.imageUrl, input.imagePath, input.imageStoragePath);
    // A caller-supplied storage path is only ever trusted after this ownership
    // check: it must live under this user's private-storage prefix.
    if (input.imageStoragePath) {
      requireCloudStorageForReference();
      assertOwnedStorageObjectPath(input.imageStoragePath, userId);
    }
    const results = await Promise.all(platforms.map(async (platform) => {
      const issues = [
        ...contentIssues,
        ...platformImageRequirements(platform, input.imageUrl, input.imagePath, input.imageStoragePath),
        ...(platform === "linkedin" && input.authorUrn
          ? [validateLinkedInAuthorUrn(input.authorUrn)].filter((issue): issue is string => Boolean(issue))
          : []),
      ];
      if (issues.length) return { platform, success: false, published: false, issues };
      if (input.dryRun) return {
        platform,
        success: true,
        published: false,
        dryRun: true,
        note: "Input passed local validation. No provider publish request was made.",
      };
      if (!input.confirmPublish) return {
        platform,
        success: false,
        published: false,
        blocked: true,
        reason: "Set confirmPublish=true in the same call to authorize a real post.",
      };
      try {
        let post;
        if (platform === "linkedin") {
          if (input.imagePath) {
            post = await publishLinkedIn(userId, input.text, input.imagePath, input.authorUrn);
          } else if (input.imageStoragePath) {
            // Direct byte upload from the private bucket; no signed URL exists.
            const image = await downloadScheduledImage(input.imageStoragePath);
            post = await publishLinkedInWithImage(userId, input.text, image.bytes, image.contentType, input.authorUrn);
          } else if (input.imageUrl) {
            const image = await fetchRemoteImage(input.imageUrl);
            post = await publishLinkedInWithImage(userId, input.text, image.bytes, image.contentType, input.authorUrn);
          } else {
            post = await publishLinkedIn(userId, input.text, undefined, input.authorUrn);
          }
        } else {
          // Meta requires a fetchable HTTPS URL at publish time. Create a fresh
          // short-lived signed URL only inside this call; it is never persisted.
          const publishImageUrl = input.imageStoragePath
            ? await createScheduledImageUrl(input.imageStoragePath)
            : input.imageUrl;
          post = await publishMeta(userId, platform, input.text, publishImageUrl, input.pageId, input.instagramAccountId);
        }
        return { platform, success: true, published: post.confirmed, post };
      } catch (error) {
        return { platform, success: false, published: false, error: explainError(error) };
      }
    }));
    return { results };
  }));

  server.registerTool("social_schedule_posts", {
    description: remote
      ? "Persistently schedule separate posts to connected social platforms. Provide an image reference (imageStoragePath, the permanent object path returned by POST /api/media/upload, or a public HTTPS imageUrl that is downloaded and re-stored immediately) for image posts, or omit every image reference for a text-only Facebook or LinkedIn post; Instagram always requires an image. The worker downloads stored media with server-side credentials and creates a fresh signed URL only when the platform needs one. For a native LinkedIn document, pass documentStoragePath (durable) or a public HTTPS documentUrl. Local imagePath/documentPath are not accepted in remote mode."
      : "Persistently schedule separate posts to connected social platforms. In local mode the MCP scheduler, image server, and public tunnel must remain running; in cloud mode media is stored privately in Supabase Storage and the separate worker publishes it. Provide a permanent imageStoragePath from POST /api/media/upload, a public HTTPS imageUrl, or a local imagePath (stdio) for image posts, or omit every image reference for a text-only Facebook or LinkedIn post; Instagram always requires an image. An optional PDF documentStoragePath, documentUrl, or documentPath is attached as a native LinkedIn document post (LinkedIn only).",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      posts: z.array(z.object({
        platform: z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"]),
        scheduledAt: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),
        text: z.string().min(1).max(63206),
      })).min(1).max(3),
      imageUrl: z.string().optional()
        .describe("Public HTTPS image URL. Downloaded immediately and stored privately for the scheduled post."),
      imageStoragePath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Permanent private-storage object path returned by POST /api/media/upload. Preferred for scheduled posts: it never expires and the worker re-signs a URL at publish time."),
      imagePath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Local image path. Available in stdio mode only; remote MCP clients are rejected."),
      documentUrl: z.string().optional()
        .describe("Public HTTPS PDF URL for a native LinkedIn document post."),
      documentStoragePath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Permanent private-storage PDF object path returned by POST /api/media/upload."),
      documentPath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Local PDF path for a native LinkedIn document post. Available in stdio mode only; remote MCP clients are rejected."),
      documentTitle: z.string().min(1).max(200).optional()
        .describe("Title shown on the LinkedIn document. Defaults to the PDF file name."),
    }),
  }, async ({ posts, imageUrl, imageStoragePath, imagePath, documentUrl, documentStoragePath, documentPath, documentTitle }) => safely(async () => {
    // Reject remote local paths before any token lookup, fetch, or filesystem read.
    rejectRemoteLocalPaths(remote, { imagePath, documentPath });
    if ([imageUrl, imageStoragePath, imagePath].filter(Boolean).length > 1) {
      throw new Error("Provide at most one of imageUrl, imageStoragePath, or imagePath.");
    }
    if ([documentUrl, documentStoragePath, documentPath].filter(Boolean).length > 1) {
      throw new Error("Provide at most one of documentUrl, documentStoragePath, or documentPath.");
    }
    // Omitting every image reference schedules text-only posts; Instagram is
    // rejected by the scheduler because its API cannot publish text-only feeds.
    let image: string | NewScheduledImage | undefined;
    if (imagePath) {
      image = imagePath;
    } else if (imageStoragePath) {
      image = { storagePath: imageStoragePath };
    } else if (imageUrl) {
      const fetched = await fetchRemoteImage(imageUrl);
      image = { bytes: fetched.bytes, contentType: fetched.contentType, extension: fetched.extension };
    }
    const document = documentUrl
      ? await remoteDocument(documentUrl, documentTitle)
      : documentStoragePath
        ? { storagePath: documentStoragePath, ...(documentTitle ? { title: documentTitle } : {}) }
        : documentPath
          ? { path: documentPath, ...(documentTitle ? { title: documentTitle } : {}) }
          : undefined;
    return {
      scheduled: await schedulePosts(userId, posts, image, document),
    };
  }));

  server.registerTool("social_schedule_media", {
    description: remote
      ? "Schedule image, video, or Instagram carousel posts to connected platforms. The durable media reference is media[].storagePath (the permanent object path returned by POST /api/media/upload); the worker downloads it with server-side credentials and creates a fresh signed URL only when the platform needs one. A public HTTPS url is also accepted and is downloaded and re-stored immediately. For a LinkedIn PDF pass documentStoragePath (durable) or a public HTTPS documentUrl. Local media[].path and documentPath are not accepted in remote mode. Provide one image, one video, two to ten images for an Instagram carousel, or no media for a text-only Facebook/LinkedIn post."
      : "Schedule image, video, or Instagram carousel posts to connected platforms. Provide a permanent media[].storagePath from POST /api/media/upload, a public HTTPS url, or a local path (stdio) for each media item. Provide one image, one video, two to ten images for an Instagram carousel, or no media for a text-only Facebook/LinkedIn post. Cloud mode stores media privately in Supabase Storage; the worker downloads it at publish time.",
    inputSchema: z.object({
      posts: z.array(z.object({
        platform: z.enum(["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"]),
        scheduledAt: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),
        text: z.string().min(1).max(63206),
      })).min(1).max(3),
      media: z.array(z.object({
        path: z.string().min(1).optional()
          .describe("Local media path. Available in stdio mode only; remote MCP clients are rejected."),
        storagePath: z.string().min(1).optional()
          .describe("Permanent private-storage object path returned by POST /api/media/upload. Preferred for scheduled posts: it never expires."),
        url: z.string().optional()
          .describe("Public HTTPS media URL. Downloaded immediately and stored privately for the scheduled post."),
        kind: z.enum(["image", "video"]),
      })).max(10).default([]),
      documentUrl: z.string().optional()
        .describe("Public HTTPS PDF URL for a native LinkedIn document post. Cannot be combined with media."),
      documentStoragePath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Permanent private-storage PDF object path returned by POST /api/media/upload. Cannot be combined with media."),
      documentPath: z.string().min(1).optional()
        .describe("Local PDF path for a native LinkedIn document post. Available in stdio mode only; remote MCP clients are rejected."),
      documentTitle: z.string().min(1).max(200).optional(),
    }),
  }, async ({ posts, media, documentUrl, documentStoragePath, documentPath, documentTitle }) => safely(async () => {
    // Reject remote local paths before any token lookup, fetch, or filesystem read.
    rejectRemoteLocalPaths(remote, { documentPath, mediaPaths: media.map((item) => item.path) });
    if ([documentUrl, documentStoragePath, documentPath].filter(Boolean).length > 1) {
      throw new Error("Provide at most one of documentUrl, documentStoragePath, or documentPath.");
    }
    const resolved: NewScheduledMedia[] = [];
    for (const item of media) {
      if ([item.path, item.storagePath, item.url].filter(Boolean).length !== 1) {
        throw new Error("Provide exactly one of path, storagePath, or url for each media item.");
      }
      if (item.path) {
        const extension = path.extname(item.path).toLowerCase();
        const contentType = item.kind === "video" ? videoContentTypeFor(extension) : imageContentTypeFor(extension);
        resolved.push({ kind: item.kind, bytes: await readFile(item.path), contentType, extension });
      } else if (item.storagePath) {
        const extension = path.extname(item.storagePath).toLowerCase();
        const contentType = item.kind === "video" ? videoContentTypeFor(extension) : imageContentTypeFor(extension);
        resolved.push({ kind: item.kind, storagePath: item.storagePath, contentType, extension });
      } else {
        const fetched = item.kind === "video" ? await fetchRemoteVideo(item.url!) : await fetchRemoteImage(item.url!);
        resolved.push({ kind: item.kind, bytes: fetched.bytes, contentType: fetched.contentType, extension: fetched.extension });
      }
    }
    const document = documentUrl
      ? await remoteDocument(documentUrl, documentTitle)
      : documentStoragePath
        ? { storagePath: documentStoragePath, ...(documentTitle ? { title: documentTitle } : {}) }
        : documentPath
          ? { path: documentPath, ...(documentTitle ? { title: documentTitle } : {}) }
          : undefined;
    return {
      scheduled: await scheduleMediaPosts(userId, posts, resolved, document),
    };
  }));

  server.registerTool("social_scheduled_posts_status", {
    description: "List scheduled posts, image validation results, retry counts, and failure stages without exposing credentials or image URLs.",
    inputSchema: z.object({}),
  }, async () => safely(async () => ({ scheduledPosts: await scheduledPostStatus(userId) })));

  server.registerTool("social_cancel_scheduled_post", {
    description: "Cancel a scheduled post only if publishing has not started.",
    inputSchema: z.object({ jobId: z.string().min(1) }),
  }, async ({ jobId }) => safely(async () => ({ cancelled: await cancelScheduledPost(userId, jobId) })));

  server.registerTool("social_retry_scheduled_post", {
    description: "Safely requeue a failed scheduled post only after pre-publish token or image validation failed. Publishing-stage failures are blocked to prevent duplicates.",
    inputSchema: z.object({ jobId: z.string().min(1) }),
  }, async ({ jobId }) => safely(async () => ({ scheduled: await retryScheduledPost(userId, jobId) })));

  server.registerTool("social_token_store_status", {
    description: "Show stored token presence, expiry, and scopes without exposing secrets.",
    inputSchema: z.object({}),
  }, async () => safely(async () => {
    const tokens = await loadTokens(userId);
    return Object.fromEntries((["instagram", "facebook", "linkedin"] as const).map((platform) => {
      const token = tokens[platform];
      return [platform, token
        ? { connected: true, expiresAt: token.expiresAt, scopes: token.scopes, pages: token.pages?.map(({ id, name }) => ({ id, name })) }
        : { connected: false }];
    }));
  }));
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/utils/http.ts

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, public readonly status: number, public readonly code?: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

type RequestOptions = Omit<RequestInit, "headers"> & {
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  accessToken?: string;
};

function cleanMessage(input: string): string {
  return input
    .replace(/access_token=[^&\s]+/gi, "access_token=[redacted]")
    .replace(/Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~-]+/gi, "Bearer [redacted]")
    .replace(/(client_secret|refresh_token|authorization_code)=([^&\s]+)/gi, "$1=[redacted]")
    .slice(0, 700);
}

export async function apiRequest<T>(input: string | URL, options: RequestOptions = {}) {
  const { accessToken, headers = {}, ...requestOptions } = options;
  const response = await fetch(input, {
    ...requestOptions,
    headers: { ...(accessToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } : {}), ...headers },
  });
  const raw = await response.text();
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
  } catch {
    parsed = { message: raw || response.statusText };
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const body = parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed as Record<string, unknown> : {};
    const errorBody = body["error"];
    const nested = errorBody && typeof errorBody === "object" ? errorBody as Record<string, unknown> : body;
    const message = cleanMessage(String(nested["message"] ?? nested["error_description"] ?? response.statusText));
    const code = nested["code"] ?? nested["type"] ?? nested["error"];
    throw new ApiError(message, response.status, code === undefined ? undefined : String(code));
  }
  return { data: parsed as T, response };
}

export function explainError(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiError) {
    if (error.status === 401) return `Authentication failed or the token has expired: ${error.message}`;
    if (error.status === 403) return `The token is missing a required permission or account role: ${error.message}`;
    if (error.status === 429) return `The platform rate limit was reached: ${error.message}`;
    return `Platform API returned HTTP ${error.status}: ${error.message}`;
  }
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : "An unknown error occurred.";
}

FILE: social-media-mcp/src/utils/remote-media.ts
