import { lookup as dnsLookup } from "node:dns/promises";
import { request as httpsRequest } from "node:https";
import { isIP, type LookupFunction } from "node:net";
import { matchesMediaSignature } from "../storage/scheduled-images.js";

// Fetches user-supplied remote media for the remote MCP endpoint. A public MCP
// client cannot send local file paths to Render, so image/video/PDF inputs may
// be public HTTPS URLs. The fetcher rejects non-HTTPS URLs, credentials,
// loopback/private/reserved addresses, oversized bodies, and re-validates every
// redirect. DNS is resolved once and pinned into the TLS connection so a second
// lookup cannot rebind the host to an internal address.

export interface RemoteMediaLimits {
  maxBytes: number;
  timeoutMs: number;
  allowedContentTypes: readonly string[];
  maxRedirects?: number;
}

export interface RemoteMedia {
  bytes: Buffer;
  contentType: string;
  extension: string;
  finalUrl: string;
}

interface ResolvedTarget {
  url: URL;
  hostname: string;
  address: string;
  family: number;
}

const extensionByContentType: Record<string, string> = {
  "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
  "image/png": ".png",
  "image/gif": ".gif",
  "video/mp4": ".mp4",
  "video/quicktime": ".mov",
  "application/pdf": ".pdf",
};

function ipv4ToNumber(address: string): number {
  const parts = address.split(".").map(Number);
  return (((parts[0]! * 256 + parts[1]!) * 256 + parts[2]!) * 256 + parts[3]!) >>> 0;
}

const ipv4Ranges: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, number]> = [
  ["0.0.0.0", 8],
  ["10.0.0.0", 8],
  ["100.64.0.0", 10],
  ["127.0.0.0", 8],
  ["169.254.0.0", 16],
  ["172.16.0.0", 12],
  ["192.0.0.0", 24],
  ["192.0.2.0", 24],
  ["192.168.0.0", 16],
  ["198.18.0.0", 15],
  ["198.51.100.0", 24],
  ["203.0.113.0", 24],
  ["224.0.0.0", 4],
  ["240.0.0.0", 4],
  ["255.255.255.255", 32],
];

function isPrivateIpv4(address: string): boolean {
  const value = ipv4ToNumber(address);
  return ipv4Ranges.some(([base, bits]) => {
    const mask = bits === 0 ? 0 : (0xffffffff << (32 - bits)) >>> 0;
    return (value & mask) === (ipv4ToNumber(base) & mask);
  });
}

function ipv4FromMappedIpv6(value: string): string | undefined {
  const dotted = /^::ffff:(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)$/.exec(value);
  if (dotted) return dotted[1];
  const hex = /^::ffff:([0-9a-f]{1,4}):([0-9a-f]{1,4})$/.exec(value);
  if (!hex) return undefined;
  const high = Number.parseInt(hex[1]!, 16);
  const low = Number.parseInt(hex[2]!, 16);
  return `${high >> 8}.${high & 0xff}.${low >> 8}.${low & 0xff}`;
}

export function isPrivateIpAddress(address: string): boolean {
  const family = isIP(address);
  if (family === 4) return isPrivateIpv4(address);
  if (family !== 6) return true;
  const value = address.toLowerCase();
  if (value === "::" || value === "::1") return true;
  const mapped = ipv4FromMappedIpv6(value);
  if (mapped) return isPrivateIpv4(mapped);
  const first = Number.parseInt(value.split(":")[0] || "0", 16);
  if ((first & 0xfe00) === 0xfc00) return true; // fc00::/7 unique-local
  if ((first & 0xffc0) === 0xfe80) return true; // fe80::/10 link-local
  if ((first & 0xff00) === 0xff00) return true; // ff00::/8 multicast
  if (value.startsWith("2001:db8:")) return true; // documentation
  if (value.startsWith("2001:10:") || value.startsWith("2001:20:")) return true; // ORCHID
  if (value.startsWith("2002:")) {
    // 6to4 embeds an IPv4 address in the next 32 bits.
    const parts = value.split(":");
    if (parts.length >= 3) {
      const high = Number.parseInt(parts[1]!, 16);
      const low = Number.parseInt(parts[2]!, 16);
      if (Number.isFinite(high) && Number.isFinite(low)) {
        return isPrivateIpv4(`${high >> 8}.${high & 0xff}.${low >> 8}.${low & 0xff}`);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }
  if (value.startsWith("64:ff9b:") || value.startsWith("100:")) return true; // NAT64 / discard
  return false;
}

function hostnameWithoutBrackets(hostname: string): string {
  return hostname.replace(/^\[/, "").replace(/\]$/, "");
}

export async function resolvePublicHttpsUrl(raw: string): Promise<ResolvedTarget> {
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(raw);
  } catch {
    throw new Error("Media URL must be a valid absolute URL.");
  }
  if (url.protocol !== "https:") throw new Error("Media URLs must use HTTPS.");
  if (url.username || url.password) throw new Error("Media URLs must not contain embedded credentials.");
  if (!url.hostname) throw new Error("Media URL must include a hostname.");
  const hostname = hostnameWithoutBrackets(url.hostname).toLowerCase();
  if (hostname === "localhost" || hostname.endsWith(".localhost")) {
    throw new Error("Media URL must be publicly reachable.");
  }
  const literalFamily = isIP(hostname);
  const addresses = literalFamily
    ? [{ address: hostname, family: literalFamily }]
    : await dnsLookup(hostname, { all: true, verbatim: true });
  if (!addresses.length) throw new Error("Media URL hostname did not resolve.");
  for (const entry of addresses) {
    if (isPrivateIpAddress(entry.address)) {
      throw new Error("Media URL must not resolve to a private, loopback, or reserved address.");
    }
  }
  const first = addresses[0]!;
  return {
    url,
    hostname,
    address: first.address,
    family: first.family,
  };
}

interface OneResponse {
  status: number;
  location?: string;
  contentType?: string;
  bytes: Buffer;
}

function requestOnce(target: ResolvedTarget, limits: RemoteMediaLimits): Promise<OneResponse> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const pinnedLookup: LookupFunction = (_hostname, _options, callback) => {
      callback(null, target.address, target.family);
    };
    const request = httpsRequest({
      protocol: "https:",
      hostname: target.hostname,
      port: target.url.port || 443,
      path: `${target.url.pathname}${target.url.search}`,
      method: "GET",
      servername: target.hostname,
      headers: {
        accept: limits.allowedContentTypes.join(", "),
        "user-agent": "social-media-mcp/1.0",
      },
      lookup: pinnedLookup,
      timeout: limits.timeoutMs,
    }, (response) => {
      const status = response.statusCode ?? 0;
      if (status >= 300 && status < 400) {
        response.resume();
        const location = response.headers.location;
        resolve({
          status,
          ...(typeof location === "string" ? { location } : {}),
          bytes: Buffer.alloc(0),
        });
        return;
      }
      if (status < 200 || status >= 300) {
        response.resume();
        resolve({ status, bytes: Buffer.alloc(0) });
        return;
      }
      const contentLength = Number(response.headers["content-length"] ?? "0");
      if (Number.isFinite(contentLength) && contentLength > limits.maxBytes) {
        response.destroy();
        reject(new Error("Remote media exceeds the configured maximum size."));
        return;
      }
      const contentTypeHeader = response.headers["content-type"];
      const contentType = typeof contentTypeHeader === "string"
        ? contentTypeHeader.split(";")[0]!.trim().toLowerCase()
        : "";
      const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
      let total = 0;
      response.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => {
        total += chunk.byteLength;
        if (total > limits.maxBytes) {
          response.destroy(new Error("Remote media exceeds the configured maximum size."));
          return;
        }
        chunks.push(chunk);
      });
      response.on("end", () => {
        resolve({
          status,
          ...(contentType ? { contentType } : {}),
          bytes: Buffer.concat(chunks, total),
        });
      });
      response.on("error", reject);
    });
    request.on("timeout", () => request.destroy(new Error("Remote media request timed out.")));
    request.on("error", reject);
    request.end();
  });
}

export async function fetchRemoteMedia(rawUrl: string, limits: RemoteMediaLimits): Promise<RemoteMedia> {
  let current = rawUrl;
  const maxRedirects = limits.maxRedirects ?? 5;
  for (let redirect = 0; redirect <= maxRedirects; redirect += 1) {
    const target = await resolvePublicHttpsUrl(current);
    const response = await requestOnce(target, limits);
    if (response.status >= 300 && response.status < 400) {
      if (!response.location) throw new Error("Remote media redirect did not include a Location header.");
      current = new URL(response.location, target.url).toString();
      continue;
    }
    if (response.status < 200 || response.status >= 300) {
      throw new Error(`Remote media URL returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
    }
    const contentType = response.contentType ?? "";
    if (!limits.allowedContentTypes.includes(contentType)) {
      throw new Error(`Remote media URL returned unsupported content type: ${contentType || "missing"}.`);
    }
    const extension = extensionByContentType[contentType];
    if (!extension) {
      throw new Error(`Remote media URL returned unsupported content type: ${contentType}.`);
    }
    if (!response.bytes.byteLength) throw new Error("Remote media URL returned empty data.");
    if (!matchesMediaSignature(response.bytes, contentType)) {
      throw new Error(`Remote media URL did not contain valid ${contentType} data.`);
    }
    return {
      bytes: response.bytes,
      contentType,
      extension,
      finalUrl: target.url.toString(),
    };
  }
  throw new Error("Remote media URL redirected too many times.");
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/src/utils/scheduled-image.ts
================================================================================

import { stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import { config } from "../config/env.js";

const allowedMimeTypes = new Set(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/gif"]);
const imageRoute = (token: string) => `/scheduled-media/${token}`;

export interface ImageEndpointValidation {
  valid: boolean;
  status?: number;
  contentType?: string;
  bytes?: number;
  error?: string;
  retryable?: boolean;
}

export interface ScheduledImageValidation {
  valid: boolean;
  checkedAt: string;
  local: ImageEndpointValidation;
  public?: ImageEndpointValidation;
  error?: string;
  retryable: boolean;
}

export interface ScheduledImageValidationOptions {
  imagePath: string;
  imageToken: string;
  contentType: string;
  checkPublic: boolean;
  localBaseUrl?: string;
  publicBaseUrl?: string;
  timeoutMs?: number;
  maxBytes?: number;
  fetcher?: typeof fetch;
}

export class ScheduledImageError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public readonly retryable: boolean,
    public readonly status?: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ScheduledImageError";
  }
}

export class ScheduledImageRetryError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly validation: ScheduledImageValidation,
    public readonly attempts: number,
  ) {
    super(`Scheduled image validation failed after ${attempts} attempt(s): ${validation.error ?? "unknown image validation error"}`);
    this.name = "ScheduledImageRetryError";
  }
}

export interface ScheduledImageAttempt {
  validation: ScheduledImageValidation;
  attempt: number;
  retries: number;
  willRetry: boolean;
  delayMs: number;
}

export async function validateScheduledImageWithRetries(
  options: ScheduledImageValidationOptions,
  maxAttempts: number,
  retryBaseDelayMs: number,
  onAttempt: (attempt: ScheduledImageAttempt) => Promise<void> | void,
  retryMaxDelayMs = 30000,
  sleep: (delayMs: number) => Promise<void> = (delayMs) =>
    new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs)),
): Promise<{ validation: ScheduledImageValidation; retries: number }> {
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt += 1) {
    const validation = await validateScheduledImage(options);
    const willRetry = !validation.valid && validation.retryable && attempt < maxAttempts;
    const retries = attempt - 1;
    const delayMs = Math.min(retryBaseDelayMs * 2 ** (attempt - 1), retryMaxDelayMs);
    await onAttempt({ validation, attempt, retries, willRetry, delayMs });
    if (validation.valid) return { validation, retries };
    if (!willRetry) throw new ScheduledImageRetryError(validation, attempt);
    await sleep(delayMs);
  }
  throw new Error("Scheduled image retry loop ended unexpectedly.");
}

function matchesImageSignature(bytes: Uint8Array, mimeType: string): boolean {
  if (mimeType === "image/jpeg") return bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8 && bytes[2] === 0xff;
  if (mimeType === "image/png") {
    return bytes.length >= 8
      && bytes[0] === 0x89 && bytes[1] === 0x50 && bytes[2] === 0x4e && bytes[3] === 0x47
      && bytes[4] === 0x0d && bytes[5] === 0x0a && bytes[6] === 0x1a && bytes[7] === 0x0a;
  }
  return mimeType === "image/gif"
    && bytes.length >= 6
    && String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(0, 6)).match(/^GIF8[79]a$/) !== null;
}

function networkFailure(error: unknown): ScheduledImageError {
  if (error instanceof ScheduledImageError) return error;
  if (error instanceof Error && (error.name === "TimeoutError" || error.name === "AbortError")) {
    return new ScheduledImageError("Image endpoint request timed out.", true);
  }
  const cause = error instanceof Error ? error.cause : undefined;
  const code = cause && typeof cause === "object" && "code" in cause ? String(cause.code) : "";
  if (code === "ECONNREFUSED") {
    return new ScheduledImageError("Image server connection refused; check IMAGE_SERVER_PORT and ensure the server is running.", true);
  }
  return new ScheduledImageError("Image endpoint request failed; check the local image server and ngrok tunnel.", true);
}

async function readAndValidateResponse(
  response: Response,
  endpoint: string,
  isPublic: boolean,
  expectedMimeType: string,
  maxBytes: number,
): Promise<number> {
  if (response.status !== 200) {
    throw new ScheduledImageError(
      `${endpoint} returned HTTP ${response.status}; check the configured image-server port, route, and ngrok forwarding.`,
      (isPublic && response.status === 404)
        || response.status === 408 || response.status === 425 || response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500,
      response.status,
    );
  }

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0]?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "";
  if (!allowedMimeTypes.has(contentType)) {
    throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} returned an invalid image MIME type (${contentType || "missing"}).`, false, response.status);
  }
  if (contentType !== expectedMimeType) {
    throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} image MIME type does not match the scheduled image file.`, false, response.status);
  }

  const declaredLength = Number(response.headers.get("content-length"));
  if (Number.isFinite(declaredLength) && declaredLength > maxBytes) {
    throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} image exceeds the configured ${maxBytes}-byte size limit.`, false, response.status);
  }

  if (!response.body) throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} returned an empty image response.`, false, response.status);
  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let totalBytes = 0;
  try {
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      totalBytes += value.byteLength;
      if (totalBytes > maxBytes) {
        await reader.cancel();
        throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} image exceeds the configured ${maxBytes}-byte size limit.`, false, response.status);
      }
      chunks.push(value);
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
  if (totalBytes === 0) throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} returned an empty image response.`, false, response.status);

  const signature = new Uint8Array(Math.min(totalBytes, 8));
  let offset = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const copyLength = Math.min(chunk.byteLength, signature.length - offset);
    if (copyLength <= 0) break;
    signature.set(chunk.subarray(0, copyLength), offset);
    offset += copyLength;
  }
  if (!matchesImageSignature(signature, contentType)) {
    throw new ScheduledImageError(`${endpoint} response is not valid ${contentType} image data.`, false, response.status);
  }
  return totalBytes;
}

async function validateEndpoint(
  url: string,
  label: string,
  expectedMimeType: string,
  timeoutMs: number,
  maxBytes: number,
  fetcher: typeof fetch,
): Promise<ImageEndpointValidation> {
  try {
    const response = await fetcher(url, {
      method: "GET",
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
      headers: { accept: "image/jpeg,image/png,image/gif" },
    });
    const bytes = await readAndValidateResponse(
      response,
      label,
      label === "Public ngrok image route",
      expectedMimeType,
      maxBytes,
    );
    const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0]?.trim().toLowerCase();
    return {
      valid: true,
      status: response.status,
      ...(contentType ? { contentType } : {}),
      bytes,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const failure = networkFailure(error);
    return {
      valid: false,
      ...(failure.status !== undefined ? { status: failure.status } : {}),
      error: failure.message,
      retryable: failure.retryable,
    };
  }
}

export async function validateScheduledImage(
  options: ScheduledImageValidationOptions,
): Promise<ScheduledImageValidation> {
  const {
    imagePath,
    imageToken,
    contentType,
    checkPublic,
    localBaseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${config.imageServerPort}`,
    publicBaseUrl = config.imagePublicBaseUrl,
    timeoutMs = config.scheduleImageTimeoutMs,
    maxBytes = config.scheduleImageMaxBytes,
    fetcher = fetch,
  } = options;
  const checkedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(imageToken)) {
    const error = "Scheduled image token is invalid.";
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, local: { valid: false, error, retryable: false }, error, retryable: false };
  }
  if (!allowedMimeTypes.has(contentType)) {
    const error = `Scheduled image MIME type is not allowed (${contentType}).`;
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, local: { valid: false, error, retryable: false }, error, retryable: false };
  }

  try {
    const file = await stat(imagePath);
    if (!file.isFile()) throw new ScheduledImageError("Scheduled image path is not a regular file.", false);
    if (file.size === 0) throw new ScheduledImageError("Scheduled image file is empty.", false);
    if (file.size > maxBytes) throw new ScheduledImageError(`Scheduled image file exceeds the configured ${maxBytes}-byte size limit.`, false);
  } catch (error) {
    const fileErrorCode = error instanceof Error && "code" in error ? error.code : undefined;
    const failure = error instanceof ScheduledImageError
      ? error
      : fileErrorCode === "ENOENT"
        ? new ScheduledImageError("Scheduled image file is missing from local scheduler storage.", false)
        : fileErrorCode === "EACCES" || fileErrorCode === "EPERM" || fileErrorCode === "ENOTDIR"
          ? new ScheduledImageError("Scheduled image file cannot be read from local scheduler storage.", false)
          : networkFailure(error);
    return {
      valid: false,
      checkedAt,
      local: { valid: false, error: failure.message, retryable: failure.retryable },
      error: failure.message,
      retryable: failure.retryable,
    };
  }

  const route = imageRoute(imageToken);
  const local = await validateEndpoint(
    `${localBaseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")}${route}`,
    "Local image route",
    contentType,
    timeoutMs,
    maxBytes,
    fetcher,
  );
  if (!local.valid) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      checkedAt,
      local,
      ...(local.error ? { error: local.error } : {}),
      retryable: local.retryable ?? false,
    };
  }

  if (!checkPublic) return { valid: true, checkedAt, local, retryable: false };

  let publicUrl: URL;
  try {
    publicUrl = new URL(`${publicBaseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")}${route}`);
  } catch {
    const error = "IMAGE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL is not a valid URL.";
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, local, public: { valid: false, error, retryable: false }, error, retryable: false };
  }
  if (publicUrl.protocol !== "https:") {
    const error = "IMAGE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL must use HTTPS for Meta image publishing.";
    return { valid: false, checkedAt, local, public: { valid: false, error, retryable: false }, error, retryable: false };
  }

  const publicResult = await validateEndpoint(
    publicUrl.toString(),
    "Public ngrok image route",
    contentType,
    timeoutMs,
    maxBytes,
    fetcher,
  );
  return {
    valid: publicResult.valid,
    checkedAt,
    local,
    public: publicResult,
    ...(publicResult.valid ? {} : { error: publicResult.error }),
    retryable: publicResult.retryable ?? false,
  };
}

import type { Platform } from "../platforms/types.js";

export function validateImageUrl(value: string): string | undefined {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    if (url.protocol !== "https:") return "Image URLs must use HTTPS.";
    if (url.username || url.password) return "Image URLs must not contain embedded credentials.";
    return undefined;
  } catch {
    return "Image URL must be a valid absolute URL.";
  }
}

export function validateLinkedInAuthorUrn(value: string): string | undefined {
  return /^urn:li:(person|organization):[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(value)
    ? undefined
    : "LinkedIn author must be a person or organization URN.";
}

export function platformImageRequirements(
  platform: Platform,
  imageUrl?: string,
  imagePath?: string,
  imageStoragePath?: string,
): string[] {
  const issues: string[] = [];
  const hasImageReference = Boolean(imageUrl ?? imagePath ?? imageStoragePath);
  if (platform === "instagram" && !hasImageReference) {
    issues.push("Instagram Graph API feed publishing requires an image reference; text-only posts are not supported.");
  }
  if (imageStoragePath && (imageUrl || imagePath)) {
    issues.push("Provide only one image reference: imageUrl, imagePath, or imageStoragePath.");
  }
  if (platform === "linkedin" && imagePath && imageUrl) {
    issues.push("LinkedIn uses either imagePath or imageUrl, not both.");
  }
  if (platform !== "linkedin" && imagePath) {
    issues.push("Meta publishing uses imageUrl or a storage object path; local image uploads are not implemented for this platform.");
  }
  if (imageUrl) {
    const issue = validateImageUrl(imageUrl);
    if (issue) issues.push(issue);
  }
  return issues;
}

export function validatePostContent(
  text?: string,
  imageUrl?: string,
  imagePath?: string,
  imageStoragePath?: string,
): string[] {
  const hasContent = text?.trim() || imageUrl || imagePath || imageStoragePath;
  return hasContent ? [] : ["Provide post text, an image URL, an image path, or an imageStoragePath."];
}

import { randomUUID, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from "node:http";

  getMcpClient,

  mcpAuthorizationServerMetadata,

const mcpUrl = new URL(config.mcpPublicUrl);
const authMetadataOptions: AuthMetadataOptions = {
  oauthMetadata: mcpAuthorizationServerMetadata(),
  resourceServerUrl: mcpUrl,
  scopesSupported: [config.mcpOauthScope],
  resourceName: "Social Media MCP",
};

const authorizationKeys = [
  "client_id",
  "redirect_uri",
  "response_type",
  "state",
  "code_challenge",
  "code_challenge_method",
  "scope",
  "resource",
] as const;

function respond(
  response: ServerResponse,
  status: number,
  value: Record<string, unknown>,
  headers: Record<string, string> = {},
): void {
  response.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
    ...headers,
  });
  response.end(JSON.stringify(value));
}

function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}

function html(response: ServerResponse, status: number, message: string): void {
  response.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
    "content-security-policy": "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'",
    "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
  });
  response.end(`<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Social Media MCP</title></head><body><p>${escapeHtml(message)}</p></body></html>`);
}

function redirect(response: ServerResponse, location: URL): void {
  response.writeHead(302, {
    location: location.toString(),
    "cache-control": "no-store",
  });
  response.end();
}

function methodNotAllowed(response: ServerResponse, allow: string): void {
  response.writeHead(405, {
    allow,
    "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
  });
  response.end(JSON.stringify({ error: "Method not allowed." }));
}

function oauthErrorResponse(response: ServerResponse, error: McpOAuthError): void {
  respond(
    response,
    error.status,
    { error: error.code, error_description: error.message },
    {
      pragma: "no-cache",
      ...(error.code === "invalid_client" ? { "www-authenticate": 'Basic realm="mcp"' } : {}),
    },
  );
}

export function isWorkerTriggerAuthorized(authorization: string | undefined, secret: string): boolean {
  const expected = Buffer.from(`Bearer ${secret}`);
  const actual = Buffer.from(authorization ?? "");
  return expected.byteLength >= 33 && actual.byteLength === expected.byteLength && timingSafeEqual(actual, expected);
}

function safeError(error: unknown): string {
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown worker error.";
  return message
    .replace(/postgres(?:ql)?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/gi, "[database URL redacted]")
    .replace(/(client_secret|access_token|refresh_token|authorization_code|code)=([^&\s]+)/gi, "$1=[redacted]")
    .replace(/Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+/-]+=*/gi, "Bearer [redacted]")
    .slice(0, 500);
}

function validateWorkerConfiguration(): void {
  if (!config.databaseUrl) throw new Error("Cloud worker requires DATABASE_URL.");
  if (!config.supabaseUrl || !config.supabaseServiceRoleKey) {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY.");
  }
  if (config.tokenStoreBackend !== "supabase" || config.scheduledImageStorage !== "supabase") {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND=supabase and SCHEDULED_IMAGE_STORAGE=supabase.");
  }
  if (!config.cloudTokenEncryptionKey || !/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(config.cloudTokenEncryptionKey)) {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires a 64-character hex CLOUD_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY.");
  }
  if (config.schedulerMode !== "cloud") {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires SCHEDULER_MODE=cloud so no local timer is started.");
  }
  if (Buffer.byteLength(config.workerTriggerSecret) < 32) {
    throw new Error("WORKER_TRIGGER_SECRET must contain at least 32 characters.");
  }
  if (config.oauthCallbackMode !== "cloud") {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE=cloud for remote MCP OAuth.");
  }
  if (!config.oauthFrontendBaseUrl) {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires OAUTH_FRONTEND_BASE_URL for the MCP consent screen.");
  }
  if (!config.mcpPublicUrl.startsWith("https://")) {
    throw new Error("Cloud worker requires an HTTPS MCP_PUBLIC_URL.");
  }
}

async function readWebRequest(
  request: IncomingMessage,
  maxBytes: number,
): Promise<Request> {
  return toWebRequest(request, config.mcpPublicUrl, maxBytes);
}

function formValue(form: FormData, key: string): string | undefined {
  const value = form.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : undefined;
}

async function handleRegister(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "POST") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "POST");
    return;
  }
  const webRequest = await readWebRequest(request, 32 * 1024);
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    body = await webRequest.json();
  } catch {
    throw new McpOAuthError("invalid_client_metadata", "Dynamic client registration requires a JSON body.");
  }
  const client = await registerMcpClient(body);
  respond(response, 201, client as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, { pragma: "no-cache" });
}

async function handleAuthorize(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "GET") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "GET");
    return;
  }
  const url = new URL(request.url ?? "/", config.mcpPublicUrl);
  const params = url.searchParams;
  const clientId = params.get("client_id");
  const redirectUri = params.get("redirect_uri");
  const state = params.get("state");
  if (!clientId || !redirectUri) {
    html(response, 400, "The authorization request is missing client_id or redirect_uri.");
    return;
  }
  const client = await getMcpClient(clientId);
  if (!client) {
    html(response, 400, "Unknown OAuth client.");
    return;
  }
  if (!isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri(client, redirectUri)) {
    html(response, 400, "The redirect_uri is not registered for this client.");
    return;
  }

  const redirectError = (code: string, description: string): void => {
    const target = new URL(redirectUri);
    target.searchParams.set("error", code);
    target.searchParams.set("error_description", description);
    if (state) target.searchParams.set("state", state);
    redirect(response, target);
  };

  if (params.get("response_type") !== "code") {
    redirectError("unsupported_response_type", "Only response_type=code is supported.");
    return;
  }
  if (params.get("code_challenge_method") !== "S256") {
    redirectError("invalid_request", "PKCE code_challenge_method must be S256.");
    return;
  }
  let validated;
  try {
    validated = await validateMcpAuthorizationRequest({
      clientId,
      redirectUri,
      responseType: "code",
      ...(params.get("scope") !== null ? { scope: params.get("scope")! } : {}),
      ...(params.get("resource") !== null ? { resource: params.get("resource")! } : {}),
      ...(params.get("code_challenge") !== null ? { codeChallenge: params.get("code_challenge")! } : {}),
      codeChallengeMethod: "S256",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof McpOAuthError) {
      redirectError(error.code, error.message);
      return;
    }
    throw error;
  }
  if (!config.oauthFrontendBaseUrl) {
    html(response, 500, "OAUTH_FRONTEND_BASE_URL is not configured on the worker.");
    return;
  }
  const target = new URL("/mcp/authorize", config.oauthFrontendBaseUrl);
  for (const key of authorizationKeys) {
    const value = params.get(key);
    if (value !== null) target.searchParams.set(key, value);
  }
  target.searchParams.set("scope", validated.scope);
  target.searchParams.set("resource", validated.resource);
  redirect(response, target);
}

async function handleToken(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "POST") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "POST");
    return;
  }
  const webRequest = await readWebRequest(request, 32 * 1024);
  const contentType = webRequest.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.toLowerCase().includes("application/x-www-form-urlencoded")) {
    throw new McpOAuthError("invalid_request", "Token requests must use application/x-www-form-urlencoded.");
  }
  const form = await webRequest.formData();
  const clientId = formValue(form, "client_id");
  const clientSecret = formValue(form, "client_secret");
  const authorizationHeader = request.headers.authorization;
  const client = await authenticateMcpClient({
    ...(authorizationHeader ? { authorizationHeader } : {}),
    ...(clientId !== undefined ? { clientId } : {}),
    ...(clientSecret !== undefined ? { clientSecret } : {}),
  });
  const grantType = formValue(form, "grant_type");
  if (grantType === "authorization_code") {
    const code = formValue(form, "code");
    const redirectUri = formValue(form, "redirect_uri");
    const codeVerifier = formValue(form, "code_verifier");
    const resource = formValue(form, "resource");
    const tokens = await exchangeMcpAuthorizationCode(client, {
      ...(code !== undefined ? { code } : {}),
      ...(redirectUri !== undefined ? { redirectUri } : {}),
      ...(codeVerifier !== undefined ? { codeVerifier } : {}),
      ...(resource !== undefined ? { resource } : {}),
    });
    respond(response, 200, tokens as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, { pragma: "no-cache" });
    return;
  }
  if (grantType === "refresh_token") {
    const tokens = await refreshMcpTokens(client, formValue(form, "refresh_token") ?? "");
    respond(response, 200, tokens as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, { pragma: "no-cache" });
    return;
  }
  throw new McpOAuthError("unsupported_grant_type", "Only authorization_code and refresh_token are supported.");
}

async function handleRevoke(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "POST") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "POST");
    return;
  }
  const webRequest = await readWebRequest(request, 16 * 1024);
  const contentType = webRequest.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.toLowerCase().includes("application/x-www-form-urlencoded")) {
    throw new McpOAuthError("invalid_request", "Revocation requests must use application/x-www-form-urlencoded.");
  }
  const form = await webRequest.formData();
  const clientId = formValue(form, "client_id");
  const clientSecret = formValue(form, "client_secret");
  const authorizationHeader = request.headers.authorization;
  const client = await authenticateMcpClient({
    ...(authorizationHeader ? { authorizationHeader } : {}),
    ...(clientId !== undefined ? { clientId } : {}),
    ...(clientSecret !== undefined ? { clientSecret } : {}),
  });
  await revokeMcpToken(client, formValue(form, "token") ?? "");
  response.writeHead(200, { "cache-control": "no-store", pragma: "no-cache" });
  response.end();
}

async function handleWellKnown(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "HEAD" && request.method !== "OPTIONS") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "GET, HEAD, OPTIONS");
    return;
  }
  const webRequest = await readWebRequest(request, 0);
  const metadata = oauthMetadataResponse(webRequest, authMetadataOptions);
  if (!metadata) {
    respond(response, 404, { error: "Not found." });
    return;
  }
  await sendWebResponse(response, metadata);
}

async function handleMcp(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
    request.resume();
    response.writeHead(204, {
      allow: "POST, GET, DELETE, OPTIONS",
      "cache-control": "no-store",
    });
    response.end();
    return;
  }
  if (request.method !== "POST" && request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "DELETE") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "POST, GET, DELETE, OPTIONS");
    return;
  }
  const webRequest = await readWebRequest(request, 4 * 1024 * 1024);
  const auth = await mcpAuthGate(webRequest);
  if (auth instanceof Response) {
    await sendWebResponse(response, auth);
    return;
  }
  const mcpResponse = await mcpHandler.fetch(webRequest, { authInfo: auth });
  await sendWebResponse(response, mcpResponse);
}

async function handleWorkerRun(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "POST") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "POST");
    return;
  }
  if (Number(request.headers["content-length"] ?? "0") > 1024) {
    request.resume();
    respond(response, 413, { error: "Request body is too large." });
    return;
  }
  if (!isWorkerTriggerAuthorized(request.headers.authorization, config.workerTriggerSecret)) {
    request.resume();
    respond(response, 401, { error: "Unauthorized." });
    return;
  }
  request.resume();
  if (activeWorkerRun) {
    respond(response, 202, { accepted: true, state: "already_running" });
    return;
  }
  const runId = randomUUID();
  activeWorkerRun = (async () => {
    console.info(JSON.stringify({ event: "cloud_worker_run_started", runId }));
    try {
      const processed = await processDueJobs();
      console.info(JSON.stringify({ event: "cloud_worker_run_completed", runId, processed }));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(JSON.stringify({
        event: "cloud_worker_run_failed",
        runId,
        error: safeError(error),
      }));
    } finally {
      activeWorkerRun = undefined;
    }
  })();
  respond(response, 202, { accepted: true, runId });
}

async function handleMediaUpload(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  if (request.method !== "POST") {
    request.resume();
    methodNotAllowed(response, "POST");
    return;
  }
  const webRequest = await readWebRequest(request, mediaUploadBodyLimit);
  const webResponse = await mediaUploadResponse(webRequest);
  await sendWebResponse(response, webResponse);
}

async function route(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
  const pathname = new URL(request.url ?? "/", "http://localhost").pathname;
  if (request.method === "GET" && pathname === "/health") {
    respond(response, 200, { status: "ok" });
    return;
  }
  if (request.method === "GET" && isOAuthCallbackPath(request.url)) {
    await handleOAuthCallback(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname.startsWith("/.well-known/")) {
    await handleWellKnown(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/oauth/register") {
    await handleRegister(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/oauth/authorize") {
    await handleAuthorize(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/oauth/token") {
    await handleToken(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/oauth/revoke") {
    await handleRevoke(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/mcp" || pathname === "/mcp/") {
    await handleMcp(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/worker/run") {
    await handleWorkerRun(request, response);
    return;
  }
  if (pathname === "/media/upload" || pathname === "/media/upload/") {
    await handleMediaUpload(request, response);
    return;
  }
  request.resume();
  respond(response, 404, { error: "Not found." });
}

export function startWorkerServer(): ReturnType<typeof createServer> {
  validateWorkerConfiguration();
  const server = createServer((request, response) => {
    void route(request, response).catch((error: unknown) => {
      if (response.headersSent) {
        response.end();
        return;
      }
      if (error instanceof HttpBodyTooLargeError) {
        respond(response, 413, { error: "Request body is too large." });
        return;
      }
      if (error instanceof McpOAuthError) {
        oauthErrorResponse(response, error);
        return;
      }
      console.error(JSON.stringify({
        event: "cloud_worker_request_failed",
        error: safeError(error),
      }));
      respond(response, 500, { error: "Internal server error." });
    });
  });
  server.listen(config.workerPort, "0.0.0.0", () => {
    console.info(JSON.stringify({ event: "cloud_worker_listening", port: config.workerPort }));
  });
  return server;
}

if (process.argv[1] && fileURLToPath(import.meta.url) === resolve(process.argv[1])) {
  const server = startWorkerServer();
  const shutdown = () => {
    server.close(() => {
      void Promise.resolve(activeWorkerRun)
        .then(() => closeDatabasePool())
        .finally(() => process.exit(0));
    });
  };
  process.once("SIGTERM", shutdown);
  process.once("SIGINT", shutdown);
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/app-credentials.test.ts
================================================================================

// User-owned Developer App credential tests that run in local MCP mode. The
// module is imported in a dedicated process (node:test isolates each file), so
// the shared-env fallback path can be asserted without a database.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";

process.env["TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND"] = "local";
delete process.env["DATABASE_URL"];
process.env["APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY"] = "b".repeat(64);
process.env["LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID"] = "shared-linkedin-id";
process.env["LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET"] = "shared-linkedin-secret";
process.env["META_APP_ID"] = "shared-meta-id";
process.env["META_APP_SECRET"] = "shared-meta-secret";
process.env["INSTAGRAM_APP_ID"] = "shared-instagram-id";
process.env["INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET"] = "shared-instagram-secret";

const {
  appCredentialFingerprint,
  appCredentialProviders,
  assertAppCredentialFingerprint,
  decryptAppSecret,
  encryptAppSecret,
  isAppCredentialProvider,
  resolveAppCredentials,
  resolveSharedAppCredentials,
  userAppCredentialsActive,
  validateAppCredentialInput,
} = await import("../src/auth/app-credentials.js");

test("exposes exactly the three per-provider developer app slots", () => {
  assert.deepEqual([...appCredentialProviders], ["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"]);
  assert.equal(isAppCredentialProvider("linkedin"), true);
  assert.equal(isAppCredentialProvider("twitter"), false);
  assert.equal(isAppCredentialProvider(42), false);
});

test("local MCP mode never activates user-owned credentials", async () => {
  assert.equal(userAppCredentialsActive(), false);
  // Even with a user id, a local install resolves the shared environment app.
  const resolved = await resolveAppCredentials("some-user", "linkedin");
  assert.equal(resolved.source, "shared");
  assert.equal(resolved.clientId, "shared-linkedin-id");
  assert.equal(resolved.clientSecret, "shared-linkedin-secret");
});

test("shared credentials are resolved per provider and can omit the secret", () => {
  assert.equal(resolveSharedAppCredentials("facebook").clientId, "shared-meta-id");
  assert.equal(resolveSharedAppCredentials("instagram").clientId, "shared-instagram-id");
  assert.equal(resolveSharedAppCredentials("linkedin").clientId, "shared-linkedin-id");
  // A cloud request that only builds a consent URL must not decrypt a secret.
  assert.equal(resolveSharedAppCredentials("linkedin", false).clientSecret, undefined);
});

test("AES-256-GCM round-trips an app secret and binds it to the user and provider", () => {
  const envelope = encryptAppSecret("user-1", "linkedin", "s3cr3t-value");
  assert.equal(decryptAppSecret("user-1", "linkedin", envelope), "s3cr3t-value");

  // AAD must reject a record replayed as another user's or provider's secret.
  assert.throws(() => decryptAppSecret("user-2", "linkedin", envelope), /Could not decrypt/);
  assert.throws(() => decryptAppSecret("user-1", "facebook", envelope), /Could not decrypt/);
});

test("decryption fails safely for an unsupported record version", () => {
  const envelope = encryptAppSecret("user-1", "instagram", "value");
  assert.throws(
    () => decryptAppSecret("user-1", "instagram", { ...envelope, version: 2 as unknown as 1 }),
    /Unsupported developer app credential record version/,
  );
});

test("each save produces an independent ciphertext", () => {
  const first = encryptAppSecret("user-1", "facebook", "same-secret");
  const second = encryptAppSecret("user-1", "facebook", "same-secret");
  assert.notEqual(first.ciphertext, second.ciphertext);
  assert.notEqual(first.iv, second.iv);
});

test("credential fingerprints change with the app or its version", () => {
  const base = appCredentialFingerprint("user", "linkedin", "client-a", "v1");
  assert.match(base, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  assert.equal(base, appCredentialFingerprint("user", "linkedin", "client-a", "v1"));
  assert.notEqual(base, appCredentialFingerprint("shared", "linkedin", "client-a", "v1"));
  assert.notEqual(base, appCredentialFingerprint("user", "facebook", "client-a", "v1"));
  assert.notEqual(base, appCredentialFingerprint("user", "linkedin", "client-b", "v1"));
  assert.notEqual(base, appCredentialFingerprint("user", "linkedin", "client-a", "v2"));
});

test("fingerprint assertion blocks a mid-flow credential change but tolerates legacy sessions", () => {
  const current = appCredentialFingerprint("user", "linkedin", "client-a", "v1");
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => assertAppCredentialFingerprint(current, current));
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => assertAppCredentialFingerprint(undefined, current));
  assert.throws(
    () => assertAppCredentialFingerprint(appCredentialFingerprint("user", "linkedin", "client-a", "v0"), current),
    /changed while this authorization was in progress/,
  );
});

test("validates provider, client id, and secret for save operations", () => {
  assert.equal(validateAppCredentialInput("linkedin", "abc-123_X.Y", "s", false), null);
  assert.match(validateAppCredentialInput("twitter", "abc", "s", false) ?? "", /Unknown provider/);
  assert.match(validateAppCredentialInput("facebook", "", "s", false) ?? "", /Enter the app\/client ID/);
  assert.match(validateAppCredentialInput("facebook", "has space", "s", false) ?? "", /may only contain/);
  assert.match(validateAppCredentialInput("facebook", "id", "", true) ?? "", /Enter the app\/client secret/);
  assert.match(validateAppCredentialInput("facebook", "id", "has space", false) ?? "", /invalid characters/);
  // A blank secret is allowed when it is not required (update preserving the stored secret).
  assert.equal(validateAppCredentialInput("facebook", "id", "", false), null);
});

test("credential storage is user-scoped, parameterized, and never stores a plaintext secret", async () => {
  const source = await readFile(new URL("../src/auth/app-credentials.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  // Reads, updates, and deletes are all scoped by the owning user and provider so
  // one user can never read or modify another user's credential.
  assert.match(source, /WHERE user_id = \$1 AND provider = \$2/);
  assert.match(source, /ON CONFLICT \(user_id, provider\) DO UPDATE/);
  assert.match(source, /INSERT INTO public\.oauth_app_credentials[\s\S]*client_secret_ciphertext/);
  // Only the ciphertext column exists; no plaintext secret column is ever written.
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /client_secret\s+TEXT/i);
  assert.match(source, /createCipheriv\("aes-256-gcm"/);
});

test("OAuth begin records the credential fingerprint and the callback verifies it", async () => {
  const oauth = await readFile(new URL("../src/auth/oauth.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  const sessions = await readFile(new URL("../src/auth/oauth-sessions.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  assert.match(oauth, /appCredentialFingerprint: credentials\.fingerprint/);
  assert.match(oauth, /assertAppCredentialFingerprint\(appCredentialFingerprint, credentials\.fingerprint\)/);
  assert.match(sessions, /app_credential_fingerprint/);
});

// Encryption key validation runs in its own process because the config is read
// once at import time.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";

process.env["TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND"] = "local";
delete process.env["DATABASE_URL"];
process.env["APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY"] = "too-short";

const { encryptAppSecret, decryptAppSecret } = await import("../src/auth/app-credentials.js");

test("a missing or malformed encryption key fails safely on use", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => encryptAppSecret("user-1", "linkedin", "value"),
    /Set APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY to a randomly generated 64-character hex key/,
  );

  const envelope = { version: 1 as const, iv: "00", tag: "00", ciphertext: "00" };
  assert.throws(
    () => decryptAppSecret("user-1", "linkedin", envelope),
    /Set APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY to a randomly generated 64-character hex key/,
  );
});

FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/cloud-storage.test.ts

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";

process.env["SUPABASE_URL"] = "https://example-project.supabase.co";
process.env["SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"] = "test-service-role-key";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_TIMEOUT_MS"] = "1000";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES"] = "1024";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_RETRY_BASE_DELAY_MS"] = "0";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_RETRY_MAX_DELAY_MS"] = "0";
const storage = await import("../src/storage/scheduled-images.js");

const png = Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, 0x00]);

async function withFetch(
  mock: (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>,
  run: () => Promise<void>,
): Promise<void> {
  const original = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = mock as typeof fetch;
  try {
    await run();
  } finally {
    globalThis.fetch = original;
  }
}

test("cloud image upload uses configured Supabase Storage and validates image bytes", async () => {
  await withFetch(async (input, init) => {
    assert.match(String(input), /^https:\/\/example-project\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/social-media-images\/scheduled\/user-1\/.+\.png$/);
    assert.equal((init?.headers as Record<string, string>)["authorization"], "Bearer test-service-role-key");
    assert.equal((init?.headers as Record<string, string>)["content-type"], "image/png");
    return new Response(null, { status: 200 });
  }, async () => {
    const objectPath = await storage.uploadScheduledImage(png, "image/png", ".png", "user-1");
    assert.match(objectPath, /^scheduled\/user-1\/[a-f0-9]{48}\.png$/);
  });

  await assert.rejects(storage.uploadScheduledImage(Buffer.from("<html>"), "image/png", ".png", "user-1"), /does not match image\/png/);
});

test("cloud image validation checks private object and public signed URL response", async () => {
  let requestNumber = 0;
  await withFetch(async (_input, init) => {
    requestNumber += 1;
    if (requestNumber === 1) {
      assert.equal(init?.method, undefined);
      return new Response(png, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/png", "content-length": String(png.length) } });
    }
    if (requestNumber === 2) {
      assert.equal(init?.method, "POST");
      return Response.json({ signedURL: "/storage/v1/object/sign/social-media-images/scheduled/test.png?token=private" });
    }
    assert.match(String(_input), /^https:\/\/example-project\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/sign\//);
    return new Response(png, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/png", "content-length": String(png.length) } });
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredImage("scheduled/test.png", true);
    assert.equal(result.valid, true);
    assert.equal(result.local.bytes, png.length);
    assert.equal(result.public?.valid, true);
    assert.equal(requestNumber, 3);
  });
});

test("cloud image validation resolves Supabase object-relative signed URLs under Storage API path", async () => {
  let requestNumber = 0;
  await withFetch(async (input, init) => {
    requestNumber += 1;
    if (requestNumber === 1) {
      return new Response(png, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } });
    }
    if (requestNumber === 2) {
      assert.equal(init?.method, "POST");
      return Response.json({ signedURL: "/object/sign/social-media-images/scheduled/test.png?token=private" });
    }
    assert.match(String(input), /^https:\/\/example-project\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/sign\//);
    return new Response(png, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } });
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredImage("scheduled/test.png", true);
    assert.equal(result.valid, true);
    assert.equal(result.public?.valid, true);
    assert.equal(requestNumber, 3);
  });
});

test("cloud image validation distinguishes permanent HTTP 404 from transient network failures", async () => {
  let requestNumber = 0;
  await withFetch(async () => {
    requestNumber += 1;
    if (requestNumber === 1) {
      return new Response(png, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "image/png" } });
    }
    if (requestNumber === 2) return Response.json({ signedURL: "https://example-project.supabase.co/signed" });
    return new Response("<html>not found</html>", { status: 404, headers: { "content-type": "text/html" } });
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredImage("scheduled/missing.png", true);
    assert.equal(result.valid, false);
    assert.equal(result.public?.valid, false);
    assert.match(result.error ?? "", /HTTP 404/);
    assert.equal(result.retryable, false);
  });

  await withFetch(async () => {
    throw new TypeError("fetch failed");
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredImage("scheduled/test.png", false);
    assert.equal(result.valid, false);
    assert.equal(result.retryable, true);
  });
});

test("Storage uploads retry bounded temporary failures using the same object key", async () => {
  let requests = 0;
  let firstUrl = "";
  await withFetch(async (input, init) => {
    requests += 1;
    if (requests === 1) firstUrl = String(input);
    else assert.equal(String(input), firstUrl);
    assert.equal((init?.headers as Record<string, string>)["x-upsert"], "true");
    return new Response(null, { status: requests === 1 ? 503 : 200 });
  }, async () => {
    await storage.uploadScheduledImage(png, "image/png", ".png", "user-1");
    assert.equal(requests, 2);
  });

  requests = 0;
  await withFetch(async () => {
    requests += 1;
    return new Response(null, { status: 429 });
  }, async () => {
    await assert.rejects(storage.uploadScheduledImage(png, "image/png", ".png", "user-1"), /HTTP 429/);
    assert.equal(requests, 3);
  });
});

test("cloud PDF upload stores application/pdf documents and rejects non-PDF data", async () => {
  const pdf = Buffer.from("%PDF-1.7\n%\xE2\xE3\xCF\xD3\n");
  await withFetch(async (input, init) => {
    assert.match(String(input), /^https:\/\/example-project\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/social-media-images\/scheduled\/user-1\/.+\.pdf$/);
    assert.equal((init?.headers as Record<string, string>)["content-type"], "application/pdf");
    return new Response(null, { status: 200 });
  }, async () => {
    const objectPath = await storage.uploadScheduledDocument(pdf, "application/pdf", ".pdf", "user-1");
    assert.match(objectPath, /^scheduled\/user-1\/[a-f0-9]{48}\.pdf$/);
  });

  await assert.rejects(
    storage.uploadScheduledDocument(Buffer.from("<html>"), "application/pdf", ".pdf", "user-1"),
    /does not match application\/pdf/,
  );
  await assert.rejects(
    storage.uploadScheduledDocument(pdf, "image/png", ".png", "user-1"),
    /Unsupported scheduled document MIME type/,
  );
});

test("cloud document validation reads the private PDF object and rejects non-PDF bytes", async () => {
  const pdf = Buffer.from("%PDF-1.7\nobj\nendobj\n");
  let requests = 0;
  await withFetch(async () => {
    requests += 1;
    return new Response(pdf, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "application/pdf" } });
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredDocument("scheduled/guide.pdf");
    assert.equal(result.valid, true);
    assert.equal(result.contentType, "application/pdf");
    assert.equal(result.bytes, pdf.length);
    assert.equal(requests, 1);
  });

  await withFetch(async () => {
    return new Response(Buffer.from("<html>not a pdf</html>"), {
      status: 200,
      headers: { "content-type": "application/pdf" },
    });
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredDocument("scheduled/broken.pdf");
    assert.equal(result.valid, false);
    assert.match(result.error ?? "", /does not match application\/pdf/);
    assert.equal(result.retryable, false);
  });
});

test("isPdf only accepts a leading %PDF- header", () => {
  assert.equal(storage.isPdf(Buffer.from("%PDF-1.4 content")), true);
  assert.equal(storage.isPdf(Buffer.from("%%PDF")), false);
  assert.equal(storage.isPdf(Buffer.from("image bytes")), false);
  assert.equal(storage.isPdf(Buffer.alloc(0)), false);
});

// Test fixture: produces the real LinkedIn consent URL via beginAuthorization
// in an isolated process whose environment is provided by the parent test.
// It performs no network requests to any provider: only the URL is built and
// printed, using the local in-memory authorization-session store.
import { beginAuthorization } from "../../src/auth/oauth.js";

try {
  const { authorizationUrl, sessionId, expiresAt } = await beginAuthorization("linkedin", "consent-url-probe");
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ authorizationUrl, sessionId, expiresAt }));
  process.exit(0);
} catch (error) {
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) }));
  process.exit(1);
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/helpers/tsx-spawn.ts
================================================================================

import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const packageRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));

// Spawns `node <tsx-cli> <args>` with an extended environment and collects the
// child's stdio. Used by LinkedIn OAuth tests to run `beginAuthorization` in a
// fresh process with a deterministic configuration (config is evaluated at
// import time, so per-test overrides need process isolation).
export function execPathSpawnTsx(
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
): Promise<{ stdout: string; stderr: string; status: number | null }> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, args, {
      cwd: packageRoot,
      env: { ...process.env, ...env },
      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
    });
    const stdout: Buffer[] = [];
    const stderr: Buffer[] = [];
    child.stdout.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => stdout.push(chunk));
    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => stderr.push(chunk));
    child.on("error", reject);
    child.on("close", (status) => {
      resolve({
        stdout: Buffer.concat(stdout).toString("utf8").trim(),
        stderr: Buffer.concat(stderr).toString("utf8").trim(),
        status,
      });
    });
  });
}
