================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/linkedin-oauth.test.ts
================================================================================

// Focused LinkedIn OAuth authorization-URL tests. The real `beginAuthorization`
// is executed in a child process with a deterministic cloud-mode-shaped
// environment (matching the production Render worker values) because local
// callback mode binds the local OAuth/media listener port. No network requests
// are made: the consent URL is only parsed and asserted against. No production
// data is touched; the child uses an in-memory session store.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { test } from "node:test";

import { execPathSpawnTsx } from "./helpers/tsx-spawn.js";
import { config } from "../src/config/env.js";
import { InMemoryOAuthSessionStore, expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath } from "../src/auth/oauth-sessions.js";
import { oauthCallbackUrl, processOAuthCallback, type OAuthCallbackOutcome } from "../src/auth/oauth.js";
import type { CreateOAuthSessionInput } from "../src/auth/oauth-sessions.js";

const PRODUCTION_REDIRECT_BASE = "https://social-media-cloud-worker.onrender.com";
const LINKEDIN_CALLBACK_URI = `${PRODUCTION_REDIRECT_BASE}/oauth/linkedin/callback`;

function linkedinSession(overrides: Partial<CreateOAuthSessionInput> = {}): CreateOAuthSessionInput {
  return {
    sessionId: "linkedin-session",
    state: "linkedin-state",
    userId: "linkedin-user",
    platform: "linkedin",
    redirectUri: LINKEDIN_CALLBACK_URI,
    expiresAt: Date.now() + 60_000,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

test("OAuth callback URLs keep the platform paths used by the registered provider apps", () => {
  // Facebook and Instagram share the Meta callback route registered in the
  // Meta dashboard; LinkedIn has its own exact route in the LinkedIn portal.
  assert.equal(oauthCallbackUrl("facebook"), `${config.redirectBaseUrl}/oauth/meta/callback`);
  assert.equal(oauthCallbackUrl("instagram"), `${config.redirectBaseUrl}/oauth/meta/callback`);
  assert.equal(oauthCallbackUrl("linkedin"), `${config.redirectBaseUrl}/oauth/linkedin/callback`);
  // No path component may ever move the callback off the registered host: after
  // the scheme the value must be exactly `//host` with no path or trailing slash.
  const parsedBase = new URL(config.redirectBaseUrl);
  assert.equal(parsedBase.pathname, "/");
  assert.equal(parsedBase.search, "");
  assert.equal(parsedBase.hash, "");
});

test("LinkedIn sessions can only be claimed from the LinkedIn callback path, and Meta callbacks cannot claim them", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(linkedinSession({ sessionId: "li-iso", state: "li-iso-state" }));

  // A Meta-path callback (facebook/instagram) must never consume the LinkedIn state.
  const metaClaim = await store.claim("li-iso-state", ["facebook", "instagram"]);
  assert.equal(metaClaim.claimed, false);
  if (!metaClaim.claimed) assert.equal(metaClaim.reason, "wrong_platform");

  // The LinkedIn path claims it and reveals the persisted redirect URI for exchange.
  const linkedinClaim = await store.claim("li-iso-state", expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath("/oauth/linkedin/callback")!);
  assert.equal(linkedinClaim.claimed, true);
  if (linkedinClaim.claimed) {
    assert.equal(linkedinClaim.session.platform, "linkedin");
    assert.equal(linkedinClaim.session.redirectUri, LINKEDIN_CALLBACK_URI);
  }
});

test("the token exchange step receives the exact persisted callback URI", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(linkedinSession({ sessionId: "li-exchange", state: "li-exchange-state" }));

  const finishCalls: Array<{ platform: string; userId: string; code: string; redirectUri: string }> = [];
  const outcome: OAuthCallbackOutcome = await processOAuthCallback(
    new URLSearchParams({ state: "li-exchange-state", code: "auth-code" }),
    expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath("/oauth/linkedin/callback")!,
    {
      store,
      finish: async (platform, userId, code, redirectUri) => {
        finishCalls.push({ platform, userId, code, redirectUri });
      },
    },
  );

  assert.equal(outcome.result, "connected");
  assert.equal(finishCalls.length, 1);
  // Byte-identical to what the consent screen was handed, otherwise LinkedIn
  // rejects the exchange with a redirect_uri mismatch.
  assert.equal(finishCalls[0]?.redirectUri, LINKEDIN_CALLBACK_URI);
  assert.equal(finishCalls[0]?.platform, "linkedin");
  assert.equal((await store.getStatus("li-exchange", "linkedin-user")).status, "connected");
});

test("LinkedIn callback rejects invalid and reused states", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();

  const unknown = await processOAuthCallback(new URLSearchParams({ state: "bogus", code: "c" }), ["linkedin"], {
    store,
    finish: async () => undefined,
  });
  assert.equal(unknown.result, "failed");

  await store.create(linkedinSession({ sessionId: "li-reuse", state: "li-reuse-state" }));
  const first = await processOAuthCallback(new URLSearchParams({ state: "li-reuse-state", code: "c1" }), ["linkedin"], {
    store,
    finish: async () => undefined,
  });
  assert.equal(first.result, "connected");

  const second = await processOAuthCallback(new URLSearchParams({ state: "li-reuse-state", code: "c2" }), ["linkedin"], {
    store,
    finish: async () => undefined,
  });
  assert.equal(second.result, "failed");
});

test("beginAuthorization('linkedin') emits the production consent URL with the registered callback URI", async () => {
  const childScript = new URL("./fixtures/linkedin-consent-child.mjs", import.meta.url);
  const tsxCli = fileURLToPath(new URL("../node_modules/tsx/dist/cli.mjs", import.meta.url));
  const { stdout, stderr, status } = await execPathSpawnTsx([tsxCli, fileURLToPath(childScript)], {
      // Force the local backend so a developer's real `.env` cannot change the
      // outcome. Local MCP mode always builds the consent URL from the shared
      // environment app, which is exactly what this test asserts.
      TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND: "local",
      SCHEDULED_IMAGE_STORAGE: "local",
      SCHEDULER_MODE: "disabled",
      OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT: "34117",
      OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE: "local",
      OAUTH_REDIRECT_BASE_URL: PRODUCTION_REDIRECT_BASE,
      LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID: "test-client-id",
      LINKEDIN_SCOPES: "openid profile w_member_social",
    },
  );

  const payload =
    stdout.length > 0
      ? (JSON.parse(stdout) as { authorizationUrl: string; sessionId: string; error?: string })
      : { authorizationUrl: "", error: `child exited ${status}: ${stderr || "(no stderr)"}` };
  assert.ok(payload.authorizationUrl, payload.error);

  const url = new URL(payload.authorizationUrl);
  assert.equal(url.host, "www.linkedin.com");
  assert.equal(url.pathname, "/oauth/v2/authorization");
  // Exactly the URI that must be registered in the LinkedIn Developer Portal.
  assert.equal(url.searchParams.get("redirect_uri"), LINKEDIN_CALLBACK_URI);
  // The raw encoded form must be percent-encoded without double encoding.
  assert.match(
    url.search,
    /redirect_uri=https%3A%2F%2Fsocial-media-cloud-worker\.onrender\.com%2Foauth%2Flinkedin%2Fcallback/,
  );
  assert.equal(url.searchParams.get("response_type"), "code");
  assert.equal(url.searchParams.get("client_id"), "test-client-id");
  assert.match(url.searchParams.get("state") ?? "", /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  assert.equal(url.searchParams.get("scope"), "openid profile w_member_social");
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/linkedin-token-refresh.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import test from "node:test";

const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "social-media-mcp-token-test-"));
process.env["SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_FILE"] = join(directory, "tokens.enc");
process.env["SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY"] = "a".repeat(64);
process.env["TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND"] = "local";
process.env["LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID"] = "test-client";
process.env["LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET"] = "test-client-secret";
const tokenStore = await import("../src/auth/token-store.js");
const { linkedinTokenForUse } = await import("../src/platforms/linkedin.js");

test.after(async () => rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }));

const OWNER = "test-owner";

test("LinkedIn refresh token renews and persists an expiring token without changing the local vault format", async () => {
  await tokenStore.saveTokens(OWNER, {
    linkedin: {
      accessToken: "old-access-token",
      refreshToken: "old-refresh-token",
      expiresAt: Date.now() - 1,
      scopes: ["w_member_social"],
      connectedAt: Date.now() - 10_000,
      accountId: "member-id",
    },
  });
  const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = async (input, init) => {
    assert.equal(String(input), "https://www.linkedin.com/oauth/v2/accessToken");
    assert.equal(init?.method, "POST");
    assert.match(String(init?.body), /grant_type=refresh_token/);
    assert.match(String(init?.body), /refresh_token=old-refresh-token/);
    return Response.json({
      access_token: "new-access-token",
      refresh_token: "new-refresh-token",
      expires_in: 3600,
    });
  };
  try {
    const refreshed = await linkedinTokenForUse(OWNER);
    assert.equal(refreshed.accessToken, "new-access-token");
    assert.equal(refreshed.refreshToken, "new-refresh-token");
    assert.ok(refreshed.expiresAt && refreshed.expiresAt > Date.now());
    assert.equal((await tokenStore.loadTokens(OWNER)).linkedin?.accessToken, "new-access-token");
  } finally {
    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
  }
});

test("expired LinkedIn tokens without a provider refresh token require reauthorization", async () => {
  await tokenStore.saveTokens(OWNER, {
    linkedin: {
      accessToken: "expired-access-token",
      expiresAt: Date.now() - 1,
      scopes: ["w_member_social"],
      connectedAt: Date.now() - 10_000,
    },
  });
  await assert.rejects(linkedinTokenForUse(OWNER), /has no refresh token\. Reauthorize LinkedIn/);
});

test("refresh uses the user-owned LinkedIn app credentials when resolved", async () => {
  await tokenStore.saveTokens(OWNER, {
    linkedin: {
      accessToken: "user-owned-old",
      refreshToken: "user-owned-refresh",
      expiresAt: Date.now() - 1,
      scopes: ["w_member_social"],
      connectedAt: Date.now() - 10_000,
    },
  });
  const resolver = async (userId: string) => {
    assert.equal(userId, OWNER);
    return {
      provider: "linkedin" as const,
      source: "user" as const,
      clientId: "user-app-id",
      clientSecret: "user-app-secret",
      fingerprint: "f".repeat(64),
    };
  };
  const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = async (input, init) => {
    assert.equal(String(input), "https://www.linkedin.com/oauth/v2/accessToken");
    const body = String(init?.body);
    assert.match(body, /grant_type=refresh_token/);
    assert.match(body, /refresh_token=user-owned-refresh/);
    // The refresh must carry the issuing app's own id and secret.
    assert.match(body, /client_id=user-app-id/);
    assert.match(body, /client_secret=user-app-secret/);
    return Response.json({ access_token: "user-owned-new", expires_in: 3600 });
  };
  try {
    const refreshed = await linkedinTokenForUse(OWNER, resolver);
    assert.equal(refreshed.accessToken, "user-owned-new");
  } finally {
    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
  }
});

test("refresh surfaces an actionable error when the resolved app is incomplete", async () => {
  await tokenStore.saveTokens(OWNER, {
    linkedin: {
      accessToken: "incomplete-old",
      refreshToken: "incomplete-refresh",
      expiresAt: Date.now() - 1,
      scopes: ["w_member_social"],
      connectedAt: Date.now() - 10_000,
    },
  });
  const resolver = async () => ({
    provider: "linkedin" as const,
    source: "shared" as const,
    clientId: "",
    clientSecret: "",
    fingerprint: "0".repeat(64),
  });
  await assert.rejects(linkedinTokenForUse(OWNER, resolver), /Configure them in Developer App Settings/);
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/mcp.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:net";
import { dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import test from "node:test";
import { Client } from "@modelcontextprotocol/client";
import { StdioClientTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/client/stdio";
import { config } from "../src/config/env.js";

async function availablePort(): Promise<number> {
  const server = createServer();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once("error", reject);
    server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve);
  });
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw new Error("Could not determine a free test port.");
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.close((error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve());
  });
  return address.port;
}

test("built MCP server exposes tools and validates dry-run publishing", async () => {
  const projectRoot = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
  const callbackPort = await availablePort();
  const transport = new StdioClientTransport({
    command: process.execPath,
    args: ["dist/index.js"],
    cwd: projectRoot,
    stderr: "pipe",
    env: { ...process.env, OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT: String(callbackPort) },
  });
  const client = new Client({ name: "social-media-mcp-test", version: "1.0.0" });

  try {
    await client.connect(transport);
    const { tools } = await client.listTools();
    const names = tools.map((tool) => tool.name).sort();
    assert.deepEqual(names, [
      "social_account_info",
      "social_authorization_start",
      "social_authorization_status",
      "social_authorization_wait",
      "social_cancel_scheduled_post",
      "social_connection_status",
      "social_publish",
      "social_recent_posts",
      "social_retry_scheduled_post",
      "social_schedule_media",
      "social_schedule_posts",
      "social_scheduled_posts_status",
      "social_token_store_status",
      "social_upload_media_intent",
    ]);

    const facebookAuthorization = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_authorization_start",
      arguments: { platform: "facebook" },
    });
    const facebookAuthorizationBody = JSON.parse(String(
      facebookAuthorization.content[0]?.type === "text" ? facebookAuthorization.content[0].text : "{}",
    )) as { authorizationUrl?: string; callback?: string };
    const facebookRedirectUri = new URL(facebookAuthorizationBody.authorizationUrl ?? "").searchParams.get("redirect_uri");
    assert.equal(facebookAuthorizationBody.callback, facebookRedirectUri);
    assert.equal(
      facebookRedirectUri,
      `${config.redirectBaseUrl}/oauth/meta/callback`,
    );

    const authorizationStatus = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_authorization_status",
      arguments: { authorizationSessionId: "unknown-session" },
    });
    const authorizationStatusBody = JSON.parse(String(
      authorizationStatus.content[0]?.type === "text" ? authorizationStatus.content[0].text : "{}",
    )) as { success?: boolean; error?: string };
    assert.equal(authorizationStatusBody.success, false);
    assert.match(authorizationStatusBody.error ?? "", /session not found or expired/);

    const result = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_publish",
      arguments: { platforms: ["facebook", "linkedin"], text: "Validation only" },
    });
    assert.equal(result.isError, undefined);
    const body = JSON.parse(String(result.content[0]?.type === "text" ? result.content[0].text : "{}")) as {
      results: Array<{ platform: string; dryRun?: boolean; published: boolean }>;
    };
    assert.deepEqual(body.results.map(({ platform, dryRun, published }) => ({ platform, dryRun, published })), [
      { platform: "facebook", dryRun: true, published: false },
      { platform: "linkedin", dryRun: true, published: false },
    ]);

    const instagram = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_publish",
      arguments: { platforms: ["instagram"], text: "Text-only validation" },
    });
    const instagramBody = JSON.parse(String(instagram.content[0]?.type === "text" ? instagram.content[0].text : "{}")) as {
      results: Array<{ published: boolean; issues?: string[] }>;
    };
    assert.equal(instagramBody.results[0]?.published, false);
    assert.match(instagramBody.results[0]?.issues?.join(" ") ?? "", /requires an image reference/);
  } finally {
    await client.close();
  }
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/mcp-oauth.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";
import { config } from "../src/config/env.js";
import {
  McpOAuthError,
  hashMcpSecret,
  isAllowedMcpRedirectUri,
  isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri,
  mcpAuthorizationServerMetadata,
  mcpProtectedResourceMetadata,
  normalizeMcpResource,
  normalizeMcpScope,
  type McpOAuthClient,
} from "../src/auth/mcp-oauth.js";

test("hashes bearer-style values with a stable SHA-256 digest", () => {
  const first = hashMcpSecret("a-secret-value");
  assert.equal(first, hashMcpSecret("a-secret-value"));
  assert.notEqual(first, hashMcpSecret("another-secret-value"));
  assert.match(first, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
});

test("normalizes the supported scope and rejects anything else", () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeMcpScope(), config.mcpOauthScope);
  assert.equal(normalizeMcpScope(config.mcpOauthScope), config.mcpOauthScope);
  assert.throws(
    () => normalizeMcpScope("read write"),
    (error: unknown) => error instanceof McpOAuthError && error.code === "invalid_scope",
  );
});

test("normalizes resource indicators and rejects credentials or fragments", () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeMcpResource("https://example.com/mcp/"), "https://example.com/mcp");
  assert.equal(normalizeMcpResource("https://example.com/mcp#fragment"), "https://example.com/mcp");
  assert.throws(
    () => normalizeMcpResource("https://user:pass@example.com/mcp"),
    (error: unknown) => error instanceof McpOAuthError,
  );
  assert.throws(() => normalizeMcpResource("not-a-url"), (error: unknown) => error instanceof McpOAuthError);
});

test("only allows registered Claude origins and loopback callbacks", () => {
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("https://claude.ai/api/mcp/auth_callback"), true);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("https://claude.com/api/mcp/auth_callback"), true);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("https://evil.example/callback"), false);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("http://localhost:3118/callback"), true);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("http://127.0.0.1:3118/callback"), true);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("http://example.com/callback"), false);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("https://claude.ai/callback#fragment"), false);
  assert.equal(isAllowedMcpRedirectUri("https://user:pass@claude.ai/callback"), false);
});

test("matches redirect URIs against the registered client allowlist", () => {
  const client: McpOAuthClient = {
    clientId: "mcp_test",
    redirectUris: ["https://claude.ai/api/mcp/auth_callback"],
    grantTypes: ["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
    responseTypes: ["code"],
    tokenEndpointAuthMethod: "none",
  };
  assert.equal(isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri(client, "https://claude.ai/api/mcp/auth_callback"), true);
  assert.equal(isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri(client, "https://claude.ai/api/mcp/other"), false);
  assert.equal(isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri(client, "https://evil.example/callback"), false);
});

test("advertises path-aware protected-resource and authorization-server metadata", () => {
  const resource = mcpProtectedResourceMetadata();
  assert.equal(resource.resource, config.mcpPublicUrl);
  assert.deepEqual(resource.scopes_supported, [config.mcpOauthScope]);
  assert.deepEqual(resource.authorization_servers, [new URL(config.mcpPublicUrl).origin]);

  const metadata = mcpAuthorizationServerMetadata();
  assert.equal(metadata.issuer, new URL(config.mcpPublicUrl).origin);
  assert.equal(metadata.authorization_endpoint, `${metadata.issuer}/oauth/authorize`);
  assert.equal(metadata.token_endpoint, `${metadata.issuer}/oauth/token`);
  assert.equal(metadata.registration_endpoint, `${metadata.issuer}/oauth/register`);
  assert.equal(metadata.revocation_endpoint, `${metadata.issuer}/oauth/revoke`);
  assert.deepEqual(metadata.code_challenge_methods_supported, ["S256"]);
});

test("migration 010 stores only digests and grants no browser role access", async () => {
  const sql = await readFile(new URL("../migrations/010_mcp_oauth.sql", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(sql, /CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public\.mcp_oauth_clients/i);
  assert.match(sql, /CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public\.mcp_oauth_authorization_codes/i);
  assert.match(sql, /CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public\.mcp_oauth_access_tokens/i);
  assert.match(sql, /CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public\.mcp_oauth_refresh_tokens/i);
  assert.match(sql, /code_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY/i);
  assert.match(sql, /token_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY/i);
  assert.match(sql, /client_secret_hash TEXT/i);
  assert.match(sql, /REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public\.mcp_oauth_clients FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated/i);
  assert.match(sql, /GRANT ALL ON TABLE public\.mcp_oauth_refresh_tokens TO service_role/i);
  assert.doesNotMatch(sql, /\bDROP\s+TABLE\b|\bDELETE\s+FROM\b|\bDROP\s+COLUMN\b/i);
});

test("filenames are display-only and cannot traverse or shape a storage key", () => {
  assert.equal(intents.sanitizeUploadFilename("../../etc/passwd.png"), "passwd.png");
  assert.equal(intents.sanitizeUploadFilename("C:\\evil\\beach shot.png"), "beach shot.png");
  assert.equal(intents.sanitizeUploadFilename("holiday\x00\x1f.mp4"), "holiday.mp4");
  assert.equal(intents.sanitizeUploadFilename("a".repeat(300)), "a".repeat(200));
});

test("content types are constrained to the private bucket's accepted set", () => {
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("image/jpeg"), ".jpg");
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("image/png"), ".png");
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("image/gif"), ".gif");
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("video/mp4"), ".mp4");
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("video/quicktime"), ".mov");
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("application/pdf"), ".pdf");
  assert.equal(intents.extensionForContentType("image/webp"), undefined);
  assert.equal(intents.contentTypeForFilename("holiday.jpg"), "image/jpeg");
  assert.equal(intents.contentTypeForFilename("holiday.JPEG"), "image/jpeg");
  assert.equal(intents.contentTypeForFilename("clip.mov"), "video/quicktime");
  assert.equal(intents.contentTypeForFilename("song.mp3"), undefined);

test("upload intents reject unsupported or mismatched declared media", () => {
  assert.throws(() => intents.assertSupportedMediaUpload("image", "image/webp"), /Unsupported upload content type/);
  assert.throws(() => intents.assertSupportedMediaUpload("video", "image/png"), /not valid for a video upload/);
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => intents.assertSupportedMediaUpload("document", "application/pdf"));
});

test("declared sizes are bounded by the per-kind bucket limits", () => {
  intents.assertAllowedDeclaredSize("image", config.scheduleImageMaxBytes);
  assert.throws(() => intents.assertAllowedDeclaredSize("image", config.scheduleImageMaxBytes + 1), /byte limit/);
  assert.throws(() => intents.assertAllowedDeclaredSize("video", 0), /positive integer/);
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => intents.assertAllowedDeclaredSize("video", config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes));
});

test("upload token is opaque, 256-bit entropy, and hashed to a stable digest", () => {
  const token = intents.newUploadToken();
  assert.match(token, /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
  assert.match(intents.hashUploadToken(token), /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
  assert.notEqual(intents.hashUploadToken(token), intents.hashUploadToken(intents.newUploadToken()));
});

test("rate limiter allows the configured burst and blocks beyond the window", () => {
  const limiter = intents.createSlidingWindowRateLimiter({ maxRequests: 3, windowMs: 1000 });
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-a", 0), true);
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-a", 10), true);
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-a", 20), true);
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-a", 30), false);
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-b", 30), true);
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-a", 999), false, "all three hits are still inside the window");
  assert.equal(limiter.allow("user-a", 1050), true, "the window has fully slid past all three hits");
});

// --- Upload endpoint request evaluation ---------------------------------------

test("uploads must match the intent's kind, content type, size, and availability", () => {
  const intent = { kind: "image" as const, contentType: "image/jpeg", expectedBytes: 0 };
  assert.equal(intents.evaluateMediaUpload(intent, "image/jpeg", new Uint8Array(8)), undefined);
  const mismatch = intents.evaluateMediaUpload(intent, "image/png", new Uint8Array(8));
  assert.equal(mismatch?.status, 415);
  assert.match(mismatch?.message ?? "", /single-use/);
  const unsupported = intents.evaluateMediaUpload(intent, "image/webp", new Uint8Array(8));
  assert.equal(unsupported?.status, 415);
  const empty = intents.evaluateMediaUpload(intent, "image/jpeg", new Uint8Array(0));
  assert.equal(empty?.status, 400);
  const oversized = intents.evaluateMediaUpload(intent, "image/jpeg", new Uint8Array(config.scheduleImageMaxBytes + 1));
  assert.equal(oversized?.status, 413);
});

// --- Endpoint token extraction (web Request, no server or database) ------------

test("the endpoint accepts the capability token via bearer header or query, never an MCP token", () => {
  const bearer = new Request("https://worker.example.com/media/upload", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { authorization: "Bearer abc123" },
  });
  assert.equal(endpoint.extractUploadToken(bearer), "abc123");
  const query = new Request("https://worker.example.com/media/upload?token=abc123", { method: "POST" });
  assert.equal(endpoint.extractUploadToken(query), "abc123");
  const missing = new Request("https://worker.example.com/media/upload", { method: "POST" });
  assert.equal(endpoint.extractUploadToken(missing), undefined);
  const wrongScheme = new Request("https://worker.example.com/media/upload", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { authorization: "Basic abc123" },
  });
  assert.equal(endpoint.extractUploadToken(wrongScheme), undefined);
});

  assert.ok(endpoint.mediaUploadBodyLimit >= config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes);
  assert.ok(endpoint.mediaUploadBodyLimit < config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes + 2 * 1024 * 1024);
});

test("expired uploads are cleaned up through the reference-counted storage helper", async () => {

  assert.match(intentsSource, /await deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects\(candidates\)/,
    "sweeping never deletes an object without the shared reference check");
  const selectIndex = intentsSource.indexOf("SELECT id, storage_path, kind");
  const cleanupIndex = intentsSource.indexOf("deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects(candidates)");
  const rowIndex = intentsSource.indexOf("storage_path IS NULL OR id = ANY($2::text[])");
  assert.ok(selectIndex >= 0 && cleanupIndex > selectIndex, "candidates are read before the reference-counted delete");
  assert.ok(rowIndex > cleanupIndex, "rows whose object survived the sweep are kept for retry");
  const sharedStorage = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

// --- Failed-attempt throttling (HTTP 429) --------------------------------------

test("failed-attempt penalty box blocks after the limit and slides with the window", () => {
  const box = intents.createSlidingWindowPenaltyBox({ maxAttempts: 3, windowMs: 1000 });
  assert.equal(box.blocked("ip-a", 0), false);
  box.record("ip-a", 0);
  box.record("ip-a", 10);
  box.record("ip-a", 20);
  assert.equal(box.blocked("ip-a", 30), true);
  assert.equal(box.blocked("ip-a", 40), true, "blocking consults the recorded history without adding a hit");
  assert.equal(box.blocked("ip-b", 40), false, "blocks are per client address");
  assert.equal(box.blocked("ip-a", 1050), false, "the penalty window has slid past all recorded failures");
});

test("the upload endpoint throttles failed attempts before any claim and records every rejection", async () => {
  const source = await readFile(new URL("../src/media/upload-endpoint.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(source, /Too many failed upload attempts/);
  assert.match(source, /media_upload_throttled/);
  assert.match(source, /windowMs: 600_000/);
  const blockedIndex = source.indexOf("attemptPenalty.blocked(clientKey)");
  const claimIndex = source.indexOf("claimPendingMediaUploadIntent(token)");
  const arrayBufferIndex = source.indexOf("request.arrayBuffer()");
  assert.ok(blockedIndex >= 0 && blockedIndex < claimIndex, "throttling rejects requests before any intent claim");
  assert.ok(claimIndex < arrayBufferIndex, "the single-use claim still precedes byte transfer");
  const recordCalls = source.match(/attemptPenalty\.record\(clientKey\)/g) ?? [];
  assert.ok(recordCalls.length >= 4, "bad tokens, rejected payloads, unknown intents, and failures all count");
  const endpoint = await readFile(new URL("../src/config/env.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(endpoint, /mediaUploadAttemptLimit = integer\("MEDIA_UPLOAD_ATTEMPT_LIMIT", "30", 1\)/);
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/meta-container.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import { waitForInstagramContainer } from "../src/platforms/meta.js";

async function withInstagramStatuses<T>(
  statuses: string[],
  run: () => Promise<T>,
): Promise<{ result: T; requests: number }> {
  const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;
  let requests = 0;
  globalThis.fetch = async (_input: RequestInfo | URL): Promise<Response> => {
    const status = statuses[requests++];
    if (!status) throw new Error("Unexpected additional Instagram status request.");
    return Response.json({ status_code: status });
  };
  try {
    return { result: await run(), requests };
  } finally {
    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
  }
}

test("publishing waits until Instagram reports the container is finished", async () => {
  const { requests } = await withInstagramStatuses(["IN_PROGRESS", "FINISHED"], () =>
    waitForInstagramContainer("container-id", "access-token"),
  );
  assert.equal(requests, 2);
});

test("Instagram container processing errors fail before publish", async () => {
  await withInstagramStatuses(["ERROR"], () =>
    assert.rejects(waitForInstagramContainer("container-id", "access-token"), /status ERROR/),
  );
});

FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/multiuser.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createCipheriv, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import test from "node:test";

import type { StoredToken } from "../src/platforms/types.js";

const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "social-media-mcp-multiuser-"));
const tokenFile = join(directory, "tokens.enc");
process.env["SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_FILE"] = tokenFile;
process.env["SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY"] = "a".repeat(64);
process.env["TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND"] = "local";
process.env["MCP_OWNER_USER_ID"] = "local-owner";

const tokenStore = await import("../src/auth/token-store.js");
const { config } = await import("../src/config/env.js");

test.after(async () => rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }));

const instagramToken: StoredToken = {
  accessToken: "alice-instagram",
  scopes: ["instagram_business_basic"],
  connectedAt: 1,
};
const linkedinToken: StoredToken = {
  accessToken: "bob-linkedin",
  scopes: ["w_member_social"],
  connectedAt: 2,
};

function writeLegacyVault(payload: unknown): string {
  const key = Buffer.from(process.env["SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY"]!, "hex");
  const iv = randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  const ciphertext = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  return JSON.stringify({
    version: 1,
    iv: iv.toString("hex"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("hex"),
    ciphertext: ciphertext.toString("hex"),
  });
}

test("local token vault isolates each user's credentials", async () => {
  await tokenStore.saveLocalTokens("alice", { instagram: instagramToken });
  await tokenStore.saveLocalTokens("bob", { linkedin: linkedinToken });

  assert.equal((await tokenStore.loadTokens("alice")).instagram?.accessToken, "alice-instagram");
  assert.equal((await tokenStore.loadTokens("alice")).linkedin, undefined);
  assert.equal((await tokenStore.loadTokens("bob")).linkedin?.accessToken, "bob-linkedin");
  assert.equal((await tokenStore.loadTokens("bob")).instagram, undefined);
  assert.deepEqual(await tokenStore.loadTokens("carol"), {});
});

test("legacy single-user vaults are adopted by the configured owner identity", async () => {
  await writeFile(tokenFile, writeLegacyVault({ linkedin: { ...linkedinToken, accessToken: "legacy-linkedin" } }));

  assert.equal((await tokenStore.loadTokens(config.ownerUserId)).linkedin?.accessToken, "legacy-linkedin");
  assert.deepEqual(await tokenStore.loadTokens("someone-else"), {});
});

test("migration 007 adds per-user ownership to scheduled posts and tokens", async () => {
  const sql = await readFile(new URL("../migrations/007_multi_user.sql", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  assert.match(sql, /ALTER TABLE public\.scheduled_posts[\s\S]*ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS user_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'local-owner'/i);
  assert.match(sql, /ALTER TABLE public\.oauth_tokens[\s\S]*ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS user_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'local-owner'/i);
  assert.match(sql, /PRIMARY KEY \(user_id, platform\)/i);
  assert.match(sql, /scheduled_posts_user_due_idx/i);
});

test("scheduler scopes ownership-sensitive queries by user_id", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  assert.match(scheduler, /async function loadJobs\(ownerId: string\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /WHERE id = \$1 AND user_id = \$2 AND status = 'scheduled'/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /WHERE id = \$1 AND user_id = \$2 FOR UPDATE/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /INSERT INTO public\.scheduled_posts[\s\S]*\(user_id, id, platform/);
});

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import { instagramPermissionScopes } from "../src/auth/oauth.js";

test("normalizes Instagram permissions returned as a comma-delimited string", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    instagramPermissionScopes("instagram_business_basic,instagram_business_content_publish"),
    ["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"],
  );
});

test("normalizes Instagram permissions returned as an array", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    instagramPermissionScopes(["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"]),
    ["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"],
  );
});

test("normalizes comma-delimited permissions within an array", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    instagramPermissionScopes(["instagram_business_basic, instagram_business_content_publish"]),
    ["instagram_business_basic", "instagram_business_content_publish"],
  );
});

test("returns no scopes when Instagram omits permissions", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(instagramPermissionScopes(undefined), []);
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/oauth-sessions.test.ts

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";

import {
  InMemoryOAuthSessionStore,
  buildFrontendRedirectUrl,
  expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath,
  hashOAuthState,
  isOAuthCallbackPath,
  type CreateOAuthSessionInput,
} from "../src/auth/oauth-sessions.js";
import { processOAuthCallback } from "../src/auth/oauth.js";

function sessionInput(overrides: Partial<CreateOAuthSessionInput> = {}): CreateOAuthSessionInput {
  return {
    sessionId: "session-1",
    state: "state-1",
    userId: "user-1",
    platform: "linkedin",
    redirectUri: "https://social-media-cloud-worker.onrender.com/oauth/linkedin/callback",
    expiresAt: Date.now() + 60_000,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

test("maps callback paths to their expected platforms", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath("/oauth/meta/callback"), ["facebook", "instagram"]);
  assert.deepEqual(expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath("/oauth/linkedin/callback"), ["linkedin"]);
  assert.equal(expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath("/worker/run"), null);
  assert.equal(expectedPlatformsForCallbackPath("/health"), null);
  assert.equal(isOAuthCallbackPath("/oauth/meta/callback?state=abc"), true);
  assert.equal(isOAuthCallbackPath("/oauth/linkedin/callback"), true);
  assert.equal(isOAuthCallbackPath("/health"), false);
  assert.equal(isOAuthCallbackPath(undefined), false);
});

test("builds a fixed allowlisted frontend redirect and refuses unsafe bases", () => {
  assert.equal(
    buildFrontendRedirectUrl("https://app.example.com/", "connected", "linkedin"),
    "https://app.example.com/accounts?oauth=connected&platform=linkedin",
  );
  assert.equal(
    buildFrontendRedirectUrl("https://app.example.com", "failed"),
    "https://app.example.com/accounts?oauth=failed",
  );
  assert.equal(buildFrontendRedirectUrl("", "failed"), null);
  assert.equal(buildFrontendRedirectUrl("   ", "failed"), null);
  assert.equal(buildFrontendRedirectUrl("javascript:alert(1)", "failed"), null);
  assert.equal(buildFrontendRedirectUrl("ftp://example.com", "failed"), null);
  assert.equal(buildFrontendRedirectUrl("https://user:pass@example.com", "failed"), null);
  // A path or host supplied elsewhere can never move the redirect off /accounts.
  const injected = buildFrontendRedirectUrl("https://app.example.com/accounts", "failed", "facebook");
  assert.equal(injected, "https://app.example.com/accounts?oauth=failed&platform=facebook");
});

test("hashes OAuth state deterministically without keeping the raw value", () => {
  const hash = hashOAuthState("super-secret-state");
  assert.match(hash, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  assert.equal(hash, hashOAuthState("super-secret-state"));
  assert.notEqual(hash, hashOAuthState("super-secret-state-2"));
});

test("in-memory store claims a state exactly once", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "once", state: "once-state", userId: "u1" }));

  const first = await store.claim("once-state", ["linkedin"]);
  assert.equal(first.claimed, true);
  if (first.claimed) assert.equal(first.session.redirectUri, "https://social-media-cloud-worker.onrender.com/oauth/linkedin/callback");

  const second = await store.claim("once-state", ["linkedin"]);
  assert.equal(second.claimed, false);
  if (!second.claimed) assert.equal(second.reason, "used");
});

test("in-memory store rejects expired states", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "expired", state: "expired-state", expiresAt: Date.now() - 1 }));

  const result = await store.claim("expired-state", ["linkedin"]);
  assert.equal(result.claimed, false);
  if (!result.claimed) assert.equal(result.reason, "expired");

  const status = await store.getStatus("expired", "user-1");
  assert.equal(status.status, "expired");
});

test("in-memory store rejects wrong-platform states without consuming them", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({
    sessionId: "fb",
    state: "fb-state",
    userId: "u2",
    platform: "facebook",
    redirectUri: "https://social-media-cloud-worker.onrender.com/oauth/meta/callback",
  }));

  const wrong = await store.claim("fb-state", ["linkedin"]);
  assert.equal(wrong.claimed, false);
  if (!wrong.claimed) assert.equal(wrong.reason, "wrong_platform");

  const right = await store.claim("fb-state", ["facebook", "instagram"]);
  assert.equal(right.claimed, true);
  if (right.claimed) assert.equal(right.session.platform, "facebook");
});

test("in-memory store isolates authorization sessions by authenticated user", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "iso", state: "iso-state", userId: "alice" }));

  await assert.rejects(() => store.getStatus("iso", "bob"), /not found or expired/i);
  await assert.rejects(() => store.waitForStatus("iso", "bob", 0), /not found or expired/i);

  const status = await store.getStatus("iso", "alice");
  assert.equal(status.status, "pending");
});

test("in-memory store wakes waiters when the callback completes", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "wait", state: "wait-state", userId: "carol" }));

  const waiting = store.waitForStatus("wait", "carol", 5);
  await store.updateStatus("wait", "connected");
  const resolved = await waiting;
  assert.equal(resolved.status, "connected");
});

test("callback marks a provider cancellation without attempting an exchange", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "cancel", state: "cancel-state", userId: "dave" }));
  let finishCalled = false;

  const outcome = await processOAuthCallback(
    new URLSearchParams({ state: "cancel-state", error: "access_denied" }),
    ["linkedin"],
    { store, finish: async () => { finishCalled = true; } },
  );

  assert.equal(outcome.result, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(finishCalled, false);
  assert.equal((await store.getStatus("cancel", "dave")).status, "cancelled");
});

test("callback fails safely when the authorization code is missing", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "nocode", state: "nocode-state", userId: "erin" }));

  const outcome = await processOAuthCallback(
    new URLSearchParams({ state: "nocode-state" }),
    ["linkedin"],
    { store, finish: async () => { throw new Error("should not run"); } },
  );

  assert.equal(outcome.result, "failed");
  assert.equal((await store.getStatus("nocode", "erin")).status, "failed");
});

test("callback records a failed exchange without leaking secrets or codes", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "fail", state: "fail-state", userId: "frank" }));

  const outcome = await processOAuthCallback(
    new URLSearchParams({ state: "fail-state", code: "auth-code-123" }),
    ["linkedin"],
    {
      store,
      finish: async () => {
        throw new Error("client_secret=super-secret&access_token=abc123 failed for authorization_code=auth-code-123");
      },
    },
  );

  assert.equal(outcome.result, "failed");
  const status = await store.getStatus("fail", "frank");
  assert.equal(status.status, "failed");
  const recorded = status.error ?? "";
  assert.match(recorded, /\[redacted\]/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(recorded, /super-secret/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(recorded, /abc123/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(recorded, /auth-code-123/);
});

test("callback rejects an unknown state and a reused state", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  const unknown = await processOAuthCallback(new URLSearchParams({ state: "nope" }), ["linkedin"], {
    store,
    finish: async () => undefined,
  });
  assert.equal(unknown.result, "failed");

  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "reuse", state: "reuse-state", userId: "gina" }));
  const first = await processOAuthCallback(new URLSearchParams({ state: "reuse-state", code: "c1" }), ["linkedin"], {
    store,
    finish: async () => undefined,
  });
  assert.equal(first.result, "connected");
  const second = await processOAuthCallback(new URLSearchParams({ state: "reuse-state", code: "c2" }), ["linkedin"], {
    store,
    finish: async () => undefined,
  });
  assert.equal(second.result, "failed");
});

test("migration 008 creates an idempotent, RLS-protected OAuth session table", async () => {
  const sql = await readFile(new URL("../migrations/008_oauth_sessions.sql", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  assert.match(sql, /CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public\.oauth_authorization_sessions/i);
  assert.match(sql, /state_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE/i);
  assert.match(sql, /status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'/i);
  assert.match(sql, /CHECK \(status IN \('pending', 'processing', 'connected', 'cancelled', 'failed', 'expired'\)\)/i);
  assert.match(sql, /expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL/i);
  assert.match(sql, /ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY/i);
  assert.match(sql, /REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public\.oauth_authorization_sessions/i);
  assert.match(sql, /GRANT ALL ON TABLE public\.oauth_authorization_sessions TO service_role/i);
  assert.match(sql, /CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS oauth_authorization_sessions_user_idx/i);
  assert.doesNotMatch(sql, /\bDROP\s+TABLE\b|\bDELETE\s+FROM\b/i);
});

test("callback forwards the credential fingerprint recorded at authorization start", async () => {
  const store = new InMemoryOAuthSessionStore();
  await store.create(sessionInput({
    sessionId: "fp",
    state: "fp-state",
    userId: "henry",
    appCredentialFingerprint: "fingerprint-abc",
  }));
  let received: string | undefined = "sentinel";

  const outcome = await processOAuthCallback(
    new URLSearchParams({ state: "fp-state", code: "code-1" }),
    ["linkedin"],
    {
      store,
      finish: async (_platform, _userId, _code, _redirectUri, fingerprint) => {
        received = fingerprint;
      },
    },
  );

  assert.equal(outcome.result, "connected");
  assert.equal(received, "fingerprint-abc");
});

test("callback passes no fingerprint for a legacy session without one", async () => {

  await store.create(sessionInput({ sessionId: "legacy", state: "legacy-state", userId: "iris" }));
  let received: string | undefined = "sentinel";

  const outcome = await processOAuthCallback(
    new URLSearchParams({ state: "legacy-state", code: "code-2" }),
    ["linkedin"],
    {
      store,
      finish: async (_platform, _userId, _code, _redirectUri, fingerprint) => {
        received = fingerprint;
      },
    },
  );

  assert.equal(outcome.result, "connected");
  assert.equal(received, undefined);
});

});

FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/remote-media.test.ts

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import { isPrivateIpAddress, resolvePublicHttpsUrl } from "../src/utils/remote-media.js";

test("rejects private, loopback, link-local, and reserved IP addresses", () => {
  for (const address of [
    "0.0.0.0",
    "10.1.2.3",
    "100.64.0.1",
    "127.0.0.1",
    "169.254.1.1",
    "172.16.5.4",
    "192.0.0.1",
    "192.168.1.1",
    "198.18.0.1",
    "224.0.0.1",
    "255.255.255.255",
    "::",
    "::1",
    "fc00::1",
    "fe80::1",
    "ff02::1",
    "2001:db8::1",
    "::ffff:10.0.0.1",
    "not-an-ip",
  ]) {
    assert.equal(isPrivateIpAddress(address), true, `${address} should be rejected`);
  }
  for (const address of ["8.8.8.8", "1.1.1.1", "2606:4700:4700::1111"]) {
    assert.equal(isPrivateIpAddress(address), false, `${address} should be allowed`);
  }
});

test("resolves only public HTTPS media URLs", async () => {
  await assert.rejects(() => resolvePublicHttpsUrl("http://example.com/a.jpg"), /HTTPS/);
  await assert.rejects(() => resolvePublicHttpsUrl("https://user:pass@example.com/a.jpg"), /credentials/);
  await assert.rejects(() => resolvePublicHttpsUrl("https://localhost/a.jpg"), /publicly reachable/);
  await assert.rejects(() => resolvePublicHttpsUrl("https://127.0.0.1/a.jpg"), /private|reserved|loopback/);
  await assert.rejects(() => resolvePublicHttpsUrl("not-a-url"), /absolute URL/);

  const target = await resolvePublicHttpsUrl("https://8.8.8.8/media/a.jpg");
  assert.equal(target.address, "8.8.8.8");
  assert.equal(target.hostname, "8.8.8.8");
  assert.equal(target.url.toString(), "https://8.8.8.8/media/a.jpg");
});

test("rejects SSRF targets before any outbound request", async () => {
  for (const url of [
    "https://10.0.0.1/media/a.jpg",
    "https://172.16.0.1/media/a.jpg",
    "https://192.168.0.5/media/a.jpg",
    "https://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
    "https://127.0.0.1/media/a.jpg",
    "https://[::1]/media/a.jpg",
    "https://[fe80::1]/media/a.jpg",
    "https://localhost/media/a.jpg",
    "http://example.com/media/a.jpg",
    "https://user:pass@example.com/media/a.jpg",
  ]) {
    await assert.rejects(() => resolvePublicHttpsUrl(url), /HTTPS|credentials|publicly reachable|private|reserved|loopback/);
  }
});

test("accepts a public HTTPS image URL", async () => {
  const target = await resolvePublicHttpsUrl("https://8.8.8.8/media/image.png");
  assert.equal(target.url.protocol, "https:");
  assert.equal(target.address, "8.8.8.8");
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/remote-media-input.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import { REMOTE_MEDIA_HINT, rejectRemoteLocalPaths } from "../src/tools/media-input.js";

// Verified behavior (MCP specification + modelcontextprotocol discussion #1197):
// tool arguments are JSON and the published protocol has no client-to-server
// file-upload primitive. Claude.ai therefore cannot hand the Render worker image
// bytes or a readable local path. Remote tools must accept only HTTPS URLs.

test("remote mode rejects imagePath before any filesystem read", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(true, { imagePath: "/etc/passwd" }),
    /imagePath[\s\S]*remote MCP/i,
  );
});

test("remote mode rejects documentPath", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(true, { documentPath: "C:\\secrets\\report.pdf" }),
    /documentPath[\s\S]*remote MCP/i,
  );
});

test("remote mode rejects any media[].path", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(true, { mediaPaths: [undefined, "/etc/shadow"] }),
    /media\[\]\.path[\s\S]*remote MCP/i,
  );
});

test("remote mode accepts URL-only media arguments", () => {
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(true, {}));
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(true, {
    imagePath: undefined,
    documentPath: undefined,
    mediaPaths: [undefined, undefined],
  }));
});

test("local stdio mode keeps local file support", () => {
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(false, {
    imagePath: "/tmp/image.png",
    documentPath: "/tmp/document.pdf",
    mediaPaths: ["/tmp/image.png", "/tmp/video.mp4"],
  }));
});

test("remote rejection points callers at the HTTPS upload fallback", () => {
  assert.throws(
    () => rejectRemoteLocalPaths(true, { imagePath: "/etc/passwd" }),
    (error: unknown) => {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      return message.includes("HTTPS") && /upload/i.test(message) && message.includes(REMOTE_MEDIA_HINT);
    },
  );
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/remote-tool-guards.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";

// Regression guard: every remote media tool must reject local paths, and the
// only local filesystem read in the tool layer must sit behind that guard.

test("all remote media tools call the local-path guard", async () => {
  const register = await readFile(new URL("../src/tools/register.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  const guardCalls = register.match(/rejectRemoteLocalPaths\(remote/g) ?? [];
  assert.equal(
    guardCalls.length,
    3,
    "social_publish, social_schedule_posts, and social_schedule_media must each call the guard",
  );

  for (const field of ["imagePath", "documentPath", "mediaPaths"]) {
    assert.match(register, new RegExp(field), `${field} must be passed to the guard`);
  }
});

test("the only tool-level readFile sits after the remote path guard", async () => {
  const register = await readFile(new URL("../src/tools/register.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  const readFileCalls = register.match(/await readFile\(/g) ?? [];
  assert.equal(readFileCalls.length, 1, "only the stdio media[].path branch may read a file");

  const guardIndex = register.indexOf("rejectRemoteLocalPaths(remote, { documentPath, mediaPaths");
  const readFileIndex = register.indexOf("await readFile(item.path)");
  assert.ok(guardIndex >= 0, "the media tool guard must be present");
  assert.ok(readFileIndex > guardIndex, "readFile must run only after the remote path guard");
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/scheduled-image.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer, type Server } from "node:http";
import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import test from "node:test";
import {
  validateScheduledImage,
  validateScheduledImageWithRetries,
} from "../src/utils/scheduled-image.js";

const imageToken = "a".repeat(64);
const jpegBytes = Buffer.from([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 0xe0, 0x00, 0x10, 0x4a, 0x46, 0x49, 0x46]);

async function fixture(): Promise<{ imagePath: string; cleanup: () => Promise<void> }> {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "scheduled-image-test-"));
  const imagePath = path.join(directory, "image.jpg");
  await writeFile(imagePath, jpegBytes);
  return { imagePath, cleanup: () => rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }) };
}

async function startServer(handler: (server: Server) => void): Promise<{ baseUrl: string; close: () => Promise<void> }> {
  const server = createServer();
  handler(server);
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once("error", reject);
    server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve);
  });
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw new Error("Could not determine test server port.");
  return {
    baseUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`,
    close: () => new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      server.close((error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve());
    }),
  };
}

function imageResponse(bytes: Uint8Array = jpegBytes, contentType = "image/jpeg", status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(bytes, { status, headers: { "content-type": contentType } });
}

test("validates the local route and dynamically configured public image URL", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  const requestedUrls: string[] = [];
  try {
    const validation = await validateScheduledImage({
      imagePath: file.imagePath,
      imageToken,
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      checkPublic: true,
      localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43127",
      publicBaseUrl: "https://changed-tunnel.example",
      fetcher: async (input) => {
        requestedUrls.push(String(input));
        return imageResponse();
      },
    });
    assert.equal(validation.valid, true);
    assert.equal(validation.local.status, 200);
    assert.equal(validation.local.bytes, jpegBytes.byteLength);
    assert.equal(validation.public?.valid, true);
    assert.deepEqual(requestedUrls, [
      `http://127.0.0.1:43127/scheduled-media/${imageToken}`,
      `https://changed-tunnel.example/scheduled-media/${imageToken}`,
    ]);
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("reports an incorrect local image-server port as a retryable connection failure", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  const unused = await startServer((server) => {
    server.on("request", (_request, response) => response.end());
  });
  await unused.close();
  try {
    const validation = await validateScheduledImage({
      imagePath: file.imagePath,
      imageToken,
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      checkPublic: false,
      localBaseUrl: unused.baseUrl,
      timeoutMs: 1000,
    });
    assert.equal(validation.valid, false);
    assert.equal(validation.retryable, true);
    assert.match(validation.error ?? "", /connection refused|request failed/i);
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("detects an image route 404 and does not inspect the public tunnel", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  const requestedUrls: string[] = [];
  try {
    const validation = await validateScheduledImage({
      imagePath: file.imagePath,
      imageToken,
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      checkPublic: true,
      localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43128",
      publicBaseUrl: "https://old-tunnel.example",
      fetcher: async (input) => {
        requestedUrls.push(String(input));
        return new Response("<html>Not found</html>", {
          status: 404,
          headers: { "content-type": "text/html" },
        });
      },
    });
    assert.equal(validation.valid, false);
    assert.equal(validation.local.status, 404);
    assert.equal(validation.retryable, false);
    assert.match(validation.error ?? "", /HTTP 404/);
    assert.equal(requestedUrls.length, 1);
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("rejects HTML, invalid MIME types, and empty image bodies", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  try {
    for (const [response, expected] of [
      [new Response("<html>ngrok error</html>", { headers: { "content-type": "text/html" } }), /invalid image MIME type/],
      [imageResponse(jpegBytes, "image/webp"), /invalid image MIME type/],
      [imageResponse(Buffer.alloc(0)), /empty image response/],
      [imageResponse(Buffer.from("<html>error</html>")), /not valid image\/jpeg image data/],
    ] as const) {
      const validation = await validateScheduledImage({
        imagePath: file.imagePath,
        imageToken,
        contentType: "image/jpeg",
        checkPublic: false,
        localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43129",
        fetcher: async () => response.clone(),
      });
      assert.equal(validation.valid, false);
      assert.match(validation.error ?? "", expected);
    }
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("bounds image response size and identifies request timeouts", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  try {
    const oversized = await validateScheduledImage({
      imagePath: file.imagePath,
      imageToken,
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      checkPublic: false,
      localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43130",
      maxBytes: 5,
      fetcher: async () => imageResponse(),
    });
    assert.match(oversized.error ?? "", /exceeds the configured 5-byte size limit/);
    assert.equal(oversized.retryable, false);

    const timeout = await validateScheduledImage({
      imagePath: file.imagePath,
      imageToken,
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      checkPublic: false,
      localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43131",
      fetcher: async () => { throw new DOMException("Timed out", "TimeoutError"); },
    });
    assert.match(timeout.error ?? "", /timed out/i);
    assert.equal(timeout.retryable, true);
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("retries temporary tunnel failures with exponential backoff and stops after recovery", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  let publicRequests = 0;
  const delays: number[] = [];
  try {
    const result = await validateScheduledImageWithRetries(
      {
        imagePath: file.imagePath,
        imageToken,
        contentType: "image/jpeg",
        checkPublic: true,
        localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43132",
        publicBaseUrl: "https://new-tunnel.example",
        fetcher: async (input) => {
          if (String(input).startsWith("https://")) {
            publicRequests += 1;
            return publicRequests < 3
              ? new Response("temporarily unavailable", { status: 503 })
              : imageResponse();
          }
          return imageResponse();
        },
      },
      3,
      10,
      () => undefined,
      30000,
      async (delayMs) => { delays.push(delayMs); },
    );
    assert.equal(result.validation.valid, true);
    assert.equal(result.retries, 2);
    assert.equal(publicRequests, 3);
    assert.deepEqual(delays, [10, 20]);
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("bounds retries for ngrok 404 failures from an unavailable or outdated public URL", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  let publicRequests = 0;
  const delays: number[] = [];
  try {
    await assert.rejects(
      validateScheduledImageWithRetries(
        {
          imagePath: file.imagePath,
          imageToken,
          contentType: "image/jpeg",
          checkPublic: true,
          localBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:43133",
          publicBaseUrl: "https://stale-tunnel.example",
          fetcher: async (input) => {
            if (String(input).startsWith("https://")) {
              publicRequests += 1;
              return new Response("<html>ngrok 404</html>", {
                status: 404,
                headers: { "content-type": "text/html" },
              });
            }
            return imageResponse();
          },
        },
        3,
        10,
        () => undefined,
        30000,
        async (delayMs) => { delays.push(delayMs); },
      ),
      /failed after 3 attempt\(s\).*HTTP 404/,
    );
    assert.equal(publicRequests, 3);
    assert.deepEqual(delays, [10, 20]);
  } finally {
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

test("checks an actual local image route bound to the configured local port", async () => {
  const file = await fixture();
  const requestedPaths: string[] = [];
  const local = await startServer((server) => {
    server.on("request", (request, response) => {
      requestedPaths.push(request.url ?? "");
      response.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "image/jpeg" });
      response.end(jpegBytes);
    });
  });
  try {
    const validation = await validateScheduledImage({
      imagePath: file.imagePath,
      imageToken,
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      checkPublic: false,
      localBaseUrl: local.baseUrl,
    });
    assert.equal(validation.valid, true);
    assert.deepEqual(requestedPaths, [`/scheduled-media/${imageToken}`]);
  } finally {
    await local.close();
    await file.cleanup();
  }
});

import assert from "node:assert/strict";

  Buffer.from([0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x18]),

]);

async function withFetch(
  mock: (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>,
  run: () => Promise<void>,
): Promise<void> {

    globalThis.fetch = original;
  }
}

test("media schema migration adds media_type, child media table, and video bucket types", async () => {

  assert.match(migration, /UNIQUE \(post_id, position\)/i);
  assert.match(migration, /video\/mp4/);

});
