test("isIsoBmffVideo and matchesMediaSignature accept MP4 headers and reject other data", () => {
  assert.equal(storage.isIsoBmffVideo(mp4), true);
  assert.equal(storage.isIsoBmffVideo(Buffer.from("<html>")), false);
  assert.equal(storage.matchesMediaSignature(mp4, "video/mp4"), true);
  assert.equal(storage.matchesMediaSignature(mp4, "image/png"), false);
});

test("cloud video upload stores video/mp4 objects and rejects non-video data", async () => {
  await withFetch(async (input, init) => {
    assert.match(String(input), /^https:\/\/example-project\.supabase\.co\/storage\/v1\/object\/social-media-images\/scheduled\/user-1\/.+\.mp4$/);
    assert.equal((init?.headers as Record<string, string>)["content-type"], "video/mp4");
    return new Response(null, { status: 200 });
  }, async () => {
    const objectPath = await storage.uploadScheduledVideo(mp4, "video/mp4", ".mp4", "user-1");
    assert.match(objectPath, /^scheduled\/user-1\/[a-f0-9]{48}\.mp4$/);
  });

  await assert.rejects(storage.uploadScheduledVideo(Buffer.from("<html>"), "video/mp4", ".mp4", "user-1"), /does not match video\/mp4/);
  await assert.rejects(storage.uploadScheduledVideo(mp4, "image/png", ".png", "user-1"), /Unsupported scheduled video MIME type/);
});

test("cloud video validation reads the private object and validates the public signed URL", async () => {
  let requestNumber = 0;
  await withFetch(async (_input, init) => {
    requestNumber += 1;
    if (requestNumber === 1) {
      return new Response(mp4, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "video/mp4", "content-length": String(mp4.length) } });
    }
    if (requestNumber === 2) {
      assert.equal(init?.method, "POST");
      return Response.json({ signedURL: "https://example-project.supabase.co/signed-video" });
    }
    return new Response(mp4, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "video/mp4", "content-length": String(mp4.length) } });
  }, async () => {
    const result = await storage.validateStoredVideo("scheduled/clip.mp4", true);
    assert.equal(result.valid, true);
    assert.equal(result.local.bytes, mp4.length);
    assert.equal(result.public?.valid, true);
    assert.equal(requestNumber, 3);
  });
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/scheduled-media-lifecycle.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";

process.env["SUPABASE_URL"] = "https://example-project.supabase.co";
process.env["SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"] = "test-service-role-key";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_TIMEOUT_MS"] = "1000";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES"] = "1024";
process.env["SCHEDULE_VIDEO_MAX_BYTES"] = "1048576";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_RETRY_BASE_DELAY_MS"] = "0";
process.env["SCHEDULE_IMAGE_RETRY_MAX_DELAY_MS"] = "0";
process.env["SCHEDULE_SIGNED_URL_TTL_SECONDS"] = "86400";
const storage = await import("../src/storage/scheduled-images.js");
const scheduler = await import("../src/scheduler.js");

const png = Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, 0x00]);
const ownerObject = (owner: string, extension = ".png") =>
  `scheduled/${owner}/${"a".repeat(48)}${extension}`;

async function withFetch(
  mock: (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>,
  run: () => Promise<void>,
): Promise<void> {
  const original = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = mock as typeof fetch;
  try {
    await run();
  } finally {
    globalThis.fetch = original;
  }
}

test("schedules 24 hours and several days ahead, because media is a permanent path", () => {
  const now = Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1, 12, 0, 0);
  const in24Hours = new Date(now + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  const inSevenDays = new Date(now + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();

  assert.doesNotThrow(() => scheduler.assertFutureScheduleTime(in24Hours, now));
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => scheduler.assertFutureScheduleTime(inSevenDays, now));
  assert.throws(() => scheduler.assertFutureScheduleTime(new Date(now - 1000).toISOString(), now), /future/);
  assert.throws(() => scheduler.assertFutureScheduleTime("not-a-date", now), /future/);
});

test("storage objects are namespaced by user and the segment is always path-safe", () => {
  assert.equal(storage.storageOwnerSegment("alice"), "alice");
  assert.match(storage.storageOwnerSegment("user/../../etc"), /^[a-f0-9]{32}$/);
  assert.match(storage.storageOwnerSegment(""), /^[a-f0-9]{32}$/);

  const owned = ownerObject("alice");
  assert.equal(storage.isOwnedStorageObjectPath(owned, "alice"), true);
  assert.equal(storage.isOwnedStorageObjectPath(owned, "bob"), false);
  assert.throws(() => storage.assertOwnedStorageObjectPath(owned, "bob"), /does not belong to this account/);
});

test("an expiring signed URL can never become the durable media reference", () => {
  const expiredSignedUrl = "https://example-project.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/sign/social-media-images/scheduled/alice/x.png?token=expired";
  assert.equal(storage.isOwnedStorageObjectPath(expiredSignedUrl, "alice"), false);
  assert.throws(() => storage.assertOwnedStorageObjectPath(expiredSignedUrl, "alice"), /does not belong/);

  // Traversal and absolute paths are rejected even with a matching prefix.
  assert.equal(storage.isOwnedStorageObjectPath("scheduled/alice/../bob/x.png", "alice"), false);
  assert.equal(storage.isOwnedStorageObjectPath("/scheduled/alice/" + "a".repeat(48) + ".png", "alice"), false);
});

test("a job scheduled days earlier retrieves the same object and signs a fresh URL at publish time", async () => {
  const objectPath = ownerObject("alice");
  const requests: Array<{ url: string; method?: string; body?: unknown }> = [];

  await withFetch(async (input, init) => {
    requests.push({ url: String(input), method: init?.method, body: init?.body });
    if (init?.method === "POST") {
      // A brand-new signed URL is created now, not at scheduling time.
      return Response.json({ signedURL: `/storage/v1/object/sign/social-media-images/${objectPath}?token=fresh` });
    }
    return new Response(png, {
      status: 200,
      headers: { "content-type": "image/png", "content-length": String(png.length) },
    });
  }, async () => {
    const image = await storage.downloadScheduledImage(objectPath);
    assert.equal(image.contentType, "image/png");
    assert.equal(image.bytes.length, png.length);

    // The download used the permanent private object path with server-side
    // credentials; it never carried an expired URL token.
    const download = requests[0]!;
    assert.equal(download.url, `https://example-project.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/social-media-images/${objectPath}`);
    assert.ok(!download.url.includes("token="));

    const url = await storage.createScheduledImageUrl(objectPath);
    assert.match(url, /\?token=fresh$/);
    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(String(requests[1]?.body)), { expiresIn: 86400 });
  });
});

test("publish and retry resolve the same permanent storage path", async () => {
  const source = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  // The worker downloads the stored object (server-side credentials) and only
  // creates a signed URL at publish time, for platforms that need one.
  assert.match(source, /downloadScheduledImage\(job\.imageStoragePath\)/);
  assert.match(source, /createScheduledImageUrl\(job\.imageStoragePath\)/);
  assert.match(source, /downloadScheduledVideo\(videoItem\.storagePath\)/);
  assert.match(source, /downloadScheduledDocument\(job\.documentStoragePath\)/);

  // Caller-supplied references are ownership-checked before use.
  assert.match(source, /assertOwnedStorageObjectPath\(imageInput\.storagePath, ownerId\)/);
  assert.match(source, /assertOwnedStorageObjectPath\(item\.storagePath, ownerId\)/);
  assert.match(source, /assertOwnedStorageObjectPath\(document\.storagePath, ownerId\)/);

  // New uploads are namespaced by the authenticated owner.
  assert.match(source, /uploadScheduledImage\(imageBytes, contentType, extension, ownerId\)/);
  assert.match(source, /uploadScheduledVideo\(bytes, item\.contentType, item\.extension\.toLowerCase\(\), ownerId\)/);
  assert.match(source, /uploadScheduledDocument\(documentBytes, documentContentType, "\.pdf", ownerId\)/);

  // The durable reference stored in PostgreSQL is the object path, never a URL.
  assert.match(source, /image_storage_path, content_type/);
  assert.match(source, /storage_path, content_type, created_at/);

  // Retrying reuses the existing row/media and must not clear the reference.
  const retryStart = source.indexOf("export async function retryScheduledPost");
  const retryEnd = source.indexOf("export async function cancelScheduledPost");
  assert.ok(retryStart >= 0 && retryEnd > retryStart);
  const retrySource = source.slice(retryStart, retryEnd);
  assert.doesNotMatch(retrySource, /image_storage_path\s*=\s*NULL|storage_path\s*=\s*NULL/i);
  assert.match(retrySource, /SET status = 'scheduled'/);
});

test("upload API returns the permanent path and scopes the object to the signed-in user", async () => {
  const route = await readFile(
    new URL("../../social-media-web/src/app/api/media/upload/route.ts", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );

  assert.match(route, /const session = await requireApiSession\(\)/);
  assert.match(route, /session\.user\.id/);
  assert.match(route, /uploadScheduledVideo/);
  assert.match(route, /storagePath,/);
  assert.match(route, /expiresInSeconds/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(route, /NEXT_PUBLIC_.*SUPABASE/);
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/scheduled-reschedule.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";
import {
  canRescheduleScheduledPost,
  normalizeRescheduledTime,
} from "../src/scheduler.js";

test("only posts still waiting to publish can be rescheduled", () => {
  assert.equal(canRescheduleScheduledPost("scheduled"), true);
  assert.equal(canRescheduleScheduledPost("publishing"), false);
  assert.equal(canRescheduleScheduledPost("published"), false);
  assert.equal(canRescheduleScheduledPost("failed"), false);
  assert.equal(canRescheduleScheduledPost("cancelled"), false);
});

test("reschedule validation requires a valid future timestamp", () => {
  const now = Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1, 12, 0, 0);
  const future = new Date(now + 3_600_000).toISOString();
  assert.equal(normalizeRescheduledTime(future, now), future);

  assert.throws(() => normalizeRescheduledTime("", now), /valid date and time/i);
  assert.throws(() => normalizeRescheduledTime("not-a-date", now), /valid date and time/i);
  assert.throws(() => normalizeRescheduledTime(new Date(now).toISOString(), now), /future/i);
  assert.throws(
    () => normalizeRescheduledTime(new Date(now - 60_000).toISOString(), now),
    /future/i,
  );
});

test("rescheduling updates the existing job in place and never inserts a duplicate", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  const start = scheduler.indexOf("export async function rescheduleScheduledPost");
  assert.ok(start >= 0, "rescheduleScheduledPost should be exported from the scheduler");
  const source = scheduler.slice(start);

  assert.match(source, /SELECT \* FROM public\.scheduled_posts WHERE id = \$1 AND user_id = \$2 FOR UPDATE/);
  assert.match(source, /UPDATE public\.scheduled_posts\s+SET scheduled_at = \$3/);
  assert.match(source, /claimed_at = NULL/);
  assert.match(source, /canRescheduleScheduledPost\(row\.status\)/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /\bINSERT INTO\b/i);
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /\bDELETE FROM\b/i);
});

test("the worker claim and the reschedule lock cooperate", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

  // Resume uses a row lock; the due-job claim skips locked rows so a post being
  // rescheduled is never published at its previous time.
  assert.match(scheduler, /WHERE status = 'scheduled' AND scheduled_at <= NOW\(\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED/);
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/scheduled-retry.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import { canSafelyRetryScheduledPost } from "../src/scheduler.js";

test("only pre-publish image or token validation failures can be manually retried safely", () => {
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("failed", "image_validation"), true);
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("failed", "token_validation"), true);
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("failed", "publishing"), false);
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("failed", undefined), false);
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("scheduled", "image_validation"), false);
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("publishing", "image_validation"), false);
  assert.equal(canSafelyRetryScheduledPost("published", "image_validation"), false);
});

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/schedule-text-only.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtemp, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { createServer } from "node:net";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import test from "node:test";
import { Client } from "@modelcontextprotocol/client";
import { StdioClientTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/client/stdio";

// A tool result body parsed from the stdio client's first text content block.
function resultBody(result: { content: Array<{ type: string; text?: string }> }): Record<string, unknown> {
  return JSON.parse(String(
    result.content[0]?.type === "text" ? result.content[0].text : "{}",
  )) as Record<string, unknown>;
}

async function availablePort(): Promise<number> {
  const server = createServer();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once("error", reject);
    server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve);
  });
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw new Error("Could not determine a free test port.");
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.close((error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve());
  });
  return address.port;
}

async function readFileText(relativePath: string): Promise<string> {
  return readFile(new URL(relativePath, import.meta.url), "utf8");
}

test("scheduler accepts optional images and stores text-only rows with no media", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFileText("../src/scheduler.ts");

  assert.match(scheduler, /export async function schedulePosts\(/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /image\?: string \| NewScheduledImage/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /mediaType: document \? "document" : imageInput \? "image" : "none"/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /contentType: imageInput \? contentType : "application\/octet-stream"/);
  // Text-only is rejected for Instagram at scheduling time, before tokens/DB.
  assert.match(scheduler, /if \(!imageInput && post\.platform === "instagram"\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /Instagram requires an image reference; text-only posts are not supported\./);
  // Local tunnel requirement applies only when an image reference is present.
  assert.match(scheduler, /if \(imageInput && config\.scheduledImageStorage === "local"/);
});

test("publishing handles text-only jobs through the existing platform publishers", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFileText("../src/scheduler.ts");
  const meta = await readFileText("../src/platforms/meta.ts");
  const linkedin = await readFileText("../src/platforms/linkedin.ts");

  // The no-image branches call the text-only platform publishers directly.
  assert.match(scheduler, /result = await publishLinkedIn\(job\.userId, job\.text\);/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /result = await publishMeta\(job\.userId, job\.platform as MetaPlatform, job\.text, undefined\);/);
  // Facebook publishes text-only posts to the Page feed; Instagram still needs a URL.
  assert.match(meta, /message: text \?\? ""/);
  assert.match(meta, /Instagram publishing requires a publicly reachable HTTPS image URL\./);
  // LinkedIn text-only posts use the Posts API without media.
  assert.match(linkedin, /export async function publishLinkedIn\(ownerId: string, text\?: string, imagePath\?: string/);
});

test("social_schedule_posts accepts at most one image reference and no image is valid", async () => {
  const register = await readFileText("../src/tools/register.ts");

  assert.match(register, /Provide at most one of imageUrl, imageStoragePath, or imagePath\./);
  assert.doesNotMatch(register, /Provide exactly one of imageUrl, imageStoragePath, or imagePath\./);
  // The image variable may be undefined and is passed through as-is.
  assert.match(register, /let image: string \| NewScheduledImage \| undefined;/);
  assert.match(register, /await schedulePosts\(userId, posts, image, document\)/);
  // Descriptions document the text-only behavior and the Instagram exception.
  assert.match(register, /omit every image reference for a text-only Facebook or LinkedIn post; Instagram always requires an image/);
});

test("built MCP server schedules text-only posts without an image", async () => {
  const projectRoot = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
  const callbackPort = await availablePort();
  const tokenFile = join(await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "social-mcp-text-only-")), "tokens.enc");
  const transport = new StdioClientTransport({
    command: process.execPath,
    args: ["dist/index.js"],
    cwd: projectRoot,
    stderr: "pipe",
    // Deterministic settings: the token vault is an empty temp file, and
    // dotenv never overrides an explicitly exported value, so real .env cloud
    // backends are ignored. DATABASE_URL stays configured because the stdio
    // server initializes the scheduler store at startup; the "not connected"
    // token check below throws before any row would be inserted.
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      OAUTH_CALLBACK_PORT: String(callbackPort),
      SOCIAL_MCP_TOKEN_FILE: tokenFile,
      SUPABASE_URL: "",
      SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY: "",
      TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND: "local",
      SCHEDULED_IMAGE_STORAGE: "local",
      SCHEDULER_MODE: "local",
      OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE: "local",
    },
  });
  const client = new Client({ name: "social-media-mcp-text-only-test", version: "1.0.0" });
  let serverStderr = "";
  transport.stderr?.setEncoding("utf8");
  transport.stderr?.on("data", (chunk) => { serverStderr += chunk; });

  try {
    await client.connect(transport);
    const scheduledAt = new Date(Date.now() + 3_600_000).toISOString();

    // A text-only Facebook post passes image validation and reaches the token
    // check ("not connected"), proving no image is required before scheduling.
    const facebook = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_schedule_posts",
      arguments: {
        posts: [{ platform: "facebook", scheduledAt, text: "Text-only validation" }],
      },
    });
    assert.equal(facebook.isError, undefined);
    const facebookBody = resultBody(facebook);
    assert.equal(facebookBody.success, false);
    assert.match(String(facebookBody.error), /facebook is not connected; authorize it before scheduling/);

    // LinkedIn behaves identically for text-only posts.
    const linkedin = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_schedule_posts",
      arguments: {
        posts: [{ platform: "linkedin", scheduledAt, text: "Text-only validation" }],
      },
    });
    const linkedinBody = resultBody(linkedin);
    assert.equal(linkedinBody.success, false);
    assert.match(String(linkedinBody.error), /linkedin is not connected; authorize it before scheduling/);

    // Instagram keeps its image requirement for scheduled posts.
    const instagram = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_schedule_posts",
      arguments: {
        posts: [{ platform: "instagram", scheduledAt, text: "Text-only validation" }],
      },
    });
    const instagramBody = resultBody(instagram);
    assert.equal(instagramBody.success, false);
    assert.match(String(instagramBody.error), /Instagram requires an image reference/);

    // Two image references are still rejected to catch ambiguous input.
    const ambiguous = await client.callTool({
      name: "social_schedule_posts",
      arguments: {
        posts: [{ platform: "facebook", scheduledAt, text: "Text-only validation" }],
        imageUrl: "https://127.0.0.1:9/unreachable.png",
        imageStoragePath: "scheduled/local-owner/" + "0".repeat(64) + ".png",
      },
    });
    const ambiguousBody = resultBody(ambiguous);
    assert.equal(ambiguousBody.success, false);
    assert.match(String(ambiguousBody.error), /Provide at most one of imageUrl, imageStoragePath, or imagePath\./);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(`stdio test failed: ${String(error)}; server stderr: ${serverStderr}`, { cause: error as Error });
  } finally {
    await client.close();
  }
});

FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/shared-storage-cleanup.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import test from "node:test";

const { dedupeStorageCandidates } = await import("../src/scheduler.js");

// --- Pure helper -------------------------------------------------------------

test("storage cleanup candidates are deduplicated by path keeping the first kind", () => {
  const deduped = dedupeStorageCandidates([
    { kind: "image", path: "scheduled/owner/aaa.jpg" },
    { kind: "video", path: "scheduled/owner/bbb.mp4" },
    { kind: "image", path: "scheduled/owner/aaa.jpg" },
    { kind: "image", path: "" },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(deduped, [
    { kind: "image", path: "scheduled/owner/aaa.jpg" },
    { kind: "video", path: "scheduled/owner/bbb.mp4" },
  ]);
});

// --- Reference-counted deletion (source assertions per project convention) ---

test("cleanup helper counts references in posts and media tables, honoring active statuses", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(scheduler, /post\.image_storage_path = ANY\(\$1::text\[\]\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /post\.document_storage_path = ANY\(\$1::text\[\]\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /media_ref\.storage_path = ANY\(\$1::text\[\]\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /JOIN public\.scheduled_posts AS post ON post\.id = media_ref\.post_id/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /refs\.status IN \('scheduled', 'publishing'\)/);
});

test("cleanup helper is concurrency-safe and fails safe", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  const helper = scheduler.slice(
    scheduler.indexOf("async function deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects"),
    scheduler.indexOf("export async function cancelScheduledPost"),
  );
  assert.match(helper, /BEGIN/);
  assert.match(helper, /COMMIT/);
  assert.match(helper, /pg_advisory_xact_lock/, "per-path advisory locks serialize racing cleanups");
  assert.match(helper, /sort\(\(a, b\) => a\.path\.localeCompare\(b\.path\)\)/, "sorted lock order prevents deadlocks");
  assert.match(helper, /scheduled_shared_object_cleanup_skipped/, "unverifiable references must not delete");
  assert.match(helper, /deleted\.add\(candidate\.path\)/, "per-path outcomes are reported to callers");
  assert.match(helper, /HTTP 404/, "an object Supabase reports as missing still counts as cleaned up");
  const referenceIndex = helper.indexOf("const referenced = new Set");
  const deleteIndex = helper.indexOf("deleteScheduledVideo");
  const beginIndex = helper.indexOf('"BEGIN"');
  assert.ok(beginIndex >= 0 && referenceIndex > beginIndex && deleteIndex > referenceIndex, "deletes happen after the reference count");
});

test("cancel routes shared objects through the reference-counted helper", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  const cancel = scheduler.slice(
    scheduler.indexOf("export async function cancelScheduledPost"),
    scheduler.indexOf("export async function rescheduleScheduledPost"),
  );
  assert.match(cancel, /deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects\(storageObjects\)/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(cancel, /deleteScheduled(Video|Image|Document)\(/, "cancel must never delete unconditionally");
  assert.match(cancel, /unregisterScheduledMedia/, "media token registration is still cleaned up");
  assert.match(cancel, /await unlink\(file\)/, "local files remain directly deleted (never shared)");
  assert.match(cancel, /kind: "document", path: row\.document_storage_path/, "documents join the reference check");
  assert.match(cancel, /WHERE id = \$1 AND user_id = \$2 AND status = 'scheduled'/, "cancel still only applies to unclaimed posts");
});

test("scheduling failure cleanup no longer deletes shared references unconditionally", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.doesNotMatch(scheduler, /deleteScheduledImage\(imageStoragePath\)/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(scheduler, /deleteScheduledDocument\(documentFields\.documentStoragePath\)/);
  const mediaCatch = scheduler.slice(
    scheduler.indexOf("const cleanupCandidates: StorageObjectCandidate[] = ["),
    scheduler.indexOf("throw error;\n  }\n}", scheduler.indexOf("const cleanupCandidates: StorageObjectCandidate[] = [")),
  );
  assert.match(mediaCatch, /deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects\(cleanupCandidates\)/);
  assert.match(scheduler, /Reference-counted cleanup: the row inserts rolled back/);
});

test("publishing still retrieves shared media through the stored permanent paths", async () => {
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(scheduler, /await downloadScheduledImage\(job\.imageStoragePath\)/, "LinkedIn publish download");
  assert.match(scheduler, /await downloadScheduledVideo\(videoItem\.storagePath\)/, "video publish download");
  assert.match(scheduler, /await createScheduledImageUrl\(job\.imageStoragePath\)/, "Meta publish fresh signed URL");
  assert.match(scheduler, /await downloadScheduledDocument\(job\.documentStoragePath\)/, "document publish download");
  assert.doesNotMatch(scheduler, /deleteScheduled.*publishing/, "no publish path deletes storage objects");
});

test("ownership validation and legacy flat paths are preserved", async () => {
  const storage = await readFile(new URL("../src/storage/scheduled-images.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(storage, /export function storageOwnerSegment/);
  assert.match(storage, /export function isOwnedStorageObjectPath/);
  assert.match(storage, /export function assertOwnedStorageObjectPath/);
  const scheduler = await readFile(new URL("../src/scheduler.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.match(scheduler, /assertOwnedStorageObjectPath\(imageInput\.storagePath, ownerId\)/, "scheduling input validation unchanged");
  assert.match(scheduler, /assertOwnedStorageObjectPath\(item\.storagePath, ownerId\)/, "media input validation unchanged");
  const helper = scheduler.slice(
    scheduler.indexOf("async function deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects"),
    scheduler.indexOf("export function dedupeStorageCandidates"),
  );
  const cleanup = scheduler.slice(
    scheduler.indexOf("async function deleteUnreferencedStorageObjects"),
    scheduler.indexOf("export async function cancelScheduledPost"),
  );
  assert.doesNotMatch(cleanup, /isOwnedStorageObjectPath|storageFilePattern/, "deletion keys on the stored path string (legacy paths included)");
  assert.doesNotMatch(helper, /isOwnedStorageObjectPath|storageFilePattern/);
});

FILE: social-media-mcp/tests/validation.test.ts
================================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import test from "node:test";
import {
  platformImageRequirements,
  validateImageUrl,
  validateLinkedInAuthorUrn,
  validatePostContent,
} from "../src/utils/validation.js";

test("requires publicly fetchable HTTPS media URLs", () => {
  assert.equal(validateImageUrl("https://cdn.example.com/image.jpg"), undefined);
  assert.match(validateImageUrl("http://cdn.example.com/image.jpg") ?? "", /HTTPS/);
  assert.match(validateImageUrl("not-a-url") ?? "", /absolute URL/);
});

test("remote image inputs accept only validated HTTPS URLs", () => {
  for (const url of ["https://cdn.example.com/a.png", "https://8.8.8.8/a.jpg"]) {
    assert.equal(validateImageUrl(url), undefined, `${url} should be accepted`);
  }
  for (const url of ["http://cdn.example.com/a.png", "ftp://cdn.example.com/a.png", "file:///etc/passwd"]) {
    assert.match(validateImageUrl(url) ?? "", /HTTPS/, `${url} should be rejected`);
  }
});

test("validates LinkedIn author URNs", () => {
  assert.equal(validateLinkedInAuthorUrn("urn:li:person:abc_123"), undefined);
  assert.equal(validateLinkedInAuthorUrn("urn:li:organization:12345"), undefined);
  assert.match(validateLinkedInAuthorUrn("urn:li:share:123") ?? "", /author/);
});

test("enforces platform image requirements and accepts non-empty text", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("instagram"), [
    "Instagram Graph API feed publishing requires an image reference; text-only posts are not supported.",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("linkedin", "https://example.com/a.jpg"), []);
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("linkedin", "https://example.com/a.jpg", "C:\\a.jpg"), [
    "LinkedIn uses either imagePath or imageUrl, not both.",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(validatePostContent("A test caption"), []);
  assert.deepEqual(validatePostContent(), [
    "Provide post text, an image URL, an image path, or an imageStoragePath.",
  ]);
});

test("permanent storage object paths satisfy platform image requirements exclusively", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("instagram", undefined, undefined, "scheduled/owner/a.jpg"), []);
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("facebook", undefined, undefined, "scheduled/owner/a.jpg"), []);
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("linkedin", undefined, undefined, "scheduled/owner/a.jpg"), []);
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("instagram", "https://example.com/a.jpg", undefined, "scheduled/owner/a.jpg"), [
    "Provide only one image reference: imageUrl, imagePath, or imageStoragePath.",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(platformImageRequirements("facebook", undefined, "C:\\a.jpg", "scheduled/owner/a.jpg"), [
    "Provide only one image reference: imageUrl, imagePath, or imageStoragePath.",
    "Meta publishing uses imageUrl or a storage object path; local image uploads are not implemented for this platform.",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(validatePostContent(undefined, undefined, undefined, "scheduled/owner/a.jpg"), []);
});

# Reuse the backend PostgreSQL connection (also read from ../social-media-mcp/.env).
# DATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres:YOUR_PASSWORD@db.YOUR_PROJECT_REF.supabase.co:5432/postgres?sslmode=require

# Generate with: node -e "console.log(require('node:crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('hex'))"
BETTER_AUTH_SECRET=
BETTER_AUTH_URL=http://localhost:3000
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://localhost:3000

# Used only by scripts/seed-admin.ts to provision the single admin account.
ADMIN_EMAIL=
ADMIN_PASSWORD=
ADMIN_NAME=Administrator

# Public sign-up is enabled by default. Set to false to restrict account creation to scripts/seed-admin.ts.
ALLOW_ADMIN_SIGNUP=true

# --- Backend OAuth configuration (read by the compiled social-media-mcp modules) ---
# The frontend imports the backend from ../social-media-mcp/dist, so these must be
# present in the Vercel environment. Set OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE=cloud for production.
# Only public client IDs are needed here; never put Meta/Instagram/LinkedIn client
# secrets or CLOUD_TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY on Vercel.
# OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE=cloud
# OAUTH_REDIRECT_BASE_URL=https://social-media-cloud-worker.onrender.com
# OAUTH_FRONTEND_BASE_URL=https://YOUR-VERCEL-PROJECT.vercel.app
# META_APP_ID=
# INSTAGRAM_APP_ID=
# LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID=

# --- Remote MCP consent (Render authorization server) ---
# The Vercel app renders the consent screen and creates the one-time code, so it
# must validate the same MCP resource, scope, and redirect-origin allowlist as the
# Render worker. These values are public and must match Render exactly.
# MCP_PUBLIC_URL=https://social-media-cloud-worker.onrender.com/mcp
# MCP_OAUTH_SCOPE=mcp
# MCP_ALLOWED_REDIRECT_ORIGINS=https://claude.ai,https://claude.com
# MCP_ALLOW_LOOPBACK_REDIRECTS=true

# Required on Vercel: POST /api/credentials encrypts each user's Developer App
# secret before it is stored in PostgreSQL. This MUST be the same 32-byte key as
# the Render worker (which decrypts during the OAuth exchange and LinkedIn
# refresh). Generate with:
#   node -e "console.log(require('node:crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('hex'))"
# Never expose it through NEXT_PUBLIC_*, a client bundle, an API response, or logs.
# APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY=

# --- Remote media upload (POST /api/media/upload) ---
# Required when signed-in users need to turn a local image, video, or PDF into a
# durable media reference for remote MCP tools (Claude.ai cannot attach files to
# a remote tool). The endpoint stores the file under a per-user prefix in the
# private `social-media-images` bucket and returns the permanent object path
# (`storagePath`); scheduling tools persist that path and the worker re-downloads
# it at publish time. Server-side only: never expose SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY
# through NEXT_PUBLIC_*, a client bundle, an API response, or logs. The bucket
# must stay private.
# SUPABASE_URL=
# SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=

# Lifetime in seconds of the signed URL the worker creates at publish time (not
# stored; regenerated for every attempt). Default 86400, max 604800. Only needs
# to outlive the destination platform's fetch of the media.
# SCHEDULE_SIGNED_URL_TTL_SECONDS=86400

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "user" (
  "id" text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  "name" text NOT NULL,
  "email" text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  "email_verified" boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  "image" text,
  "created_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  "updated_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "session" (
  "id" text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  "expires_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
  "token" text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  "created_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  "updated_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  "ip_address" text,
  "user_agent" text,
  "user_id" text NOT NULL REFERENCES "user" ("id") ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "account" (
  "id" text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  "account_id" text NOT NULL,
  "provider_id" text NOT NULL,
  "user_id" text NOT NULL REFERENCES "user" ("id") ON DELETE CASCADE,
  "access_token" text,
  "refresh_token" text,
  "id_token" text,
  "access_token_expires_at" timestamp with time zone,
  "refresh_token_expires_at" timestamp with time zone,
  "scope" text,
  "password" text,
  "created_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  "updated_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "verification" (
  "id" text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  "identifier" text NOT NULL,
  "value" text NOT NULL,
  "expires_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
  "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
  "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "session_user_id_idx" ON "session" ("user_id");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "account_user_id_idx" ON "account" ("user_id");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "verification_identifier_idx" ON "verification" ("identifier");

FILE: social-media-web/next.config.ts

import type { NextConfig } from "next";
import path from "node:path";

// The backend lives in the sibling `social-media-mcp` package, outside this
// project's root. Trace from the monorepo root so the serverless bundle
// mirrors the repository layout.
const monorepoRoot = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "..");

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  cacheComponents: false,
  serverExternalPackages: ["pg"],
  // Next's file tracing defaults to the project directory and misses files
  // outside it. Runtime-read files (see below) must be named explicitly.
  outputFileTracingRoot: monorepoRoot,
  // `database-migrations.ts` reads the backend's `migrations/*.sql` files at
  // runtime via a computed path, which Next's static tracing cannot see
  // (Vercel: `ENOENT ... /var/task/social-media-mcp/migrations/001_...sql`).
  // Trace them into every server entry; the files are tiny SQL scripts.
  outputFileTracingIncludes: {
    "/*": [
      // Runtime-read SQL migration scripts (computed path, invisible to tracing).
      "../social-media-mcp/migrations/*.sql",
      // The compiled backend modules: Turbopack keeps `import.meta.url`
      // pointing at `<root>/social-media-mcp/dist/<file>.js`, and some
      // resolvers expect the file itself to exist at runtime.
      "../social-media-mcp/dist/**/*.js",
    ],
  },
  turbopack: {
    // The backend lives in the sibling `social-media-mcp` package and is
    // consumed from its compiled `dist`. Turbopack does not resolve files
    // outside the project root unless the root is widened.
    // Keep this consistent with `outputFileTracingRoot`.
    root: monorepoRoot,
    rules: {
      "*.css": {
        loaders: ["@tailwindcss/turbopack"],
        as: "*.css",
      },
    },
  },
};

export default nextConfig;

FILE: social-media-web/README.md
================================================================================

This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/cli/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

## Environment

Copy `.env.example` to `.env.local` and fill in the values. In addition to the
auth variables, the OAuth/worker modules read `DATABASE_URL`,
`OAUTH_CALLBACK_MODE=cloud`, `OAUTH_REDIRECT_BASE_URL`, `OAUTH_FRONTEND_BASE_URL`,
and the public `META_APP_ID` / `INSTAGRAM_APP_ID` / `LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID` values from
the Vercel environment.

`APP_CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY` is also required on Vercel: `POST /api/credentials`
encrypts each signed-in user's Developer App secret before it is stored. Use the
same 64-character hex value as the Render worker, generate it with
`node -e "console.log(require('node:crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('hex'))"`,
and never expose it through `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`, a client bundle, an API response, or
logs. Users can save, update, and delete their Facebook, Instagram, and LinkedIn
app credentials from **Accounts → Developer App Settings**; deleting them never
disconnects an account or cancels a scheduled post.

## Authenticated media upload

Remote MCP tools (for example the Claude.ai connector) receive JSON arguments,
not uploaded files, so a local image, video, or PDF must first become a durable
media reference. Signed-in users can upload one from the **Media** page (which
posts to `POST /api/media/upload`, multipart `file` field). The endpoint
validates the type and signature, stores
the file under a per-user prefix in the private `social-media-images` Supabase
bucket, and returns:

- `storagePath` — the permanent object path. Pass this as `imageStoragePath`,
  `media[].storagePath`, or `documentStoragePath` when scheduling (or to
  `social_publish.imageStoragePath` for immediate publishing). It never
  expires and is what PostgreSQL records.
- `url` — a short-lived signed URL for an immediate publish only. Do not store
  it.

This page is also the browser fallback for Claude.ai: the MCP spec has no
client→server file-upload primitive, so when the in-chat upload tool
(`social_upload_media_intent` on the MCP server) cannot run in the model's
environment, the user uploads here and pastes the returned `storagePath` into
the Claude conversation.

At publish time the worker downloads `storagePath` with the server-side
service-role key and creates a fresh signed URL only for platforms that require
a public HTTPS URL (Instagram/Facebook). Platforms with direct upload (LinkedIn)
receive the bytes directly. Retries re-download the same original object, so a
post scheduled days ahead is unaffected by any earlier URL's expiry.

This route needs `SUPABASE_URL` and `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` on the server.
Keep the service-role key out of `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`, the browser, API responses, and
logs. The bucket stays private; the response never contains a storage
credential. `SCHEDULE_SIGNED_URL_TTL_SECONDS` (default `86400`) controls the
publish-time signed URL lifetime.

FILE: social-media-web/scripts/migrate.mjs
================================================================================

import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { Client } from "pg";

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");

async function loadEnv() {
  const candidates = [
    path.join(root, ".env.local"),
    path.join(root, ".env"),
    path.join(root, "..", "social-media-mcp", ".env"),
  ];
  for (const file of candidates) {
    if (!existsSync(file)) continue;
    const text = await readFile(file, "utf8");
    for (const line of text.split(/\r?\n/)) {
      const match = /^\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*)\s*$/.exec(line);
      if (!match || process.env[match[1]] !== undefined) continue;
      process.env[match[1]] = match[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
    }
  }
}

await loadEnv();

const connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL?.trim();
if (!connectionString) {
  console.error("Set DATABASE_URL (web .env.local or social-media-mcp/.env) before running migrations.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const directory = path.join(root, "migrations");
const files = (await readdir(directory)).filter((file) => file.endsWith(".sql")).sort();

const client = new Client({ connectionString });
await client.connect();
try {
  for (const file of files) {
    const sql = await readFile(path.join(directory, file), "utf8");
    await client.query("begin");
    try {
      await client.query(sql);
      await client.query("commit");
      console.log(`Applied ${file}`);
    } catch (error) {
      await client.query("rollback");
      throw error;
    }
  }
} finally {
  await client.end();
}

FILE: social-media-web/scripts/seed-admin.mts
================================================================================

import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");

for (const file of [
  path.join(root, ".env.local"),
  path.join(root, ".env"),
  path.join(root, "..", "social-media-mcp", ".env"),
]) {
  if (!existsSync(file)) continue;
  const text = await readFile(file, "utf8");
  for (const line of text.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const match = /^\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*)\s*$/.exec(line);
    if (!match || process.env[match[1]] !== undefined) continue;
    process.env[match[1]] = match[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
  }
}

process.env.ALLOW_ADMIN_SIGNUP = "true";

const email = process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL?.trim();
const password = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD;
const adminName = process.env.ADMIN_NAME?.trim() || "Administrator";

if (!email || !password) {
  console.error("Set ADMIN_EMAIL and ADMIN_PASSWORD before seeding the admin account.");
  process.exit(1);
}
if (password.length < 8) {
  console.error("ADMIN_PASSWORD must be at least 8 characters.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const { auth } = await import("../src/lib/auth");

const alreadyValid = await auth.api
  .signInEmail({ body: { email, password } })
  .then(() => true)
  .catch(() => false);

if (alreadyValid) {
  console.log(`Admin ${email} already exists and the password matches.`);
  process.exit(0);
}

try {
  await auth.api.signUpEmail({ body: { email, password, name: adminName } });
  console.log(`Created admin ${email}.`);
} catch (error) {
  console.error("Could not create the admin account:");
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
  process.exit(1);
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(auth)/login/login-form.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { authClient } from "@/lib/auth-client";

export function LoginForm({ next }: { next: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPending(true);
    setError(null);

    const { error: signInError } = await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password });
    if (signInError) {
      setError(signInError.message ?? "Sign in failed. Check your credentials.");
      setPending(false);
      return;
    }

    router.replace(next);
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-sm">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>Sign in</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>Administrator access to the social media console.</CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form className="flex flex-col gap-4" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="username"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              required
              value={password}
              onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          {error ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
          <Button type="submit" disabled={pending}>
            {pending ? "Signing in..." : "Sign in"}
          </Button>
          <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Don&apos;t have an account?{" "}
            <Link
              className="underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-foreground"
              href={next === "/" ? "/signup" : `/signup?next=${encodeURIComponent(next)}`}
            >
              Sign up
            </Link>
          </p>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(auth)/signup/signup-form.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { authClient } from "@/lib/auth-client";

export function SignupForm({ next }: { next: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPending(true);
    setError(null);

    const { error: signUpError } = await authClient.signUp.email({ name, email, password });
    if (signUpError) {
      setError(signUpError.message ?? "Sign up failed. Try a different email.");
      setPending(false);
      return;
    }

    router.replace(next);
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <Card className="w-full max-w-sm">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>Create account</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>Set up access to the social media console.</CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form className="flex flex-col gap-4" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="name">Name</Label>
            <Input
              id="name"
              type="text"
              autoComplete="name"
              required
              value={name}
              onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="username"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              minLength={8}
              required
              value={password}
              onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          {error ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p> : null}
          <Button type="submit" disabled={pending}>
            {pending ? "Creating account..." : "Sign up"}
          </Button>
          <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Already have an account?{" "}
            <Link
              className="underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-foreground"
              href={next === "/" ? "/login" : `/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(next)}`}
            >
              Sign in
            </Link>
          </p>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(dashboard)/accounts/accounts-panel.tsx

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";

type PlatformId = "facebook" | "instagram" | "linkedin";

type PlatformStatus = PlatformStatusResult & { platform: PlatformId };

interface PlatformStatusResult {
  connected?: boolean;
  reason?: string;
  accountName?: string;
  grantedPermissions?: string[];
  grantedScopes?: string[];
  missingPermissions?: string[];
  expiresAt?: number;
  tokenExpired?: boolean;
}

type AuthSessionStatus = "pending" | "connected" | "cancelled" | "failed" | "expired";

const PLATFORM_LABELS: Record<PlatformId, string> = {
  facebook: "Facebook",
  instagram: "Instagram",
  linkedin: "LinkedIn",
};

const MAX_WAIT_LOOPS = 18;

export function AccountsPanel() {
  const [statuses, setStatuses] = useState<PlatformStatus[] | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<PlatformId | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    return fetch("/api/platforms", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
          platforms?: PlatformStatus[];
          error?: string;
        };
        if (!response.ok) {
          toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not load account status.");
          return;
        }
        setStatuses(body.platforms ?? []);
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load account status.");
      });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  // The provider redirects the OAuth tab back to a fixed /accounts page. Surface
  // the coarse result and refresh the connection list even if the original tab's
  // polling already finished.
  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const oauth = params.get("oauth");
    if (!oauth) return;
    const platform = params.get("platform");
    const label = platform && platform in PLATFORM_LABELS
      ? PLATFORM_LABELS[platform as PlatformId]
      : "Social account";
    if (oauth === "connected") toast.success(`${label} connected.`);
    else if (oauth === "cancelled") toast.error(`${label} connection was cancelled.`);
    else if (oauth === "expired") toast.error(`${label} connection request expired. Try again.`);
    else toast.error(`${label} connection failed. Try again.`);
    const clean = new URL(window.location.href);
    clean.searchParams.delete("oauth");
    clean.searchParams.delete("platform");
    window.history.replaceState({}, "", `${clean.pathname}${clean.search}${clean.hash}`);
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const reconnect = useCallback(
    async (platform: PlatformId) => {
      setBusy(platform);
      setMessage(null);
      try {
        const startResponse = await fetch("/api/platforms/authorize", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ platform }),
        });
        const startBody = (await startResponse.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
          authorizationUrl?: string;
          sessionId?: string;
          error?: string;
        };
        if (!startResponse.ok || !startBody.authorizationUrl || !startBody.sessionId) {
          toast.error(startBody.error ?? "Could not start authorization.");
          return;
        }

        window.open(startBody.authorizationUrl, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
        setMessage(
          `Complete the ${PLATFORM_LABELS[platform]} sign-in in the new tab, then approve here.`,
        );

        let finalStatus: AuthSessionStatus | null = null;
        let finalError: string | undefined;
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_WAIT_LOOPS; attempt += 1) {
          const waitResponse = await fetch("/api/platforms/authorize/wait", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ sessionId: startBody.sessionId, timeoutSeconds: 5 }),
          });
          const waitBody = (await waitResponse.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
            status?: { status?: AuthSessionStatus; error?: string };
            error?: string;
          };
          if (!waitResponse.ok) {
            setMessage(waitBody.error ?? "Authorization wait failed.");
            return;
          }
          const status = waitBody.status?.status;
          if (status && status !== "pending") {
            finalStatus = status;
            finalError = waitBody.status?.error;
            break;
          }
        }

        if (finalStatus === "connected") {
          toast.success(`${PLATFORM_LABELS[platform]} reconnected.`);
          setMessage(null);
        } else if (finalStatus) {
          toast.error(
            finalError ?? `Authorization ended as "${finalStatus}".`,
          );
        } else {
          setMessage("Authorization did not finish in time. Approve the sign-in in the opened tab, or try again.");
        }
        await load();
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Reconnect failed.");
      } finally {
        setBusy(null);
      }
    },
    [load],
  );

  if (statuses === null) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <Skeleton className="h-24 w-full" />
        <Skeleton className="h-24 w-full" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        Reconnecting opens the provider sign-in in a new tab. Approve the requested
        permissions there; this page updates automatically and you can refresh the
        connection list if it does not.
      </p>
      {message ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{message}</p> : null}

      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {statuses.map((status) => (
          <Card key={status.platform}>
            <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <CardTitle>{PLATFORM_LABELS[status.platform]}</CardTitle>
                <CardDescription>{status.accountName ?? "Provider account"}</CardDescription>
              </div>
              <Badge variant={status.connected ? "default" : "destructive"}>
                {status.connected ? "Connected" : "Disconnected"}
              </Badge>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              {!status.connected && status.reason ? (
                <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{status.reason}</p>
              ) : null}
              {status.expiresAt ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {status.tokenExpired ? "Token expired" : "Token valid"} ·{" "}
                  {new Date(status.expiresAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                </p>
              ) : null}
              {status.missingPermissions && status.missingPermissions.length > 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-destructive">
                  Missing: {status.missingPermissions.join(", ")}
                </p>
              ) : null}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                {(status.grantedScopes ?? status.grantedPermissions ?? []).slice(0, 6).map(
                  (permission) => (
                    <Badge key={permission} variant="outline" className="text-[10px]">
                      {permission}
                    </Badge>
                  ),
                )}
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                disabled={busy !== null}
                onClick={() => void reconnect(status.platform)}
              >
                {busy === status.platform ? "Waiting for sign-in..." : "Reconnect"}
              </Button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(dashboard)/accounts/developer-app-settings.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";

type ProviderId = "facebook" | "instagram" | "linkedin";

interface CredentialStatus {
  provider: ProviderId;
  configured: boolean;
  source: "user" | "shared";
  clientId?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

interface ProviderMeta {
  label: string;
  idLabel: string;
  secretLabel: string;
}

// Facebook, Instagram and LinkedIn each issue their own app/client id, so the
// user configures one record per provider.
const PROVIDERS: Record<ProviderId, ProviderMeta> = {
  facebook: { label: "Facebook (Meta App)", idLabel: "App ID", secretLabel: "App Secret" },
  instagram: { label: "Instagram (Instagram App)", idLabel: "App ID", secretLabel: "App Secret" },
  linkedin: { label: "LinkedIn", idLabel: "Client ID", secretLabel: "Client Secret" },
};

const PROVIDER_ORDER: ProviderId[] = ["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"];

interface FormState {
  clientId: string;
  clientSecret: string;
}

function emptyForms(): Record<ProviderId, FormState> {
  return {
    facebook: { clientId: "", clientSecret: "" },
    instagram: { clientId: "", clientSecret: "" },
    linkedin: { clientId: "", clientSecret: "" },
  };
}

export function DeveloperAppSettings() {
  const [statuses, setStatuses] = useState<CredentialStatus[] | null>(null);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(true);
  const [forms, setForms] = useState<Record<ProviderId, FormState>>(emptyForms);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<ProviderId | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    return fetch("/api/credentials", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
          active?: boolean;
          credentials?: CredentialStatus[];
          error?: string;
        };
        if (!response.ok) {
          toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not load developer app settings.");
          setStatuses([]);
          return;
        }
        setActive(body.active ?? true);
        const credentials = body.credentials ?? [];
        setStatuses(credentials);
        // Prefill the (public) client id so an update only needs a changed value;
        // the secret is never returned and stays blank unless the user types one.
        setForms((current) => {
          const next = { ...current };
          for (const entry of credentials) {
            next[entry.provider] = {
              clientId: entry.clientId ?? current[entry.provider]?.clientId ?? "",
              clientSecret: "",
            };
          }
          return next;
        });
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load developer app settings.");
        setStatuses([]);
      });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const configuredFor = (provider: ProviderId) =>
    statuses?.find((entry) => entry.provider === provider)?.configured ?? false;

  const save = useCallback(
    async (provider: ProviderId) => {
      const form = forms[provider];
      setBusy(provider);
      try {
        const response = await fetch("/api/credentials", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            provider,
            clientId: form.clientId.trim(),
            // Omit the secret when the field is blank so the stored one is kept.
            ...(form.clientSecret.trim() ? { clientSecret: form.clientSecret.trim() } : {}),
          }),
        });
        const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
        if (!response.ok) {
          toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not save credentials.");
          return;
        }
        toast.success(`${PROVIDERS[provider].label} credentials saved.`);
        await load();
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not save credentials.");
      } finally {
        setBusy(null);
      }
    },
    [forms, load],
  );

  const remove = useCallback(
    async (provider: ProviderId) => {
      setBusy(provider);
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/credentials?provider=${provider}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
        if (!response.ok) {
          toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not delete credentials.");
          return;
        }
        // Deleting app credentials never disconnects the account or removes
        // scheduled posts; the account simply falls back to the shared app.
        toast.success(`${PROVIDERS[provider].label} credentials removed. Connected accounts were not changed.`);
        setForms((current) => ({ ...current, [provider]: { clientId: "", clientSecret: "" } }));
        await load();
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not delete credentials.");
      } finally {
        setBusy(null);
      }
    },
    [load],
  );

  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Developer App Settings</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Connect through your own Meta, Instagram, and LinkedIn apps. Accounts without their
          own credentials keep using the shared app. App secrets are encrypted at rest and are
          never shown again after saving.
        </p>
      </div>

      {!active ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          You can save credentials when the platform is running against the cloud backend.
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {statuses === null ? (
        <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full" />
          <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full" />
          <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full" />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {PROVIDER_ORDER.map((provider) => {
            const meta = PROVIDERS[provider];
            const configured = configuredFor(provider);
            const form = forms[provider];
            return (
              <Card key={provider}>
                <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <CardTitle>{meta.label}</CardTitle>
                    <CardDescription>
                      {configured ? "Using your app" : "Using the shared app"}
                    </CardDescription>
                  </div>
                  <Badge variant={configured ? "default" : "secondary"}>
                    {configured ? "Your app" : "Shared app"}
                  </Badge>
                </CardHeader>
                <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                    <Label htmlFor={`${provider}-client-id`}>{meta.idLabel}</Label>
                    <Input
                      id={`${provider}-client-id`}
                      value={form.clientId}
                      autoComplete="off"
                      spellCheck={false}
                      disabled={!active || busy !== null}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setForms((current) => ({
                          ...current,
                          [provider]: { ...current[provider], clientId: event.target.value },
                        }))
                      }
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                    <Label htmlFor={`${provider}-client-secret`}>{meta.secretLabel}</Label>
                    <Input
                      id={`${provider}-client-secret`}
                      type="password"
                      value={form.clientSecret}
                      autoComplete="off"
                      spellCheck={false}
                      disabled={!active || busy !== null}
                      placeholder={configured ? "Leave blank to keep the saved secret" : ""}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setForms((current) => ({
                          ...current,
                          [provider]: { ...current[provider], clientSecret: event.target.value },
                        }))
                      }
                    />
                  </div>
                  {configured && configuredStatus(statuses, provider)?.updatedAt ? (
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      Updated {new Date(configuredStatus(statuses, provider)!.updatedAt!).toLocaleDateString()}
                    </p>
                  ) : null}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <Button
                      size="sm"
                      disabled={!active || busy !== null || !form.clientId.trim()}
                      onClick={() => void save(provider)}
                    >
                      {busy === provider ? "Saving..." : configured ? "Update" : "Save"}
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      disabled={!active || busy !== null || !configured}
                      onClick={() => void remove(provider)}
                    >
                      Delete
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function configuredStatus(
  statuses: CredentialStatus[] | null,
  provider: ProviderId,
): CredentialStatus | undefined {
  return statuses?.find((entry) => entry.provider === provider);
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(dashboard)/calendar/calendar-panel.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Calendar } from "@/components/ui/calendar";

export interface CalendarPost {
  id: string;
  platform: "facebook" | "instagram" | "linkedin";
  status: "scheduled" | "publishing" | "published" | "failed" | "cancelled";
  mediaType: string;
  text: string;
  when: string;
}

const PLATFORM_LABELS: Record<CalendarPost["platform"], string> = {
  facebook: "Facebook",
  instagram: "Instagram",
  linkedin: "LinkedIn",
};

function dayKey(date: Date): number {
  return date.getFullYear() * 10_000 + (date.getMonth() + 1) * 100 + date.getDate();
}

export function CalendarPanel({ posts }: { posts: CalendarPost[] }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Date | undefined>(undefined);

  const entries = useMemo(
    () =>
      posts.map((post) => ({
        post,
        date: new Date(post.when),
      })),
    [posts],
  );

  const scheduledDates = useMemo(() => entries.map((entry) => entry.date), [entries]);
  const selectedKey = selected ? dayKey(selected) : null;
  const selectedPosts = selectedKey === null
    ? []
    : entries.filter((entry) => dayKey(entry.date) === selectedKey);

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Schedule</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Days with scheduled or published posts are marked.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <Calendar
            mode="single"
            selected={selected}
            onSelect={setSelected}
            modifiers={{ scheduled: scheduledDates }}
            modifiersClassNames={{ scheduled: "bg-primary/15 font-semibold" }}
            className="rounded-md border"
          />
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>{selected ? selected.toDateString() : "Select a day"}</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            {selected
              ? `${selectedPosts.length} post(s) on this day.`
              : "Pick a marked day to see its posts."}
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {selectedPosts.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nothing on this day.</p>
          ) : (
            selectedPosts.map(({ post }) => (
              <div key={post.id} className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b pb-3 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium">
                    {PLATFORM_LABELS[post.platform]}
                  </span>
                  <span className="line-clamp-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    {post.text || "—"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {new Date(post.when).toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
                  <Badge variant="secondary">{post.mediaType}</Badge>
                  <Badge variant="outline">{post.status}</Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
