import { CalendarPanel, type CalendarPost } from "./calendar-panel";
import { scheduledPostStatus } from "@/lib/backend";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/session";

export default async function CalendarPage() {
  const session = await requireAdmin();
  const posts = await scheduledPostStatus(session.user.id);

  const entries: CalendarPost[] = posts
    .filter((post) => post.status !== "cancelled")
    .map((post) => ({
      id: post.id,
      platform: post.platform,
      status: post.status,
      mediaType: post.mediaType,
      text: post.text,
      when: post.publishedAt ?? post.scheduledAt,
    }));

  return (
    <>
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Calendar</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Scheduled and published posts across the month.
        </p>
      </div>
      <CalendarPanel posts={entries} />
    </>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(dashboard)/compose/composer.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";

const PLATFORMS = [
  { id: "facebook", label: "Facebook" },
  { id: "instagram", label: "Instagram" },
  { id: "linkedin", label: "LinkedIn" },
] as const;

type PlatformId = (typeof PLATFORMS)[number]["id"];

const TEXT_LIMITS: Record<PlatformId, number> = {
  instagram: 2200,
  facebook: 63206,
  linkedin: 3000,
};

const ACCEPT = "image/jpeg,image/png,image/gif,video/mp4,video/quicktime";
const MAX_ITEMS = 10;

type MediaKind = "image" | "video";

function kindFor(file: File): MediaKind {
  if (file.type.startsWith("image/")) return "image";
  if (file.type.startsWith("video/")) return "video";
  throw new Error(`Unsupported file type "${file.type || file.name}".`);
}

function localDateTimeDefault(offsetMinutes: number): string {
  const date = new Date(Date.now() + offsetMinutes * 60_000);
  const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}T${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

export function Composer() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [platforms, setPlatforms] = useState<PlatformId[]>(["instagram"]);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [scheduledAt, setScheduledAt] = useState(() => localDateTimeDefault(60));
  const [publishNow, setPublishNow] = useState(false);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [previewUrls, setPreviewUrls] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  const previews = useMemo(
    () =>
      files.map((file, index) => ({
        file,
        url: previewUrls[index],
        kind: kindFor(file),
      })),
    [files, previewUrls],
  );

  const imageCount = previews.filter((item) => item.kind === "image").length;
  const videoCount = previews.filter((item) => item.kind === "video").length;
  const isCarousel = imageCount >= 2 && videoCount === 0;

  const selectedLimit = platforms.reduce(
    (limit, id) => Math.min(limit, TEXT_LIMITS[id]),
    Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
  );

  const issues: string[] = [];
  if (platforms.length === 0) issues.push("Select at least one platform.");
  if (!text.trim()) issues.push("Add post text.");
  if (text.length > selectedLimit) {
    issues.push(`Text exceeds the ${selectedLimit}-character limit for the selected platforms.`);
  }
  if (files.length > MAX_ITEMS) issues.push(`Choose at most ${MAX_ITEMS} media items.`);
  if (videoCount > 0 && imageCount > 0) {
    issues.push("Use either a single video or only images.");
  }
  if (platforms.includes("instagram") && files.length === 0) {
    issues.push("Instagram requires at least one image or video.");
  }
  if (isCarousel) {
    if (!platforms.includes("instagram") || platforms.length > 1) {
      issues.push("Carousels can only be published to Instagram.");
    }
  }
  if (!publishNow && !scheduledAt) issues.push("Choose a schedule time.");

  function togglePlatform(id: PlatformId) {
    setPlatforms((current) =>
      current.includes(id) ? current.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...current, id],
    );
  }

  function onFiles(next: FileList | null) {
    if (!next) return;
    const incoming = Array.from(next);
    try {
      for (const file of incoming) kindFor(file);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unsupported file.");
      return;
    }

    const combined = [...files, ...incoming].slice(0, MAX_ITEMS);
    setFiles(combined);
    setPreviewUrls((current) => {
      current.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
      return combined.map((file) => URL.createObjectURL(file));
    });
    if (inputRef.current) inputRef.current.value = "";
  }

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (issues.length > 0 || pending) return;

    setPending(true);
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      platforms.forEach((platform) => form.append("platforms", platform));
      form.append("text", text.trim());
      form.append("publishNow", publishNow ? "true" : "false");
      if (!publishNow) {
        form.append("scheduledAt", new Date(scheduledAt).toISOString());
      }
      files.forEach((file) => form.append("media", file));

      const response = await fetch("/api/posts", { method: "POST", body: form });
      const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        error?: string;
        scheduled?: unknown[];
      };

      if (!response.ok) {
        toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not schedule the post.");
        return;
      }

      toast.success(
        publishNow
          ? "Queued for immediate publishing."
          : `Scheduled ${Array.isArray(body.scheduled) ? body.scheduled.length : 1} post(s).`,
      );
      router.push("/queue");
      router.refresh();
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className="flex flex-col gap-6" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Post</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            Choose platforms, write the caption, and attach media.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label>Platforms</Label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {PLATFORMS.map((platform) => {
                const active = platforms.includes(platform.id);
                return (
                  <Button
                    key={platform.id}
                    type="button"
                    variant={active ? "default" : "outline"}
                    size="sm"
                    aria-pressed={active}
                    onClick={() => togglePlatform(platform.id)}
                  >
                    {platform.label}
                  </Button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <Label htmlFor="text">Caption</Label>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {text.length} / {selectedLimit}
              </span>
            </div>
            <Textarea
              id="text"
              rows={6}
              value={text}
              onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Write your caption..."
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="media">Media</Label>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              JPEG, PNG, or GIF images (2–10 for an Instagram carousel), or a single MP4/MOV
              video up to 50 MB. Instagram needs at least one item.
            </p>
            <Input
              id="media"
              ref={inputRef}
              type="file"
              accept={ACCEPT}
              multiple
              onChange={(event) => onFiles(event.target.files)}
            />
            {previews.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 pt-2">
                {previews.map((item, index) => (
                  <figure key={`${item.file.name}-${index}`} className="w-24">
                    <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-md border">
                      {item.kind === "image" && item.url ? (
                        // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                        <img
                          src={item.url}
                          alt={item.file.name}
                          className="aspect-square w-full object-cover"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <video
                          src={item.url}
                          className="aspect-square w-full object-cover"
                          muted
                          playsInline
                        />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <figcaption className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                      <span className="truncate text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
                        {item.file.name}
                      </span>
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-[9px]">
                        {item.kind}
                      </Badge>
                    </figcaption>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-destructive hover:underline"
                      onClick={() => {
                        URL.revokeObjectURL(item.url);
                        setFiles((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
                        setPreviewUrls((current) =>
                          current.filter((_, i) => i !== index),
                        );
                      }}
                    >
                      Remove
                    </button>
                  </figure>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Timing</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            Publish immediately, or schedule for a future time.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant={publishNow ? "default" : "outline"}
              size="sm"
              aria-pressed={publishNow}
              onClick={() => setPublishNow(true)}
            >
              Publish now
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant={!publishNow ? "default" : "outline"}
              size="sm"
              aria-pressed={!publishNow}
              onClick={() => setPublishNow(false)}
            >
              Schedule
            </Button>
          </div>

          {!publishNow ? (
            <div className="flex max-w-xs flex-col gap-2">
              <Label htmlFor="scheduledAt">Publish at</Label>
              <Input
                id="scheduledAt"
                type="datetime-local"
                value={scheduledAt}
                min={localDateTimeDefault(1)}
                onChange={(event) => setScheduledAt(event.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              The post is queued now and published by the worker within seconds.
            </p>
          )}

          {issues.length > 0 ? (
            <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-destructive">
              {issues.map((issue) => (
                <li key={issue}>{issue}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}

          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button type="submit" disabled={issues.length > 0 || pending}>
              {pending ? "Submitting..." : publishNow ? "Publish now" : "Schedule"}
            </Button>
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => router.push("/")}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </form>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(dashboard)/media/media-uploader.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

// Browser fallback for remote MCP clients that cannot transfer attachment
// bytes. The published MCP spec has no client-to-server file-upload primitive,
// so Claude.ai cannot hand an attached image's binary to a remote MCP tool. A
// signed-in user uploads the file here; the response's permanent storagePath
// can then be pasted back into the Claude conversation and passed to
// social_publish (imageStoragePath) or social_schedule_media
// (media[].storagePath). The path never expires; signed URLs are never stored.

const ACCEPT = "image/jpeg,image/png,image/gif,video/mp4,video/quicktime,application/pdf";

interface UploadResult {
  storagePath: string;
  kind: string;
  contentType: string;
  bytes: number;
  note?: string;
}

export function MediaUploader() {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [fileLabel, setFileLabel] = useState("");
  const [result, setResult] = useState<UploadResult | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  function onFileChange(event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    setFileLabel(file?.name ?? "");
    setResult(null);
  }

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const file = inputRef.current?.files?.[0];
    if (!file || pending) return;

    setPending(true);
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", file);
      const response = await fetch("/api/media/upload", { method: "POST", body: form });
      const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as UploadResult & { error?: string };
      if (!response.ok || !body.storagePath) {
        toast.error(body.error ?? "Upload failed.");
        return;
      }
      setResult(body);
      toast.success("Stored in private media storage.");
    } catch {
      toast.error("Upload failed. Check the file type and size, then try again.");
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
      if (inputRef.current) inputRef.current.value = "";
      setFileLabel("");
    }
  }

  async function copyStoragePath() {
    if (!result?.storagePath) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(result.storagePath);
      toast.success("storagePath copied.");
    } catch {
      toast.error("Copy failed. Select the path text and copy it manually.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>Upload media</CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          JPEG, PNG, or GIF images; MP4 or MOV videos; PDF documents. Files are stored under your
          account in a private bucket.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="media-file">File</Label>
            <Input
              id="media-file"
              ref={inputRef}
              type="file"
              accept={ACCEPT}
              onChange={onFileChange}
              disabled={pending}
            />
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" disabled={pending || !fileLabel} className="w-fit">
            {pending ? "Uploading…" : "Upload"}
          </Button>
        </form>
        {result && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-md border bg-muted/40 p-3">
            <div className="text-sm font-medium">
              {result.kind} · {result.contentType} · {(result.bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
              <code className="min-w-0 flex-1 break-all rounded bg-muted px-2 py-1 text-xs">
                {result.storagePath}
              </code>
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={copyStoragePath}>
                Copy
              </Button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Share this path in Claude and say: “Publish/schedule this image”. In the MCP tools it
              goes to <code>imageStoragePath</code> (social_publish) or{" "}
              <code>media[].storagePath</code> / <code>documentStoragePath</code> (social_schedule_media,
              social_schedule_posts). The path is permanent — signed URLs are generated fresh at
              publish time and must never be stored.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/(dashboard)/page.tsx

import { CalendarClock, CheckCircle2, CircleAlert, Send } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { scheduledPostStatus } from "@/lib/backend";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/session";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const PLATFORM_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  facebook: "Facebook",
  instagram: "Instagram",
  linkedin: "LinkedIn",
};

export default async function OverviewPage() {
  const session = await requireAdmin();
  const posts = await scheduledPostStatus(session.user.id);

  const scheduled = posts.filter((post) => post.status === "scheduled").length;
  const published = posts.filter((post) => post.status === "published").length;
  const failed = posts.filter((post) => post.status === "failed").length;

  const stats: {
    label: string;
    value: number;
    icon: typeof CalendarClock;
    href?: string;
  }[] = [
    { label: "Scheduled", value: scheduled, icon: CalendarClock, href: "/queue" },
    { label: "Published", value: published, icon: CheckCircle2, href: "/history" },
    { label: "Failed", value: failed, icon: CircleAlert, href: "/history" },
    { label: "Total", value: posts.length, icon: Send, href: "/history" },
  ];

  const upcoming = posts
    .filter((post) => post.status === "scheduled")
    .sort((a, b) => a.scheduledAt.localeCompare(b.scheduledAt))
    .slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <>
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Overview</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Delivery status across connected platforms.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map((stat) => {
          const contents = (
            <>
              <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <CardDescription>{stat.label}</CardDescription>
                <stat.icon className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <span className="text-3xl font-semibold">{stat.value}</span>
              </CardContent>
            </>
          );

          if (stat.href) {
            return (
              <Link
                key={stat.label}
                href={stat.href}
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl outline-none transition-colors hover:bg-accent/50 focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50"
              >
                <Card className="h-full">{contents}</Card>
              </Link>
            );
          }

          return <Card key={stat.label}>{contents}</Card>;
        })}
      </div>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Upcoming</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Next scheduled posts due to publish.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {upcoming.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nothing scheduled.</p>
          ) : (
            upcoming.map((post) => (
              <div
                key={post.id}
                className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b pb-3 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium">
                    {PLATFORM_LABELS[post.platform] ?? post.platform}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {new Date(post.scheduledAt).toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <Badge variant="secondary">{post.mediaType}</Badge>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/api/credentials/route.ts
================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  appCredentialStatuses,
  deleteAppCredentials,
  saveAppCredentials,
  userAppCredentialsActive,
  validateAppCredentialInput,
} from "@/lib/backend";

const providerEnum = z.enum(["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"]);

// Returns only safe metadata: the (public) client/app id for the user's own
// record and whether they are on the shared fallback. The secret is never read
// here and never leaves the database in plaintext.
export async function GET() {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const credentials = await appCredentialStatuses(session.user.id);
    return Response.json({ active: userAppCredentialsActive(), credentials });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

const saveSchema = z.object({
  provider: providerEnum,
  clientId: z.string(),
  // Optional so a blank field preserves the stored secret on an update.
  clientSecret: z.string().optional(),
});

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    if (!userAppCredentialsActive()) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "User-owned developer app credentials require the cloud backend (TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND=supabase).",
        409,
      );
    }
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const parsed = saveSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!parsed.success) throw new ApiError("Invalid developer app credentials.");
    const { provider, clientId, clientSecret } = parsed.data;

    const issue = validateAppCredentialInput(provider, clientId, clientSecret, false);
    if (issue) throw new ApiError(issue);

    const configured = (await appCredentialStatuses(session.user.id))
      .find((entry) => entry.provider === provider)?.configured ?? false;
    if (!configured && !(clientSecret ?? "").trim()) {
      throw new ApiError("Enter the app/client secret to save this credential.");
    }

    await saveAppCredentials(session.user.id, provider, { clientId, clientSecret });
    return Response.json({ credentials: await appCredentialStatuses(session.user.id) });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    if (!userAppCredentialsActive()) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "User-owned developer app credentials require the cloud backend (TOKEN_STORE_BACKEND=supabase).",
        409,
      );
    }
    const parsed = providerEnum.safeParse(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("provider"));
    if (!parsed.success) throw new ApiError("Invalid provider.");

    // Removing the app configuration never touches stored social tokens or
    // scheduled posts; the user simply falls back to the shared app.
    await deleteAppCredentials(session.user.id, parsed.data);
    return Response.json({ credentials: await appCredentialStatuses(session.user.id) });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-web/src/app/api/mcp/oauth/authorize/route.ts
================================================================================

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  McpOAuthError,
  createMcpAuthorizationCode,
  getMcpClient,
  isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri,
  validateMcpAuthorizationRequest,
} from "@/lib/backend";

// Consent approval endpoint. The Render worker redirects the browser here with
// an already validated OAuth authorization request. This route re-validates the
// request against the registered client, binds the one-time code to the signed-in
// better-auth user, and then redirects back to the client's registered callback.

function field(form: FormData, name: string): string | undefined {
  const value = form.get(name);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : undefined;
}

function sameOrigin(request: Request): boolean {
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin");
  if (!origin) return false;
  try {
    return new URL(origin).origin === new URL(request.url).origin;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function redirectResponse(location: URL): Response {
  return new Response(null, {
    status: 303,
    headers: {
      location: location.toString(),
      "cache-control": "no-store",
    },
  });
}

function authorizationErrorRedirect(
  redirectUri: string,
  state: string | undefined,
  code: string,
  description: string,
): Response {
  const target = new URL(redirectUri);
  target.searchParams.set("error", code);
  target.searchParams.set("error_description", description);
  if (state) target.searchParams.set("state", state);
  return redirectResponse(target);
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!sameOrigin(request)) {
    return Response.json({ error: "Invalid request origin." }, { status: 403 });
  }

  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const form = await request.formData().catch(() => null);
    if (!form) throw new ApiError("Consent must be submitted as a form.");

    const clientId = field(form, "client_id");
    const redirectUri = field(form, "redirect_uri");
    const state = field(form, "state");
    if (!clientId || !redirectUri) {
      throw new ApiError("client_id and redirect_uri are required.");
    }

    // Never redirect to an unverified URI. Check the registration before using
    // the URI in any Location header, including error responses.
    const client = await getMcpClient(clientId);
    if (!client) throw new ApiError("Unknown OAuth client.");
    if (!isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri(client, redirectUri)) {
      throw new ApiError("The redirect_uri is not registered for this client.");
    }

    const action = field(form, "action");
    if (action === "deny") {
      return authorizationErrorRedirect(
        redirectUri,
        state,
        "access_denied",
        "The user denied the authorization request.",
      );
    }
    if (action !== "approve") throw new ApiError("Invalid consent action.");

    const codeChallenge = field(form, "code_challenge");
    if (!codeChallenge) throw new ApiError("A valid PKCE code_challenge is required.");

    let validated;
    try {
      validated = await validateMcpAuthorizationRequest({
        clientId,
        redirectUri,
        ...(field(form, "response_type") !== undefined
          ? { responseType: field(form, "response_type")! }
          : {}),
        ...(field(form, "scope") !== undefined ? { scope: field(form, "scope")! } : {}),
        ...(field(form, "resource") !== undefined ? { resource: field(form, "resource")! } : {}),
        codeChallenge,
        codeChallengeMethod: field(form, "code_challenge_method") ?? "S256",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof McpOAuthError) {
        return authorizationErrorRedirect(redirectUri, state, error.code, error.message);
      }
      throw error;
    }

    const code = await createMcpAuthorizationCode({
      clientId: validated.client.clientId,
      userId: session.user.id,
      redirectUri,
      scope: validated.scope,
      resource: validated.resource,
      codeChallenge,
      codeChallengeMethod: "S256",
    });

    const target = new URL(redirectUri);
    target.searchParams.set("code", code);
    if (state) target.searchParams.set("state", state);
    return redirectResponse(target);
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-web/src/app/api/media/upload/route.ts
================================================================================

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  config,
  createScheduledImageUrl,
  matchesMediaSignature,
  uploadScheduledDocument,
  uploadScheduledImage,
  uploadScheduledVideo,
} from "@/lib/backend";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

type MediaKind = "image" | "video" | "document";

const contentTypeByExtension: Record<string, { contentType: string; kind: MediaKind }> = {
  "image/jpeg": { contentType: "image/jpeg", kind: "image" },
  "image/png": { contentType: "image/png", kind: "image" },
  "image/gif": { contentType: "image/gif", kind: "image" },
  "video/mp4": { contentType: "video/mp4", kind: "video" },
  "video/quicktime": { contentType: "video/quicktime", kind: "video" },
  "application/pdf": { contentType: "application/pdf", kind: "document" },
};

const extensionByContentType: Record<string, string> = {
  "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
  "image/png": ".png",
  "image/gif": ".gif",
  "video/mp4": ".mp4",
  "video/quicktime": ".mov",
  "application/pdf": ".pdf",
};

// Authenticated upload bridge for remote MCP clients such as Claude.ai.
//
// The published MCP spec has no client-to-server file-upload primitive, so a
// remote tool receives neither image bytes nor a browser file handle. A
// signed-in user uploads the image/video/PDF here. The file is validated,
// stored under a per-user prefix in the private Supabase bucket, and the
// response contains the *permanent* object path (`storagePath`). Scheduling
// tools persist that path; the worker downloads the object with server-side
// credentials at publish time and only then creates a short-lived signed URL
// for platforms that require one. A `url` is also returned for immediate use,
// but it is never the durable reference.
//
// The Supabase service-role key is read only on the server. It is never placed
// in the response, a client bundle, or a NEXT_PUBLIC_* variable.
export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    if (!config.supabaseUrl || !config.supabaseServiceRoleKey) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "Media upload is not configured on this deployment. Set SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY on the server.",
        503,
      );
    }

    const form = await request.formData().catch(() => null);
    if (!form) throw new ApiError("Upload must be multipart/form-data with a 'file' field.");
    const file = form.get("file");
    if (!(file instanceof File) || file.size === 0) {
      throw new ApiError("Attach a non-empty file in the 'file' field.");
    }

    const mediaType = contentTypeByExtension[file.type];
    const extension = extensionByContentType[file.type];
    if (!mediaType || !extension) {
      throw new ApiError("Only JPEG, PNG, or GIF images, MP4 or MOV videos, and PDF documents are supported.");
    }

    const maxBytes = mediaType.kind === "image"
      ? config.scheduleImageMaxBytes
      : mediaType.kind === "video"
        ? config.scheduleVideoMaxBytes
        : config.scheduleDocumentMaxBytes;
    if (file.size > maxBytes) {
      throw new ApiError(`File exceeds the ${maxBytes}-byte limit for ${mediaType.kind} uploads.`, 413);
    }

    const bytes = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
    if (!matchesMediaSignature(bytes, mediaType.contentType)) {
      throw new ApiError("File contents do not match the declared content type.");
    }

    // Namespaced by the authenticated user so one account can never reference
    // another account's object. The scheduler re-validates ownership too.
    const storagePath = mediaType.kind === "image"
      ? await uploadScheduledImage(bytes, mediaType.contentType, extension, session.user.id)
      : mediaType.kind === "video"
        ? await uploadScheduledVideo(bytes, mediaType.contentType, extension, session.user.id)
        : await uploadScheduledDocument(bytes, mediaType.contentType, extension, session.user.id);
    const url = await createScheduledImageUrl(storagePath);

    return Response.json(
      {
        // Permanent reference: pass this as imageStoragePath, media[].storagePath,
        // or documentStoragePath. It never expires.
        storagePath,
        kind: mediaType.kind,
        contentType: mediaType.contentType,
        bytes: bytes.byteLength,
        // Convenience URL for an immediate publish; do not store it for later.
        url,
        expiresInSeconds: config.scheduleSignedUrlTtlSeconds,
        note: "Use storagePath (permanent) when scheduling. The signed url expires and is only for immediate use.",
      },
      { status: 201 },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { z } from "zod";

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import { beginAuthorization, getAuthorizationStatus } from "@/lib/backend";

const platformEnum = z.enum(["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"]);

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const parsed = z
      .object({ platform: platformEnum })
      .safeParse(body);
    if (!parsed.success) throw new ApiError("Invalid platform.");
    const { platform } = parsed.data;
    const result = await beginAuthorization(platform, session.user.id);
    return Response.json({ platform, ...result });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const sessionId = url.searchParams.get("sessionId");
    if (!sessionId) throw new ApiError("sessionId is required.");
    const status = await getAuthorizationStatus(sessionId, session.user.id);
    return Response.json({ status });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import { linkedinConnectionStatus, metaConnectionStatus } from "@/lib/backend";

type PlatformId = "facebook" | "instagram" | "linkedin";

async function check(ownerId: string, platform: PlatformId): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  try {
    const status =
      platform === "linkedin"
        ? await linkedinConnectionStatus(ownerId)
        : await metaConnectionStatus(ownerId, platform);
    return { platform, ...status };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      platform,
      connected: false,
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Status check failed.",
    };
  }
}

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const platforms = await Promise.all(
      (["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"] as const).map((platform) => check(session.user.id, platform)),
    );
    return Response.json({ platforms });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import { cancelScheduledPost, scheduledPostStatus } from "@/lib/backend";

export async function POST(
  _request: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const { id } = await params;

    const post = (await scheduledPostStatus(session.user.id)).find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!post) throw new ApiError("That post was not found.", 404);
    if (post.status !== "scheduled") {
      throw new ApiError("Only scheduled posts can be cancelled.");
    }

    const cancelled = await cancelScheduledPost(session.user.id, id);
    return Response.json({ cancelled });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

import { z } from "zod";

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  canRescheduleScheduledPost,
  rescheduleScheduledPost,
  scheduledPostStatus,
} from "@/lib/backend";

const bodySchema = z.object({
  scheduledAt: z.string().min(1, "Choose a new date and time."),
});

export async function POST(
  request: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const { id } = await params;

    const json = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(json);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new ApiError(parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Choose a new date and time.");
    }

    const post = (await scheduledPostStatus(session.user.id)).find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!post) throw new ApiError("That post was not found.", 404);
    if (!canRescheduleScheduledPost(post.status)) {
      throw new ApiError(
        `Only scheduled posts can be rescheduled; this post is ${post.status}.`,
      );
    }

    const rescheduled = await rescheduleScheduledPost(session.user.id, id, parsed.data.scheduledAt);
    return Response.json({ rescheduled });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

export async function POST(
  _request: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const { id } = await params;

    const post = (await scheduledPostStatus(session.user.id)).find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!post) throw new ApiError("That post was not found.", 404);
    if (!canSafelyRetryScheduledPost(post.status, post.failureStage)) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "This post cannot be retried. Schedule it again from the Composer.",
      );
    }

    const retry = await retryScheduledPost(session.user.id, id);
    return Response.json({ retry });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: social-media-web/src/app/api/posts/route.ts
================================================================================

import path from "node:path";

import { after } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";

import { ApiError, apiErrorResponse, requireApiSession } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  processDueJobs,
  scheduleMediaPosts,
  scheduledPostStatus,
  type NewScheduledMedia,
} from "@/lib/backend";

const platformEnum = z.enum(["facebook", "instagram", "linkedin"]);

const imageTypes = new Set(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/gif"]);
const videoTypes = new Set(["video/mp4", "video/quicktime"]);

const fallbackExtension: Record<string, string> = {
  "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
  "image/png": ".png",
  "image/gif": ".gif",
  "video/mp4": ".mp4",
  "video/quicktime": ".mov",
};

async function fileToMedia(file: File): Promise<NewScheduledMedia> {
  const contentType = file.type;
  const hasDotExtension = path.extname(file.name).toLowerCase();
  const extension = hasDotExtension || fallbackExtension[contentType] || "";

  let kind: "image" | "video";
  if (imageTypes.has(contentType)) kind = "image";
  else if (videoTypes.has(contentType)) kind = "video";
  else {
    throw new ApiError(
      `Unsupported media type "${contentType || "unknown"}". Use JPEG, PNG, GIF, MP4, or MOV.`,
    );
  }

  return {
    kind,
    bytes: Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer()),
    contentType,
    extension,
  };
}

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();
    const posts = await scheduledPostStatus(session.user.id);
    return Response.json({ posts });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const session = await requireApiSession();

    const form = await request.formData();

    const platforms = form
      .getAll("platforms")
      .map((value) => String(value).toLowerCase());
    const parsed = z
      .object({
        platforms: z.array(platformEnum).min(1, "Select at least one platform."),
        scheduledAt: z.string().optional(),
        publishNow: z.enum(["true", "false"]).optional(),
        text: z.string().min(1, "Post text is required."),
      })
      .safeParse({
        platforms,
        scheduledAt: form.get("scheduledAt") ? String(form.get("scheduledAt")) : undefined,
        publishNow: form.get("publishNow") ? String(form.get("publishNow")) : "false",
        text: String(form.get("text") ?? ""),
      });

    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new ApiError(parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid request.");
    }

    const { publishNow, text } = parsed.data;
    const files = form
      .getAll("media")
      .filter((value): value is File => value instanceof File && value.size > 0);

    const media: NewScheduledMedia[] = [];
    for (const file of files) media.push(await fileToMedia(file));

    const scheduledAt =
      publishNow === "true"
        ? new Date(Date.now() + 5_000).toISOString()
        : parsed.data.scheduledAt;

    if (!scheduledAt) throw new ApiError("Choose a schedule time.");

    let scheduled: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof scheduleMediaPosts>>;
    try {
      scheduled = await scheduleMediaPosts(
        session.user.id,
        parsed.data.platforms.map((platform) => ({ platform, scheduledAt, text })),
        media,
      );
    } catch (error) {
      throw new ApiError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not schedule the post.",
      );
    }

    if (publishNow === "true") {
      after(async () => {
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 6_000));
        try {
          await processDueJobs();
        } catch (error) {
          console.error("[api] immediate publish failed:", error);
        }
      });
    }

    return Response.json({ scheduled }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    return apiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/app/mcp/authorize/page.tsx

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardFooter,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  McpOAuthError,
  getMcpClient,
  isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri,
  validateMcpAuthorizationRequest,
} from "@/lib/backend";
import { getSession } from "@/lib/session";

const oauthFields = [
  "client_id",
  "redirect_uri",
  "response_type",
  "state",
  "code_challenge",
  "code_challenge_method",
  "scope",
  "resource",
] as const;

type OAuthField = (typeof oauthFields)[number];

function single(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : undefined;
}

function ErrorCard({ message }: { message: string }) {
  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-svh flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-muted/40 p-6">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Authorization request rejected</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            This request could not be verified, so no access was granted.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{message}</p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

export default async function McpAuthorizePage({
  searchParams,
}: PageProps<"/mcp/authorize">) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const values = Object.fromEntries(
    oauthFields.map((field) => [field, single(params[field])]),
  ) as Record<OAuthField, string | undefined>;

  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) {
    const query = new URLSearchParams();
    for (const field of oauthFields) {
      const value = values[field];
      if (value) query.set(field, value);
    }
    redirect(`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(`/mcp/authorize?${query.toString()}`)}`);
  }

  const clientId = values.client_id;
  const redirectUri = values.redirect_uri;
  if (!clientId || !redirectUri) {
    return <ErrorCard message="The request is missing client_id or redirect_uri." />;
  }

  let client;
  try {
    client = await getMcpClient(clientId);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[mcp-consent] failed to load OAuth client:", error);
    return <ErrorCard message="The authorization server is temporarily unavailable." />;
  }
  if (!client) return <ErrorCard message="Unknown OAuth client." />;
  if (!isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri(client, redirectUri)) {
    return <ErrorCard message="The redirect_uri is not registered for this client." />;
  }

  const codeChallenge = values.code_challenge;
  if (!codeChallenge) {
    return <ErrorCard message="A valid PKCE code_challenge is required." />;
  }

  let scope: string;
  let resource: string;
  try {
    const validated = await validateMcpAuthorizationRequest({
      clientId,
      redirectUri,
      ...(values.response_type !== undefined ? { responseType: values.response_type } : {}),
      ...(values.scope !== undefined ? { scope: values.scope } : {}),
      ...(values.resource !== undefined ? { resource: values.resource } : {}),
      codeChallenge,
      codeChallengeMethod: values.code_challenge_method ?? "S256",
    });
    scope = validated.scope;
    resource = validated.resource;
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof McpOAuthError ? error.message : "The authorization request is invalid.";
    return <ErrorCard message={message} />;
  }

  const clientName = client.clientName ?? "Claude";
  let redirectHost = redirectUri;
  try {
    redirectHost = new URL(redirectUri).host;
  } catch {
    // isRegisteredMcpRedirectUri already parsed it; keep the raw value as a
    // defensive fallback for display only.
  }

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-svh flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-muted/40 p-6">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Connect {clientName} to Social Media</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            Signed in as {session.user.email}. {clientName} is requesting access to
            your social media console.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <span className="font-medium">Requested access</span>
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">
              Create, schedule, publish, reschedule, retry, and cancel posts, and
              read connection status for the Instagram, Facebook Page, and LinkedIn
              accounts you have connected in this console.
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <span className="font-medium">Scope</span>
            <code className="rounded bg-muted px-2 py-1 text-xs">{scope}</code>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <span className="font-medium">Callback host</span>
            <code className="break-all rounded bg-muted px-2 py-1 text-xs">
              {redirectHost}
            </code>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <span className="font-medium">MCP resource</span>
            <code className="break-all rounded bg-muted px-2 py-1 text-xs">
              {resource}
            </code>
          </div>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            Only approve if you started this connection from {clientName}. This
            grants access to your account only; it does not share your password,
            provider tokens, or other users&apos; accounts.
          </p>
        </CardContent>
        <CardFooter>
          <form
            action="/api/mcp/oauth/authorize"
            method="post"
            className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row-reverse"
          >
            {oauthFields.map((field) => {
              const value = values[field];
              return value ? (
                <input key={field} type="hidden" name={field} value={value} />
              ) : null;
            })}
            <Button className="flex-1" type="submit" name="action" value="approve">
              Approve
            </Button>
            <Button
              className="flex-1"
              type="submit"
              name="action"
              value="deny"
              variant="outline"
            >
              Deny
            </Button>
          </form>
        </CardFooter>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/components/posts-list.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { RescheduleDialog } from "@/components/reschedule-dialog";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";

export interface ScheduledPostSummary {
  id: string;
  platform: "facebook" | "instagram" | "linkedin";
  text: string;
  scheduledAt: string;
  status: "scheduled" | "publishing" | "published" | "failed" | "cancelled";
  publishedAt?: string;
  error?: string;
  retryCount: number;
  mediaType: "none" | "image" | "video" | "carousel" | "document";
  failureStage?: "token_validation" | "image_validation" | "document_validation" | "publishing";
}

const STATUS_TONES: Record<ScheduledPostSummary["status"], "default" | "secondary" | "destructive" | "outline"> = {
  scheduled: "secondary",
  publishing: "default",
  published: "default",
  failed: "destructive",
  cancelled: "outline",
};

const PLATFORM_LABELS: Record<ScheduledPostSummary["platform"], string> = {
  facebook: "Facebook",
  instagram: "Instagram",
  linkedin: "LinkedIn",
};

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<ScheduledPostSummary["status"], string> = {
  scheduled: "Scheduled",
  publishing: "Publishing",
  published: "Published",
  failed: "Failed",
  cancelled: "Cancelled",
};

const PLATFORM_ORDER: ScheduledPostSummary["platform"][] = [
  "facebook",
  "instagram",
  "linkedin",
];

type StatusFilter = "all" | ScheduledPostSummary["status"];
type PlatformFilter = "all" | ScheduledPostSummary["platform"];

function canRetry(post: ScheduledPostSummary): boolean {
  return (
    post.status === "failed" &&
    post.failureStage !== undefined &&
    post.failureStage !== "publishing"
  );
}

function canReschedule(post: ScheduledPostSummary): boolean {
  return post.status === "scheduled";
}

export function PostsList({
  statuses,
  showWorkerButton = false,
  showFilters = false,
}: {
  statuses: ScheduledPostSummary["status"][];
  showWorkerButton?: boolean;
  showFilters?: boolean;
}) {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<ScheduledPostSummary[] | null>(null);
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [rescheduling, setRescheduling] = useState<ScheduledPostSummary | null>(null);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>("all");
  const [platformFilter, setPlatformFilter] = useState<PlatformFilter>("all");

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    return fetch("/api/posts", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
          posts?: ScheduledPostSummary[];
          error?: string;
        };
        if (!response.ok) {
          toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not load posts.");
          return;
        }
        setPosts(body.posts ?? []);
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load posts.");
      });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const visible = (posts ?? []).filter((post) => statuses.includes(post.status));
  const filtered = visible.filter(
    (post) =>
      (statusFilter === "all" || post.status === statusFilter) &&
      (platformFilter === "all" || post.platform === platformFilter),
  );
  const filtersActive = statusFilter !== "all" || platformFilter !== "all";

  function resetFilters() {
    setStatusFilter("all");
    setPlatformFilter("all");
  }

  async function runAction(
    id: string,
    path: string,
    successMessage: string,
  ) {
    setBusyId(id);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(path, { method: "POST" });
      const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
      if (!response.ok) {
        toast.error(body.error ?? "Action failed.");
        return;
      }
      toast.success(successMessage);
      await load();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Action failed.");
    } finally {
      setBusyId(null);
    }
  }

  async function runWorker() {
    setBusyId("worker");
    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/worker/run", { method: "POST" });
      const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        processed?: number;
        error?: string;
      };
      if (!response.ok) {
        toast.error(body.error ?? "Worker run failed.");
        return;
      }
      toast.success(`Processed ${body.processed ?? 0} due post(s).`);
      await load();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Worker run failed.");
    } finally {
      setBusyId(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <CardTitle>Posts</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Live status from the publishing scheduler.</CardDescription>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {showWorkerButton ? (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={runWorker}
              disabled={busyId !== null}
            >
              Run worker now
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={load} disabled={busyId !== null}>
            Refresh
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        {showFilters && posts !== null ? (
          <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <Select
                value={statusFilter}
                onValueChange={(value) =>
                  setStatusFilter((value ?? "all") as StatusFilter)
                }
              >
                <SelectTrigger
                  size="sm"
                  className="w-full sm:w-44"
                  aria-label="Filter by status"
                >
                  <SelectValue>
                    {(value) =>
                      value === "all" || value == null
                        ? "All Statuses"
                        : STATUS_LABELS[value as ScheduledPostSummary["status"]]
                    }
                  </SelectValue>
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="all">All Statuses</SelectItem>
                  {statuses.map((status) => (
                    <SelectItem key={status} value={status}>
                      {STATUS_LABELS[status]}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>

              <Select
                value={platformFilter}
                onValueChange={(value) => setPlatformFilter((value ?? "all") as PlatformFilter)}
              >
                <SelectTrigger
                  size="sm"
                  className="w-full sm:w-40"
                  aria-label="Filter by app"
                >
                  <SelectValue>
                    {(value) =>
                      value === "all" || value == null
                        ? "All Apps"
                        : PLATFORM_LABELS[value as ScheduledPostSummary["platform"]]
                    }
                  </SelectValue>
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="all">All Apps</SelectItem>
                  {PLATFORM_ORDER.map((platform) => (
                    <SelectItem key={platform} value={platform}>
                      {PLATFORM_LABELS[platform]}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Showing {filtered.length} of {visible.length}
              </span>
              {filtersActive ? (
                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={resetFilters}>
                  Reset
                </Button>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {posts === null ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <Skeleton className="h-8 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="h-8 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="h-8 w-full" />
          </div>
        ) : visible.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">No posts to show.</p>
        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-6 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              No posts match the selected filters.
            </p>
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={resetFilters}>
              Reset filters
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow>
                <TableHead>Platform</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Text</TableHead>
                <TableHead>When</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Media</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="text-right">Actions</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {filtered.map((post) => (
                <TableRow key={post.id}>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap">
                    {PLATFORM_LABELS[post.platform]}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="max-w-[24rem]">
                    <span className="line-clamp-2 text-sm">
                      {post.text || "—"}
                    </span>
                    {post.error ? (
                      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-destructive">
                        {post.error}
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-sm">
                    {new Date(post.publishedAt ?? post.scheduledAt).toLocaleString()}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <Badge variant="secondary">{post.mediaType}</Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <Badge variant={STATUS_TONES[post.status]}>{post.status}</Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right">
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                      {canRetry(post) ? (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="outline"
                          disabled={busyId !== null}
                          onClick={() =>
                            void runAction(post.id, `/api/posts/${post.id}/retry`, "Post retried.")
                          }
                        >
                          Retry
                        </Button>
                      ) : null}
                      {canReschedule(post) ? (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="outline"
                          disabled={busyId !== null}
                          onClick={() => setRescheduling(post)}
                        >
                          Reschedule
                        </Button>
                      ) : null}
                      {post.status === "scheduled" ? (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="outline"
                          disabled={busyId !== null}
                          onClick={() =>
                            void runAction(post.id, `/api/posts/${post.id}/cancel`, "Post cancelled.")
                          }
                        >
                          Cancel
                        </Button>
                      ) : null}
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </CardContent>
      {rescheduling ? (
        <RescheduleDialog
          key={rescheduling.id}
          post={rescheduling}
          onOpenChange={(open) => {
            if (!open) setRescheduling(null);
          }}
          onRescheduled={load}
        />
      ) : null}
    </Card>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/components/reschedule-dialog.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import type { ScheduledPostSummary } from "@/components/posts-list";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

const PLATFORM_LABELS: Record<ScheduledPostSummary["platform"], string> = {
  facebook: "Facebook",
  instagram: "Instagram",
  linkedin: "LinkedIn",
};

function pad(value: number): string {
  return String(value).padStart(2, "0");
}

function toLocalInputValue(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}T${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

function minLocalInputValue(): string {
  const date = new Date(Date.now() + 60_000);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}T${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

export function RescheduleDialog({
  post,
  onOpenChange,
  onRescheduled,
}: {
  post: ScheduledPostSummary;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onRescheduled: () => void | Promise<void>;
}) {
  const [scheduledAt, setScheduledAt] = useState(() => toLocalInputValue(post.scheduledAt));
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const parsed = new Date(scheduledAt);
    if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      toast.error("Choose a valid date and time.");
      return;
    }
    if (parsed.getTime() <= Date.now()) {
      toast.error("The new schedule time must be in the future.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/posts/${post.id}/reschedule`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ scheduledAt: parsed.toISOString() }),
      });
      const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
      if (!response.ok) {
        toast.error(body.error ?? "Could not reschedule the post.");
        return;
      }
      toast.success("Post rescheduled.");
      onOpenChange(false);
      await onRescheduled();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not reschedule the post.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Reschedule post</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Update when this {PLATFORM_LABELS[post.platform]} post is published.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <form className="flex flex-col gap-4" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-md border bg-muted/40 p-3 text-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">Platform</span>
              <Badge variant="secondary">{PLATFORM_LABELS[post.platform]}</Badge>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">Caption</span>
              <span className="line-clamp-3">{post.text || "—"}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">Currently scheduled</span>
              <span>{new Date(post.scheduledAt).toLocaleString()}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Only the {PLATFORM_LABELS[post.platform]} scheduled post is updated. Other platforms
            keep their current schedule.
          </p>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="reschedule-at">New date and time</Label>
            <Input
              id="reschedule-at"
              type="datetime-local"
              value={scheduledAt}
              min={minLocalInputValue()}
              onChange={(event) => setScheduledAt(event.target.value)}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <DialogFooter>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              disabled={pending}
              onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" disabled={pending || !scheduledAt}>
              {pending ? "Saving..." : "Save Changes"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

FILE: social-media-web/src/components/sign-out-button.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useTransition } from "react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { authClient } from "@/lib/auth-client";

export function SignOutButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  function onSignOut() {
    startTransition(async () => {
      await authClient.signOut();
      router.replace("/login");
      router.refresh();
    });
  }

  return (
    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onSignOut} disabled={pending}>
      {pending ? "Signing out..." : "Sign out"}
    </Button>
  );
}

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { AlertDialog as AlertDialogPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/alert-dialog"
import { cn } from "cn"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

function AlertDialog({ ...props }: AlertDialogPrimitive.Root.Props) {
  return <AlertDialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="alert-dialog" {...props} />
}

function AlertDialogTrigger({ ...props }: AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger.Props) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="alert-dialog-trigger" {...props} />
  )
}

function AlertDialogPortal({ ...props }: AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal.Props) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="alert-dialog-portal" {...props} />
  )
}

function AlertDialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: AlertDialogPrimitive.Backdrop.Props) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Backdrop
      data-slot="alert-dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 isolate z-50 bg-black/10 duration-100 supports-backdrop-filter:backdrop-blur-xs data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogContent({
  className,
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: AlertDialogPrimitive.Popup.Props & {
  size?: "default" | "sm"
}) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPortal>
      <AlertDialogOverlay />
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Popup
        data-slot="alert-dialog-content"
        data-size={size}
        className={cn(
          "group/alert-dialog-content fixed top-1/2 left-1/2 z-50 grid w-full -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 gap-4 rounded-xl bg-popover p-4 text-popover-foreground ring-1 ring-foreground/10 duration-100 outline-none data-[size=default]:max-w-xs data-[size=sm]:max-w-xs data-[size=default]:sm:max-w-sm data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-open:zoom-in-95 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0 data-closed:zoom-out-95",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    </AlertDialogPortal>
  )
}

  className,

}: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {

    <div
      data-slot="alert-dialog-header"
      className={cn(
        "grid grid-rows-[auto_1fr] place-items-center gap-1.5 text-center has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]:grid-rows-[auto_auto_1fr] has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]:gap-x-4 sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:place-items-start sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:text-left sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]:grid-rows-[auto_1fr]",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="alert-dialog-title"
      className={cn(
        "font-heading text-base font-medium sm:group-data-[size=default]/alert-dialog-content:group-has-data-[slot=alert-dialog-media]/alert-dialog-content:col-start-2",

      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )

function AlertDialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Description>) {

      data-slot="alert-dialog-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-balance text-muted-foreground md:text-pretty *:[a]:underline *:[a]:underline-offset-3 *:[a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogAction({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>) {
  return (
    <Button
      data-slot="alert-dialog-action"
      className={cn(className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDialogCancel({
  className,
  variant = "outline",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: AlertDialogPrimitive.Close.Props &
  Pick<React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>, "variant" | "size">) {

      data-slot="alert-dialog-cancel"

FILE: social-media-web/src/components/ui/badge.tsx

import { mergeProps } from "@base-ui/react/merge-props"
import { useRender } from "@base-ui/react/use-render"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "cn"

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "group/badge inline-flex h-5 w-fit shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 overflow-hidden rounded-4xl border border-transparent px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&>svg]:pointer-events-none [&>svg]:size-3!",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-primary/80",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-secondary/80",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive/10 text-destructive focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40 [a]:hover:bg-destructive/20",
        outline:
          "border-border text-foreground [a]:hover:bg-muted [a]:hover:text-muted-foreground",
        ghost:
          "hover:bg-muted hover:text-muted-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Badge({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  render,
  ...props
}: useRender.ComponentProps<"span"> & VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants>) {
  return useRender({
    defaultTagName: "span",
    props: mergeProps<"span">(
      {
        className: cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className),
      },
      props
    ),
    render,
    state: {
      slot: "badge",
      variant,
    },
  })
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants }

import * as React from "react"
import { mergeProps } from "@base-ui/react/merge-props"
import { useRender } from "@base-ui/react/use-render"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { ChevronRightIcon, MoreHorizontalIcon } from "lucide-react"

function Breadcrumb({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"nav">) {
  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="breadcrumb"
      data-slot="breadcrumb"
      className={cn(className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbList({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"ol">) {
  return (
    <ol
      data-slot="breadcrumb-list"
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm wrap-break-word text-muted-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbItem({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"li">) {
  return (
    <li
      data-slot="breadcrumb-item"
      className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbLink({
  className,
  render,
  ...props
}: useRender.ComponentProps<"a">) {
  return useRender({
    defaultTagName: "a",
    props: mergeProps<"a">(
      {
        className: cn("transition-colors hover:text-foreground", className),
      },
      props
    ),
    render,
    state: {
      slot: "breadcrumb-link",
    },
  })
}

function BreadcrumbPage({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="breadcrumb-page"
      role="link"
      aria-disabled="true"
      aria-current="page"
      className={cn("font-normal text-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function BreadcrumbSeparator({
  children,
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"li">) {
  return (
    <li
      data-slot="breadcrumb-separator"
      role="presentation"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cn("[&>svg]:size-3.5", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children ?? (
        <ChevronRightIcon />
      )}
    </li>
  )
}

function BreadcrumbEllipsis({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="breadcrumb-ellipsis"
      role="presentation"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cn(
        "flex size-5 items-center justify-center [&>svg]:size-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <MoreHorizontalIcon
      />
      <span className="sr-only">More</span>
    </span>
  )
}

export {
  Breadcrumb,
  BreadcrumbList,
  BreadcrumbItem,
  BreadcrumbLink,
  BreadcrumbPage,
  BreadcrumbSeparator,
  BreadcrumbEllipsis,
}
